|| śrīḥ ||


prāṇatoṣiṇī

śrīrāmatoṣaṇa bhaṭṭācāryeṇa

tantraśāstrāt saṃkalayya viracitā

Vi, e upādhidhāriṇā paṇḍitakulapatinā

�srījīvānandavidyāsāgarabhaṭṭāacaryyeṇa prakā�sitā |

Kalikātānagare

Kalikātāyantre mudritā

1898 āḍ


3) arthakāṇḍa


navaśaśadharakhaṇḍaṃ bibhratī candrakāntadyutiramaranarādyairarcitā bhāratī naḥ | 

śravatu viśadapadme sanniṣaṇā prasannā navarasarucirā saṅgītavidyādyadhīśā || 

śrīprāṇakṛṣṇakṛtirasya nideśavavarttī maitreyavaṃśajakulāśritacakravartī |

śrīrāmatoṣaṇa idaṃ vidadhe nijeṣṭadevīprasādajanitaṃ rucirārthakāṇḍam || 

tṛtīyasyārthakāṇḍasya nirghaṇṭaḥ kriyate mayā | 

keṣāñcidanyannirddiṣṭaṃ yathāsthānaṃ vilokyatām | 

ācārasaptakaṃ tatra vedācārakramastathā | 

utthāya bhūtaśuddhyādikāryyaṃ dhyānaṃ gurostataḥ | 

pañcabhūtātmakaiḥ pañcopacārairmānasārccanam | 

tataśca sadgurudhyānaṃ kulavṛkṣanatistataḥ | 

pādukāmantrakathanaṃ tasya māhātmyakīrttanam | 

gurutatstrīpūjanasya mantrastu tadanantaram | 

japamantro gurustotrakavacāni gurornatiḥ | 

sadgurośca namaskāro brāhmaṇādinamaskṛtiḥ | 

strīgurudhyānastavanakavacāni tataḥ param | 

tadgātā gurupūjā ca gurupaṅktinirūpaṇam | 

iti granthasvarūpādyaparicchedasamāpanam | 

tataḥ kuṇḍalinīdhyānaṃ tatstotrakavace tataḥ | 

sauragaṇeśamantrādyajapasamarpaṇaṃ tataḥ | 

haṃsadhyānājapāsaṃkhyājapābhedanirūpaṇam | 

p. 277) ātmadhyānādikaṃ tasmādvahirgatikramastathā | 

maitraṃ dantadhāvanañca nāsāśuddhyañjane tathā | 

devālayopalepādikramastotraṃ tataḥ param | 

paricchedo dvitīyaśca samāptastadanantaram | 

snānaphalaṃ snānabhedābhyantarasnānanirṇayaḥ | 

nityasnāna snānavidhiryastat saditi bhāṣaṇam | 

saṅkalpaśca tanniṣiddhapātraṃ tatpātranirṇayam | 

tīrthāvāhanāṅgaṣaṭkanyāsau mūlajapastathā | 

mṛtikālambhanādiśca śaṅkhatoyābhiṣecanam | 

saṅkṣepatarpaṇaṃ paścāt parakhātetikāryyatā | 

tena snānaniṣedhaśca gātramārjanavastrakam | 

gātramārjananiṣedhastatkāraṇanirūpaṇam | 

gaṅgādhyānamantraphalaṃ kṣetramāhātmyameva ca | 

gaṅgānāmādimāhātmyaṃ gaṅgāmṛtyuphalaṃ tataḥ || 

tadyātrikasyādhvamṛtyutattīramṛtyujaphalam | 

gaṅgājalāccaturhastamadhye piṇḍapradānajam | 

phalaṃ gaṅgātarpaṇajaṃ kālākālavicāraṇam | 

tatra nāsti ca gaṅgāyāṃ cauryyādidoṣanirṇayaḥ | 

abhiṣekāmṛtakṛtamānasasnānameva ca |

paridheyavastramānaṃ karmabhede'ṃśukaṃ tataḥ | 

nīlāṃśukādeśca tato niṣedhaścottarīyakam | 

tridhā yogapadaṃ yajñopavītamārjanaṃ tataḥ | 

tilakaṃ sthānabhede tu tadākāranirūpaṇam | 

ūrddhvapuṇḍraṃ tatphalañca tadṛte karmanindanam | 

ūrddhvapuṇḍre tripuṇḍrasya niṣedhastadanantaram | 

vaiṣṇavānāmūrddhvapuṇḍraṃ brāhmaṇādestripuṇḍrakam | 

ūrddhvapuṇḍrasya nirmāṇavidhistredhottamādikaḥ | 

tasya varṇabhedaphalaṃ nindyapuṇḍraṃ tataḥ param |

ūrddhvapuṇḍrākṛtistredhā harimandiralakṣaṇam |

puṇḍrakṛtye'ṅgulībhedaphalaṃ puṇḍrasya mṛttikā | 

gopīcandanamāhātmyaṃ taddānasya phalaṃ tataḥ | 

tulasīmūlamṛtsnāyā māhātmyaṃ tadanantaram | 

viṣṇunirmālyānulepatilakañca tataḥ param | 

dvādaśatilakavidhivaddāne copakramastataḥ | 

tripuṇḍradhāraṇasthānanirūpaṇamataḥ param | 

bhasmāntaraṃ tripuṇḍrasya pañcabrahma-ṛcastāḥ | 

p. 278) kālāgnirudropaniṣattripuṇḍrasya phalaṃ tataḥ | 

tripuṇḍrākaraṇe nindā śaktitilakanirṇayaḥ | 

iticchedatṛtīyasya samāpanamathocyate | 

sandhyā vedoditā kāryyā tāntrikī tadanantaram | 

sandhyāvihīnanindā ca dvādaśyādau ca tatkriyā | 

tataḥ paraṃ yogisandhyā yogināṃ tarpaṇaṃ tataḥ | 

teṣāmādityapūjāpi kaulasandhyā tataḥ param | 

mārjanasya vaidikasya prakārastadanantaram | 

gāyatrīvyāhṛterartho gāyatryarthastataḥ param | 

sandhyāsavarṇarūpastu savarṇadaivatantathā | 

savarṇadhyānaphalakantasyā yantraṃ tataḥ param | 

ṛṣayaḥ praṇavasyāpi gāyatryāśca tataḥ param | 

padanyāsaḥ śironyāsaḥ ṣaḍaṅganyāsa eva ca | 

dhyānaṃ tasyāstato dhyānāntare ṛṣyādireva ca | 

nindā japasya nyāsena vinā nyāsasya mudrikā | 

gāyatrīpūjanaṃ pīṭhapūjanaṃ tadanantaram | 

daśāvaraṇapūjā ca dravyābhāve jalādinā | 

punarjantu japaphalaṃ viśeṣo japanakramaḥ | 

aśauce'pi ca gāyatryā mānaso japa īritaḥ | 

tasyai japārpaṇaṃ tasyāḥ kavacaṃ tatpuraskriyā | 

tatkāmyakarmma tāntrikyā gāyatryāḥ phalanirṇayaḥ | 

tajjapasya phalaṃ tasyāḥ puraścaraṇameva ca | 

tasyā ṛṣyādivivanyāsastrisandhyadhyānameva ca | 

yāgasthānāgatiḥ pādaprakṣālanamataḥ param | 

dvārapūjā vaiṣṇavādimatabhedena tatparam | 

pūjāgṛhapraveśaścāsanañca tadanantaram | 

phalabhede tvāsanasya bhedaśca gṛhamedhinām | 

kṛṣṇājinaviśeṣaścāsanānāñca phalaṃ tataḥ | 

kāṣṭhāsanaviśeṣaśca mṛdvādyāsanameva ca | 

parimāṇamāsanasya karmabhedāsanaṃ tataḥ | 

śavāsanādi ca tataḥ salomāsananindanam | 

āsanasya ca ṛṣyādi yatīnāmāsanākṛtiḥ | 

yatibhinnāsanaṃ jñeyaṃ caturasraṃ samantataḥ | 

prauḍhapādaniṣedhaśca prauḍhapādasya lakṣaṇam | 

siddhāsanaṃ tataḥ padmāsanaṃ yogopayogi ca | 

dravyasthāpanadeśaśca trividhaṃ pātrameva ca | 

pādyapātraṃ tataḥ pādyādhārapātrasya lakṣaṇam | 

p. 279) carmapātralakṣaṇañca cāmapātrasya lakṣaṇam | 

pānīyapātratāmbūlapātrasya lakṣaṇaṃ tataḥ | 

iti pallavavicchedacaturthasya samāpanam | 

bhūtaśuddhirbhūtaśuddhau pāpadehavināśanam | 

pāpapuruṣadhyānantu pṛthivyādivilāpanam | 

āvaśyakaṃ bhūtaśuddheḥ svaprāṇānāṃ pratiṣṭhitiḥ | 

prāṇāyāmastu tatpūjādhāro mantraśilārccane | 

na yantrakaraṇaṃ mūrttinikaṭe'pi kadācana | 

pūjādvayaṃ kṛte yantre pārthive'rccāniṣedhanam | 

vidyāviśeṣe tatrāpi pūjāsādhakasiddhaye | 

brahmaṇo mūrtirākhyātā śvetakṛṣṇādirūpiṇī | 

kālikāyā rūpanetrādibījaṃ tadanantaram | 

āvirbhāvo devatāyāḥ pratimādau nirūpitaḥ |

kṛṣṇādimūrtternityatvaṃ mantraḥ prāṇapratiṣṭhite | 

tasya ṛṣyādivinyāsaḥ ṣaḍaṅganyāsa eva ca | 

mantranyāsastato dhyānaṃ pūjā tasya puraskriyā | 

prāṇadūtyaḥ prayogaśca tadyantrakaraṇaṃ tataḥ || 

āsanaṃ pādyamarghyañcācamanīyaṃ tataḥ param | 

madhuparkaḥ punarācamanīyaṃ tadanantaram | 

gandhaśabdasya vyutpattirgandhastaddānajaṃ phalam | 

gandhāṣṭakaṃ pañcavidhaṃ puṣpavyutpattireva ca | 

puṣpañca karavīrasya phalaṃ tadanu kīrttitam | 

karavīrādimūle tu prāṇatyāgaphalaṃ tataḥ | 

śvetādikaravīrāṇāṃ māhātmyaṃ tadanantaram | 

aparājitādimāhātmyaṃ viśeṣapuṣpadānajam | 

phalaṃ puṣpaphalaṃ kṛṣṇāparājitāphalaṃ tataḥ | 

rasālamañjarītyādiphalaṃ śrīcakrapūjane | 

kṛte puṣpaviśeṣeṇa prāyaścittaṃ tataḥ param | 

vṛkṣasthapuṣpadānasya niṣedhakāraṇaṃ tataḥ | 

madhyāhnasnānamarttyasya puṣpāharaṇanindanam | 

puṣpamālāyajñasūtramālābhedaniṣedhanam | 

varjyapuṣpaṃ kālabhede devabhede'pi tatparam | 

dhūpaśabdasya vyutpattirdhūpastaddānajaṃ phalam | 

dīpaśabdasya vyutpattirdīpo vastraṃ tataḥ param | 

alaṅkāraśca naivedyaṃ tāmbūlaṃ tadanantaram | 

niṣiddhañcaiva tāmbūlaṃ natibhedastataḥ param | 

nirmālyakālavijñānaṃ devatāyai nivedyakam | 

sandeśādinārikelaśasyaṃ miṣṭasamanvitam | 

p. 280) iṅgudyādiphalaṃ śastaṃ śāko mūlādisambhavaḥ | 

tintiḍīgirikuṣmāṇḍe niṣiddhe vanajaṃ tathā | 

vījapūrṇakadalyādi cākālapanasastathā | 

śasyāni yāni deyāni niṣiddhāni tataḥ param | 

niṣiddhaṃ lavaṇaṃ kṣāraṃ prācībhūtādikaṃ tathā | 

mārgādimāṃsaṃ dātavyaṃ niṣiddhaṃ māhiṣaṃ tathā | 

kṣīraṃ dadhi ghṛtañcaiva pakṣiṇāṃ haritādayaḥ | 

deyā matsyāścitrakādyā niṣiddhā ca viḍālikā | 

abhakṣyā jānapādādyāḥ śakunā ye tathābhidhāḥ | 

abhakṣyāśca tathā proktāḥ śākāḥ kausumbhakādayaḥ | 

niṣiddhaṃ rāmarambhādi dhānyaṃ śrāvaṇikādi ca | 

nārikelaṃ suvarṇābhaṃ karkandhuḥ śarkarānvitā | 

na deyāstiktakamaṭho matsyā gomīnakādayaḥ | 

bhakṣyābhakṣye deśabhede tāntrikairvarjyameva ca | 

mṛtamāṃsaṃ trirātrāvasthitaṃ māṃsaṃ tathaiva ca | 

māṃsatyāgaphalañcaiva kārttikādau viśeṣataḥ | 

pakṣī bhakṣyo'pyabhakṣyo'pyabhakṣyā nṛvānarādayaḥ | 

kukkurādimāṃsabhoge prāyaścittaṃ tataḥ param | 

māṃsatyāge tulādau tu phalaṃ nānāvidhaṃ tathā | 

yāvajjīvanamāṃsādityāgasya phalameva ca | 

havivyānnaṃ tato'nyāni jñeyānīti yathāsthalam | 

nityātantre prathamapaṭale || 

catvāro devi ! vedādyā paśubhāvapratiṣṭhitāḥ | 

vāmādyāstraya ācārā divye vīre pratiṣṭhitāḥ | 

etān saptācārān spaṣṭayati kulārṇave pañcamakhaṇḍe || 

sarvebhyaścottama vedā vedebhyo vaiṣṇavaṃ mahat | 

vaiṣṇavāduttamaṃ śaivaṃ śaivāddakṣiṇamuttamam | 

dakṣiṇāduttamaṃ vāmaṃ vāmāt siddhāntamuttamam | 

siddhāntāduttamaṃ kaulaṃ kaulātparataraṃ na hīti || 

nityātantre || 

vedācāraṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu sarvāṅgasundari ! | 

brāhmye muhūrte utthāya guruṃ natvā svanāmabhiḥ | 

ānandanāthaśabdānte pūjayedatha sādhakaḥ | 

sahasrārāmbuje dhyātvā upacāraistu pañcabhiḥ | 

prajapya vāgbhavaṃ bījaṃ cintayet paramāṃ kalām || 

rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍe dvitīyapaṭale || 

p. 281) prabhāte ca samutthāya aruṇodayakālataḥ | 

prātaḥkṛtyādikaṃ kṛtvā punaḥ śayyāsthito naraḥ | 

guruṃ sañcintayet śīrṣāmbhoje sahasrake dale | 

atra pūrvoktavacanenaikavākyatayā prabhāte brāhmamuhūrte evaṃ 
vakṣyamāṇapaścimayāmetyādāvapi || 

tatra rudrajāmala prathamakhṇḍe prathamapaṭale | 

brāhmye muhūrte utthāya baddhapadmāsanaḥ sudhīḥ | 

vidhāya bhūtaśuddhyādīn guruṃ prātaḥ smarennaraḥ | 

ādi śabdādiṣṭadevatādhyānādikameva ca || 

tathā ca nīlātantre prathamapaṭale || 

utthāya paścime yāme cintayedugratāriṇīm | 

āmūlādbrahmarandhrāntaṃ viṣatantusvarūpiṇīm | 

mūlamantramayīṃ sākṣādamṛtānandarūpiṇīm | 

sūryyakoṭipratīkāśāṃ candrakoṭisuśītalām | 

taḍitkāṭisamaprekṣāṃ kāmānalaśikhopari | 

tatprabhāpaṭalavyāptapāṭalīkṛtadehavān | 

sahasradalapaṅkaje sakalaśītaraśmiprabhaṃ varābhayakarāmbujaṃ vimalagandhapuṣpokṣitam 
| 

prasannavadanekṣaṇaṃ sakaladevatārūpiṇam | 

smarecchirasi haṃsagaṃ tadabhidhānapūrvaṃ gurum || 

rudrajāmale pūrvakhaṇḍe'pi etat kintu dvitīyacaraṇānte puṣpasrajamiti pāṭhaḥ || 

viśvasāre dvitīyapaṭale'pi || 

prātaḥ śirasi śukle'bje dvinetraṃ dvibhujaṃ gurum | 

varābhayakaraṃ śāntaṃ smarettannāmapūrvakam || 

kaṅkālamālinītantre prathamapaṭale ca | 

sahasradalapadmasthaṃ antarātmānamujjvalam | 

tasyopari nādavindormadhye siṃhāsanojjvale | 

tatra nijaguruṃ nityaṃ rajatācalasannibham | 

vīrāsanasamāsīnaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam | 

śuklamālyāmbaradharaṃ varadābhayapāṇinam | 

vāmoruśaktisahitaṃ kāruṇyenāvalokitam | 

priyayā savyahastena dhṛtacārukalevaram | 

vāmenotpaladhāriṇyā raktābharaṇabhūṣayā | 

jñānānandasamāyuktaṃ smarettannāmapūrvakam || 

pūrvakhaṇḍe'pi || 

vāmorunyastaśaktiñca svapāṇyāliṅgitaprabham | 

vāmahastotpalāṃ raktāṃ dhyāyetparamatṛpyatīm || 

p. 282) yattu rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍe dvitīyapaṭale || 

taruṇādityasaṅkāśaṃ tejovimbaṃ mahāgurum | 

anantānantamahimāsāgaraṃ śaśiśekharam | 

mahāsūkṣmabhāsvarāṅgaṃ tejovimbaṃ mahāgurum | 

mahāśuklāmbarābjasthaṃ dvinetraṃ dvibhujaṃ gurum | 

ātmopalabdhiviṣayaṃ tejasā śuklavāsasam | 

ājñācakrorddhanikaraṃ kāraṇañca satāṃ sukham | 

dharmārthakāmamokṣāṅgaṃ varābhayakaraṃ vibhum | 

praphullakamalārūḍhaṃ sarvajñaṃ jagadīśvaram | 

antaḥprakāśacapalaṃ vanamālāvibhūṣitam | 

ratnālaṅkārbhūṣāḍhyaṃ devadevaṃ sadā bhajet || 

iti raktavarṇagurudhyānamuktam | 

tadrājasaguruparam | 

rāghavabhaṭṭamate tu śaktimātraviṣaye raktavarṇasyaiva gurordhyānam | 

na tu śuklasya taditaraviṣaye śuklavarṇameva dhyāyediti tanna samīcīnam | 

śaktiviṣaye'pi nīlatantrādau śuklavarṇadhyānadarśanāt | 

gurukavace'pi | 

sahasrāre mahāpadme karpūradhavalo gururityasya vakṣyamāṇatvācca | 

arvācīnaistu karpūrairdhavala iti tṛtīyāsamāsāṅgīkāreṇa dhavalatva muktaṃ vastuto 
raktavarṇadhyānamiti vadati tadanuśāsanaviruddhaṃ karpūralepanasya kutrāpi pramāṇaṃ 
nāsti iti | 

raktavarṇaśaktiprabhāpaṭalena raktaṃ vā | 

etattu sitaraktaprabhamiti vakṣyamāṇe vyaktībhaviṣyati | 

atra tu javāsphāṭikanyāyena śaktiprabhādhikyadyotanāya raktamātramityuktam | 

kaścittu vyatyayo bahulamiti pāṇinīyasūtrabalālliṅgavyatyayena strīgurudhyānamidamāha | 

rājase raktavarṇadhyānantvagre pratipādayiṣye || 

nīlatantre || 

tato mānasagandhādyairararcayet svasvamudrayā | 

kaniṣṭhā pṛthivī tattvaṃ tadyogādgandhayojanam | 

aṅguṣṭho gaganaṃ tattvaṃ tenaiva puṣpayojanam | 

tarjjanī vāyutattvaṃ syāddhūpaṃ tenaiva yojayet | 

tejastattvaṃ madhyamā syāddīpaṃ tenaiva yojayet | 

anāmā jalatattvaṃ syāttenaiva yojayecca kam | 

tatastu vāgbhavaṃ japyādaṣṭottaraśataṃ sudhīḥ | 

japaṃ samarpya bhaktyā ca praṇameddaṇḍavadbhuvi || 

p. 283) annadākalpe'pi || 

mithunaṃ cintayitvā tu mānasairupacārakaiḥ | 

bhautikaiḥ pūjayedvidvān mudrāmantrasamanviteḥ | 

bhūmyākāśamarudvahnivārimantraiḥ sabindukaiḥ | 

kaniṣṭhāṅgulitarjjanyau madhyamānāmike tathā | 

aṅguṣṭhena samāyogānmudrāḥ pañcaprakīrttitāḥ | 

pṛthivyātmakagandhaḥ syādākāśātmakapuṣpakam | 

dhūpo vāyvātmakaḥ prokto dīpo vahnyātmakaḥ paraḥ | 

rasātmakañca naivedyaṃ pūjā pañcopacārikā | 

tatastu vāgbhavaṃ japyādaṣṭottaraśataṃ sudhīḥ || 

kaṅkālamālinītantre prathamapaṭale ca || 

mānasairupacāraiśca sampūjyākalpayenmudā | 

gandhaṃ bhūmyātmakaṃ dadyādbhāvapuṣpaṃ tataḥ param | 

dhūpaṃ vāyvātmakaṃ deyaṃ tejasā dīpameva ca | 

naivedyamamṛtaṃ dadyāt pānīyaṃ varuṇātmakam | 

ambaraṃ mukuraṃ dadyāccāmaraṃ chatrameva ca | 

tattanmudrāvidhānena sampūjyātmaguruṃ japet | 

yathāśakti japaṃ kṛtvā samāpya kavacaṃ paṭhet || 

pūjākramastu kulamūlāvatārakalpasūtraṭīkāyāṃ tṛtīyakāṇḍe | 

nijaguruṃ dhyātvā mukānandanāthāmukaśaktyambā śrīpādukāṃ pūjayāmīti 
mānasairgandhākṣatakusumairarccayet || 

kaniṣṭhābhyāṃ laṃ pṛthivyātmakaṃ gandhaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ | 

aṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ haṃ ākāśātmakaṃ puṣpaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ | 

tarjanībhyāṃ yaṃ vāyvātmakaṃ dhūpaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ | 

madhyamābhyāṃ raṃ vahnyātmakaṃ dīpaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ | 

anāmikābhyāṃ vaṃ amṛtātmakaṃ naivedyakaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ || 

dhyānāntarantu gurugītāyām | 

hṛdambuje karṇikamadhyasaṃsthaṃ siṃhāsane saṃsthitadivyamūrttim | 

dhyāyedguruṃ candrakalāprakāśaṃ saccitsukhābhīṣṭavarapradānam | 

muktāphalābhūṣitadivyamūrttiṃ vāmāṅkapīṭhasthitadivyaśaktim | 

śvetāmbaraṃ śvetavilepayuktaṃ mandasmitaṃ pūrṇakalāvidhānam || 

atha śiraḥpadmaṃ gurusthānamuktam | 

tatkathaṃ hṛdambuje dhyānamiti cenna kadācit hṛdayāmbhoje kadāciddṛṣṭigocare | 

iti pūrvalikhitarudrajāmalavacanenaiva tatsandehanirāsaḥ kṛtaḥ || 

p. 284) atha sadgurudhyānam gurugītāyām || 

brahmānandaṃ paramasukhadaṃ kevalaṃ jñānamūrttiṃ dvandvātītaṃ gaganasadṛśaṃ 
tattvamasyādilakṣyam | 

ekaṃ nityaṃ vimalamacalaṃ sarvadā sākṣibhūtaṃ bhāvātītaṃ triguṇarahitaṃ sadguruṃ 
taṃ namāmi || 

iti sadgurudhyānam || 

rudrajāmale pūrvakhaṇḍe tu kulavṛkṣapraṇāmānantaraṃ gurudhyānamuktam || 

kulavṛkṣāśca revatītantre caturthapaṭale uktāḥ || 

yathā | 

harītakī tathā dhātrī nimbāśvatthakadambakāḥ | 

ḍumbarurvaṭavilvau ca tintiḍī navamaḥ smṛtaḥ | 

kulakāṣṭhādikaṃ devi ! homārthañca samāharet || 

etena tintiḍīprabhṛtikāṣṭhenāpi homārthavahniprajvālanam | 

samiddhomastūktakāṣṭhādibhiḥ kāryya ityāyātam || 

rudrayāmale || 

brāhmye muhūrte utthāya kulavṛkṣaṃ praṇamya ca | 

śiraḥpadme sahasrāre candramaṇḍalamadhyake | 

akathāditrirekhīye haṃsamantrasupīṭhake | 

dhyāyennijaguruṃ vīro rajatācalasannibham | 

padmāsīnaṃ smitamukhaṃ varābhayakarāmbujam | 

śuklamālyāmbaradharaṃ śuklagandhānulepanam | 

vāmorusthitayā raktaśaktyāliṅgitavigraham | 

tayā svadakṣahastena dhṛtacārukalevaram | 

vāmenotpaladhāriṇyā suraktavasanasrajā | 

sitaraktaprabhāṃ bibhracchivadurgāsvarūpiṇam || 

parānandarasāpūrṇaṃ smarettannāmapūrvakam | 

tāratrayaṃ samuccāryya hasakhaphreṃ tataḥ param | 

hasakṣamalavarayūṃ hasakhaphreṃ hasau tataḥ | 

amukānandanāthānte amukīdevyanantaram | 

ambāśrīpādukāṃ dattvā pūjayāmi namo'ntakaḥ | 

ayaṃ śrīpādukāmantraḥ sarvepsitaphalapradaḥ | 

mithunaṃ cintayitvā tu ityādi | 

tatastu vāgbhavaṃ japyādaṣṭottaraśataṃ sudhīrityantamannadākalpena samānamiti || 

pādukāmāhātmyantu kulamūlāvatārakalpasūtraṭīkādhṛtapadmavāhinyām || 

pādukājñānamātreṇa jīvanmuktā bhavanti hi || 

kulārṇave || 

vāgurāmūlabalayamudrādyāḥ kavalāḥ kṛtāḥ | 

evaṃ kulārṇavajñānāṃ pādukāyāṃ pratiṣṭhitam | 

p. 285) koṭikoṭimahādānāt koṭikoṭimahāvratāt | 

koṭikoṭimahāyajñāt parā śrīpādukāsmṛtiḥ | 

koṭikoṭimahātantrāt koṭitīrthāvagāhanāt | 

koṭidevārccanāddevi ! parā śrīpādukāsmṛtiḥ | 

mahāroge mahotpāte mahādoṣe mahābhaye | 

mahāpadi mahāpāpe smṛtā ca gurupādukā || 

rudrajāmale pūrvakhaṇḍe prathamapaṭale || 

māyāṃ lakṣmīṃ puro dattvā sarvatra pūjane vidhiḥ | 

pṛthivyāṃ bhūmivījañca gandhaṃ samarpayeti ca | 

ākāśātmakaṃ śambhubījaṃ nādabindusamāhṛtam | 

puṣpaṃ samarpayāmīti vāyubījaṃ tataḥ param || 

puraścaraṇarasollāse ṣaṣṭhapaṭale | 

gurupūjāṃ vidhāyādau srakcandanavibhūṣaṇaiḥ | 

alaṅkārairvicitraiśca suvarṇādivinirmitaiḥ |

vasanairatisūkṣmaiśca śuklaraktaistathāparaiḥ |

pītaiḥ kṛṣṇaiśca cārvaṅgi ! pūjayed gurumuttamam | 

caturvidhairbhojanaiśca svarṇapātrairmanoharaiḥ | 

ṣoḍaśairupacāraiśca svarṇarājatanirmitaiḥ | 

pānapātrairmaheśāni ! śuddhasvarṇavinirmitaiḥ | 

tāmbūlaiśca sakarpūrairnānāgandhasamanvitaiḥ | 

svarṇapātrayutairdevi ! śivasiṃhaṃ prapūjayet || 

śivasiṃhaṃ gurum | 

anena vidhinā devi ! pūjayejjagadīśvaram | 

ataḥ paraṃ varārohe ! gurudārān prapūjayet | 

ṣoḍaśairupacāraiśca iṣṭadevīṃ subhaktibhiḥ | 

ādau guruṃ tato devīṃ sampūrṇaṃ pūjayet priye ! || 

evaṃ guruṃ dhyātvā antaryāgakrameṇaiva padmapuṣpaiḥ 
samarccayeditivacanānmānasopacāraiḥ sampūjayet | 

pūjāmantrastu mātṛkābhedatantre saptamapaṭale || 

vāgvījañca mahāmāyāṃ viṣṇuśaktiṃ samuccaret | 

hasakhaphreṃ tathānandabhairavasya manuṃ tataḥ | 

tasya śaktermanuṃ paścāttataścaivaṃ sehau sehau | 

śrīgurośca tathā śaktermantrametat sureśvari ! | 

gururānandanāthāntaścāmbāntā śaktirīritā | 

pūjayāmīti deveśi ! pūjāvidhiriti priye ! || 

annadākalpe'pi || 

tāratrayaṃ samuccāryya hasakhaphreṃ tataḥ param | 

hasakṣamalavarayūḥ hasakhaphreṃ sehau tataḥ | 

p. 286) amukānandanāthānte amukīdevyanantaram | 

ambā śrīpādakāṃ dattvā cintayāmi namo'ntakaḥ | 

ayaṃ śrīpadukāmantraḥ sarvepsitaphalapradaḥ | 

rudrajāmale pūrvakhaṇḍe prathamapaṭale || 

tritārañca puro dattvā śivaśaktikhakārakam || 

phāgnirekārabinduñca śivaśaktikrameti ca | 

bhūmīndūvahnivāyū ca ukārāntaṃ sureśvari ! | 

ānandanāthaśaktiśca śivaśca bhuvaneśvaraḥ | 

savisargo mahatyeva prakāśinī paretyapi | 

vahneḥ kāralakṣmīrmāyā sarasvatī śrīpādukāṃ pūjayāmīti smṛtvā pādukām | 

japamantrastu rudrajāmale pūrvakhaṇḍe pañcadaśapaṭale || 

ānandanāthaśabdānte guruṃ ṅe'ntaṃ samuddharet | 

namaḥ padaṃ tato brūyāt praṇavaṃ savasiddhidam | 

mahāguṇermanuṃ japtvā sarvasiddhīśvaro bhavet | 

vāksiddhirvadanāmbhoje gurumantraprasādataḥ | 

śatamaṣṭottaraṃ nityaṃ sahasraṃ vā tathāṣṭakam | 

prajapyārpaṇamākṛtya prāṇāyāmatrayañcaret | 

vāgbhavena tataḥ kuryyāt prāṇāyāmavidhiṃ mudā | 

pūrvoktānnadākalpoktanīlatantroktavacanābhyāṃ vāgbhavo'pi japamantra iti | 

sampradāyakrameṇāṣṭottaraśataṃ sahasraṃ vā gurumantraṃ japtvā vāgbhavena 
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā guhyātītyādinā japaṃ samarpya stavakavacādikaṃ paṭhet || 

stavo yathā || 

śiraḥsthitasupaṅkaje taruṇakoṭicandraprabhaṃ varābhayakarāmbujaṃ sakaladevatārūpiṇam | 

bhajāmi varadaṃ guruṃ kiraṇacāruśobhojjvalaṃ 
prakāśitapadadvayāmbujamalaktakoṭiprabham || 1 || 

jagadbhyanivāraṇaṃ bhuvanabhogamokṣapradaṃ guroḥ padayugāmbujaṃ bhajati yatra yoge 
jayam | 

bhajāmi paramaṃ guruṃ nayanapadmamadhyasthitaṃ bhavābdhibhayanāśanaṃ 
śamanarogakālakṣayam || 2 || 

prakāśitasupaṅkaje sudalaṣoḍaśākhye prabhuṃ parāparaguruṃ bhaje 
sakalarājyabhogapradam | 

viśālanayanadvayāmbujataḍitprabhāmaṇḍalaṃ kaḍāramaṇipāṭaladyutimadinduvindvindukam || 
3 || 

mahaujasamumāpatervigatadakṣabhāge hṛdi prabhākaraśatojjvalaṃ suvimalendukoṭyānanam | 

p. 287) bhajāmi parameṣṭhinaṃ gurugabhīrarāvolvanaṃ calācalakalevaraṃ pracapale dale 
dvādaśe || 4 || 

gurvaṅghryabjaṃ śubhaṃ mādanagaṇadahanaṃ hemamañjīrasāraṃ 
nānāśabdādbhutāklāditaparicaraṇācārucakraṃ tribhaṅgam | 

nityaṃ dhyāyet prabhāte aruṇaśataghaṭāśobhanaṃ yogagamyaṃ nābhau padmeti kānte 
daśadalamaṇibhe bhāvyate yogibhiryat || 5 || 

yā mātā maya dānavādisubhujā nirmāṇasīmā pure svādhiṣṭhānaniketane rasadale 
vaikuṇṭhamūle mayā | 

janmoddhāravikārabhāvalaharī vedaprabhā bhāvyate kandarpārpitaśāntiyonijananī 
viṣṇupriyā śāṅkarī || 6 || 

yā bhāsānalakuṇḍalīkulapathoddāmābhaśobhākarī mūle padmacaturdale kulavatī 
niśvāsadeśāśritā | 

sākṣātkāṅkṣitakalpavṛkṣalatikā suptāsvayambhūpriyā nityā yogibhayāpahā viṣaharā 
gurvambikā bhāvyate || 7 || 

ūrddhvāmbhoruhaniḥsṛtāmṛtaghaṭāmodājjalāplāvitā gurvādyā paripātu 
sūkṣmapathagā tejomayī bhāsvatī | 

sūkṣmā sādhanagocarāmṛtamayī mūlādiśīrṣāmbuje pūjyā cetasi bhāvyate bhuvi 
kadā mātā sadorddhvodgamā || 8 || 

sthitipālanayogena dhyānena pūjanena vā | 

yaḥ paṭhet pratyahaṃ vyāpya sa devo na tu mānuṣaḥ | 

kalyāṇaṃ dhanadañcaiva kīrttimāyuryaśaḥ śriyam | 

sāyāhne ca prabhāte ca paṭhedyadi subuddhimān | 

sa bhavet sādhakaśreṣṭhaḥ kalpadrumakalevaraḥ | 

stavasyāsya prabhāveṇa vāgīśatvamavāpnuyāt || 

iti rudrajāmale svaśaktikagurustotraṃ samāptam ||

kulamūlāvatārakalpasūtraṭīkāyām || 

pādukāpañcakastotra syādhikaśloko varttate | 

so'pyatra likhitaḥ | 

ādi kādi kila khādi tārakaṃ varṇamaṇḍalamakhaṇḍasiddhidam | 

antarullasitahakṣalākṣaraṃ lakṣayanti paśavaḥ kathaṃ śive ! || 1 || 

brahmarandhrasarasīruhodare nityalagnamavadātamadbhutam | 

kuṇḍalīvivarakāṇḍamaṇḍitaṃ dvādaśārṇasarasīruhaṃ bhaje || 2 || 

p. 288) tasya kandalitakarṇikāpuṭe kḷptarekhamakathādirekhayā | 

koṇalakṣitahalakṣamaṇḍalībhāvalakṣyamabalāmayaṃ bhaje || 3 || 

tatpuṭe paṭutaḍitkaḍārimasparddhamānamaṇipāṭalaprabham | 

cintayāmi hṛdi cinmayaṃ vapurbindunādamaṇipīṭhamaṇḍalam || 4 || 

ūrddhvamasya hutabhukśikhākhaṃ tadvilāsaparivṛṃhaṇāspadaṃ 
viśvaghasmaramahotsavotkaṭaṃ vyāmṛṣāmi yugamādihaṃsayoḥ || 5 || 

tatra nāthacaraṇāravindrayoḥ kuṅkumāsavajharīmarandayoḥ | 

dvandvamindumakarandaśītalaṃ mānasaṃ smarati maṅgalāspadam || 6 || 

niṣaktamaṇipādukāniyamitāghakolāhalasphuratkriyaśalayāruṇaṃ nakhasamullasaccandrakam | 

parāmṛtasarovaroditasarojasadrociṣaṃ bhajāmi śirasi sthitaṃ gurupadāravindadvayam || 7 || 

pādukāpañcakastotraṃ vañcavaktrādvinirgatam | 

ṣaḍāmnāyaphalaṃ prāptaṃ prapañce cātidurlabham || 

iti śivapañcavaktrādvinirgataṃ pādukāpañcakastotraṃ samāptam ||

kubjikātantre || 

atha gurustotram || 

oṃ namastubhyaṃ mahāmantradāyine śivarūpiṇe | 

brahmajñānaprakāśāya saṃsāraduḥkhatāriṇe | 

atisaumyāya divyāya vīrāya jñānahāriṇe | 

namaste kulanāthāya kulakaulinyadāyine | 

śivatattvaprabodhāya brahmatattvaprakāśine ! | 

namaste gurave tubhyaṃ sādhakābhayadāyine | 

anācārācārabhāvabodhāya bhāvahetave | 

bhāvābhāvavinirmuktamuktidātre namo namaḥ | 

namaste śambhave tubhyaṃ divyabhāvaprakāśine | 

jñānānandasvarūpāya vibhavāya namo namaḥ | 

śivāya śaktināthāya saccidānandarūpiṇe | 

kāmarūpāya kāmāya kāmakelikalātmane | 

kulapūjopadeśāya kulācārasvarūpiṇe |

āraktanijatacchaktisamabhāgavibhūtaye |

namaste'stu maheśāya namaste'stu namo namaḥ | 

idaṃ stotraṃ paṭhennityaṃ sādhako gurudiṅmukhaḥ | 

prātarutthāya deveśi ! tato vidyā prasīdati || 

p. 289) iti kubjikātantroktaṃ gurustotraṃ samāptam ||

atha gurukavacaṃ kaṅkālamālinītantre || 

devyuvāca || 

bhūtanātha ! mahādeva ! kavacaṃ tasya me vada | 

gurudevasya deveśa ! sākṣādbrahmasvarūpiṇaḥ || 

īśvara uvāca || 

athātaḥ kathayāmīśe ! kavacaṃ mokṣadāyakam | 

yasya jñānaṃ vinā devi ! na siddhirna ca sadgatiḥ || 

brahmādayo'pi girije ! sarvatra yājinaḥ smṛtāḥ | 

asya prasādāt sakalā vedāgamapuraḥsarāḥ | 

kavacasyāsya deveśi ! ṛṣirviṣṇurudāhṛtaḥ | 

chando virāḍ devatā ca gurudevaḥ svayaṃ śivaḥ | 

caturvargajñānamārge viniyogaḥ prakīrttitaḥ | 

sahasrāre mahāpadme karpūradhavalo guruḥ | 

vāmorusthitaśaktiryaḥ sarvatra parirakṣatu || 

paramākhyo guruḥ pātu śirasaṃ mama vallabhe ! | 

parāparākhyo nāsāṃ me parameṣṭhī mukhaṃ sadā | 

kaṇṭhaṃ mama sadā pātu prahlādānandanāthakaḥ | 

bāhū dvau sanakānandaḥ kumārānanda eva ca | 

vaśiṣṭhānandanāthaśca hṛdayaṃ pātu sarvadā | 

krodhānandaḥ kaṭiṃ pātu sukhānandaḥ padaṃ mama | 

dhyānānandaśca sarvāṅgaṃ bodhānandaśca kānane || 

sarvatra guravaḥ pāntu sarve īśvararūpiṇaḥ | 

iti te kathitaṃ bhadre ! kavacaṃ paramaṃ śive ! | 

bhaktihīne durācāre dattvaitanmṛtyumāpnuyāt || 

asyaiva paṭhanāddevi ! dhāraṇāt śravaṇāt priye ! | 

jāyate mantrasiddhiśca kimanyat kathayāmi te | 

kaṇṭhe vā dakṣiṇe bāhau śikhāyāṃ vīravandite ! | 

dhāraṇānnāśayet pāpaṃ gaṅgāyāṃ kalmaṣaṃ yathā | 

idaṃ kavacamajñātvā yadi mantraṃ japet priye ! | 

tat sarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ kṛtvā gururyāti suniścitam | 

śive ruṣṭe gurustrātā gurau ruṣṭe na kaścana || 

iti kaṅkālamālinītantre gurukavacaṃ samāptam ||

puraścaraṇarasollāse dvitīyapraśne daśamapaṭale || 

īśvara uvāca | 

p. 290) śṛṅu devi ! pravakṣyāmi guhyādguhyataraṃ mahat | 

lokopakārakaṃ praśnaṃ na kenāpi kṛtaṃ purā | 

adyaprabhṛti kasyāpi nākhyātaṃ kavacaṃ mayā | 

deśikā bahavaḥ santi mantrasādhanatatparāḥ | 

na teṣāṃ jāyate siddhirmantrairvā cakrapūjanaiḥ | 

gurorvidhānaṃ kavacamajñātvā kriyate japaḥ | 

vṛthā śramo bhavettasya na siddhirmantrapūjanaiḥ | 

gurupādaṃ puraskṛtya prāpyate kavacaṃ śubham | 

tadā mantrasya yantrasya siddhirbhavati tatkṣaṇāt | 

sugopyantu prajaptavyaṃ na vaktavyaṃ varānane ! || 

oṃ namo'sya śrīgurukavacanāmamantrasya paramabrahmarṣiḥ sarvavedānujño devadeva śrī-
ādiśivo devatā namo hesauṃ haṃsaḥ hasakṣamalavarayūṃ vījaṃ so'haṃ haṃsaḥ 
hasakṣamalavarayūṃ śaktiḥ haṃsaḥ so'haṃ kīlakaṃ śrīsamastagurumaṇḍalaprītyarthe 
jape viniyogaḥ | 

atha dhyānam | 

śrīsiddhamānavamukhā guravaḥ svarūpaṃ saṃsāradāhaśamanaṃ dvibhujaṃ trinetram | 

vāmāṅgaśaktisakalābharaṇairvibhūṣaṃ dhyāyejjapet sakalasiddhiphalapradañca | 

iti manasā praṇamya || 

hasāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ | 

hasīṃ śirase svāhā | 

hasūṃ śikhāyai vaṣaṭ | 

haiṃ kavacāya huṃ | 

hesauṃ netratrayāya vauṣaṭ | 

hasaḥ astrāya phaṭ | 

evaṃ karāṅganyāsaṃ kṛtvā upacāraiḥ sampūjya | 

oṃ namaḥ prakāśānandanāthaśca śikhāyāṃ pātu me sadā | 

paraśivānandanāthaśca śiro me rakṣayet sadā | 

paraśaktidivyānandanātho bhāle ca rakṣatu | 

kāmeśvarānandanātho mukhaṃ rakṣatu sarvadhṛk | 

divyaugho mastakaṃ devi ! pātu sarvaṃ śiraḥ sadā | 

kaṇṭhādinābhiparyyantaṃ siddhaughā guravaḥ priye ! | 

bhogānandanāthaguruḥ pātu dakṣiṇabāhukam | 

samayānandanāthaśca santataṃ hṛdayeṃ'vatu | 

sahajānandanāthaśca kaṭiṃ nābhiñca rakṣatu | 

eṣu sthāneṣu siddhaughā rakṣantu guravaḥ sadā | 

adhare mānavaughāśca guravaḥ kulanāyike ! | 

gaganānandanāthaśca gulphayoḥ pātu sarvadā | 

nīlaughānandanāthaśca rakṣayetpādapṛṣṭhataḥ | 

svātmānandanāthaguruḥ pādāṅgulīśca rakṣatu |

p. 291) kandolānandanāthaśca rakṣetpādatale sadā | 

ityevaṃ mānavaughāśca nyasennābhyādipādayoḥ | 

gururme rakṣayedurvyāṃ salile paramo guruḥ | 

parāparagururvahnau rakṣayet śivavallabhe ! | 

parameṣṭhī guruścaiva rakṣayedvāyumaṇḍale | 

śivādiguravaḥ sākṣādākāśe rakṣayet sadā | 

indro guruḥ pātu pūrve āgneyyāṃ gururagnayaḥ | 

dakṣe yamo guruḥ pātu nair-ṛtyāṃ nir-ṛtirguruḥ | 

varuṇo guruḥ paścime ca vāyavyāṃ māruto guruḥ || 

uttare dhanadaḥ pātu aiśānyāmīśvaro guruḥ | 

ūrddhvaṃ pātu gururbrahmā ananto gururapyadhaḥ | 

evaṃ daśa diśaḥ pāntu indrādyā guravaḥ kramāt | 

śirasaḥ pādaparyyantaṃ pāntu divyaughasiddhayaḥ | 

mānavaughāśca guravo vyāpakaṃ pāntu sarvadā | 

sarvatra gururūpeṇa saṃrakṣet sādhakottamam | 

ātmānaṃ gururūpañca dhyāyenmantraṃ sadā budhaḥ | 

ityevaṃ gurukavacaṃ brahmaloke'pi durlabham | 

tava prītyā mayākhyātaṃ na kasya kathitaṃ priye ! | 

pūjākāle paṭhed yastu japakāle viśeṣataḥ | 

trailokyadurlabhaṃ devi ! bhuktimuktiphalapradam | 

sarvamantraphalaṃ tasya sarvayantraphalaṃ tathā | 

sarvatīrthaphalaṃ devi ! yaḥ paṭhet kavacaṃ guroḥ | 

aṣṭagandhena bhūrje ca likhyate cakrasaṃyutam | 

kavacaṃ gurupaṅktestu bhaktyā ca śubhavāsare | 

pūjayeddhūpadīpādyaiḥ sudhābhiḥ sitasaṃyutaiḥ | 

tarpayedgurumantreṇa sādhakaḥ śuddhacetasā | 

dhārayet kavacaṃ devi ! iha bhūtabhayāpaham | 

paṭhenmantrī trikālaṃ hi sa mukto bhavabandhanāt | 

evaṃ kavacaṃ parātparaṃ divyaughasiddhaughakālavān || 

iti gurukavacaṃ samāptam ||

śaktaścettadā yogasārokta gurusahasranāmastotramapi paṭhet || 

iha tu granthagauravabhiyā tanna likhitam || 

rudrajāmale || 

ekāntabhaktyā praṇamedāyurārogyavṛddhaye | 

akhaṇḍamaṇḍalākāraṃ vyāptaṃ yena carācaram | 

tatpadaṃ darśitaṃ yena tasmai śrīgurave namaḥ | 

ajñānatimirāndhasya jñānāñjanaśalākayā | 

cakṣurunmīlitaṃ yena tasmai śrīgurave namaḥ | 

p. 292) devatāyā darśanañca karuṇāvaruṇālayam | 

sarvasiddhipradātāraṃ śrīguruṃ praṇamāmyaham | 

varābhayakaraṃ nityaṃ śvetapadmanivāsinam | 

mahābhayanihantāraṃ gurudevaṃ namāmyaham | 

mahājñānācchāditāṅgaṃ narākāraṃ varapradam | 

caturvargapradātāraṃ sthūlasūkṣmadayānvitam | 

sadānandamayaṃ devaṃ nityāñjananirañjanam | 

śuddhasattvamayaṃ devaṃ nityakālaṃ kuleśvaram | 

brahmarandhre mahāpadme tejovimbe nirākule | 

yogibhiryogagamye ca cāruśukravirājite | 

sahasradalasaṅkāśe karṇikāmadhyamadhyake | 

mahāśuklabhāsvarārkakoṭikoṭīmahaujasam | 

sarvapīṭhasthamamalaṃ parahaṃsaṃ parātparam | 

vedoddhārakaraṃ nityaṃ kāmyakarmaphalapradam | 

sadā manaḥśaktimayalayasthānapadāmbujam | 

śarajjyotsnājvalanmālāśobhendukoṭivanmukham | 

vāñchātiriktadātāraṃ sarvasiddhīśvaraṃ gurum | 

bhajāmi tanmayo bhūtvā taṃ haṃsamaṇḍalopari | 

ātmānaṃ sunirākāraṃ sākārabrahmarūpiṇam | 

vidyāmantrapradātāraṃ śrīguruṃ parameśvaram | 

sarvasiddhipradātāraṃ gurudevaṃ namāmyaham | 

kāyena manasā vācā ye namanti nirantaram | 

avaśyaṃ śrīguroḥ pādāmbhoruhe te vasanti hi | 

prabhāte koṭipuṇyañca prāpnoti sādhakottamaḥ | 

madhyāhne daśalakṣañca sāyāhne'nantapuṇyadam || 

iti rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍe gurupraṇāmastuti kathanam ||

nityaṃ śuddhaṃ nirābhāsaṃ nirakāraṃ nirañjanam | 

nityabodhacidānandaṃ guruṃ brahma namāmyaham | 

ānandamānandakaraṃ rasannaṃ jñānasvarūpaṃ nijabodhayuktam | 

yogīndramīḍyaṃ bhavarogavaidyaṃ śrīmadguruṃ nityamahaṃ namāmi || 

iti gurugītoktasadgurupraṇāmaḥ || 

nirvāṇatantre tṛtīyapaṭale || 

turīyadhāma ! mahādeva ! paramātmāśaye sthitaḥ | 

śiraḥpadme sthite vāhye namaskāraḥ kathaṃ bhavet || 

śiva uvāca || 

p. 293) śiraḥpadme mahādevastathaiva paramo guruḥ | 

tatsamo na tu deveśi ! pūjyo'sti bhuvanatraye | 

tadrūpaṃ cintayenmantrī vāhye gurucatuṣṭayam | 

tadaṃśabhāvasambhūtā ye cānye guravo janāḥ | 

tathaiva brāhmaṇāḥ sarve cāṃśāvatārasaṃsthitāḥ | 

yadaiva vāhye caitāṃśca pratyakṣe bhāvayettadā | 

sahasrāre mahāpadme sadā cittaṃ vivarjjayet | 

pratyakṣadarśane devi ! sākṣāttaṃ brahma cintayet | 

namaskārādikaṃ devi ! kuryyāt sādhakasattamaḥ | 

brāhmaṇādīn samālokya brahmacāriyatitrayam | 

dṛṣṭimātreṇa girije ! praṇameddaṇḍavadbhuvi | 

mahāpātakayukto'pi mukto bhavati nānyathā | 

na kuryyād yadi mohena mahāpātakavān bhavet | 

raktavastraṃ samālokya tathā bhasmāṅgabhūsaṇam | 

daṇḍahastaṃ triśūlañca dṛṣṭvā pradakṣiṇatrayam | 

prakuryyāt sādhakaśreṣṭhaścānyathā pātakī bhavet | 

iti vāhyagurvādidarśane jāte tadānīṃ sahasrāre gurucintāniṣedhaḥ || 

atha strīgurudhyānaṃ guptasādhanatantre dvitīyapaṭale || 

pārvatyuvāca || 

gurudhyānaṃ śrutaṃ nātha ! sarvatantreṣu gopitam || 

striyā dīkṣā śubhā proktā sarvakāmaphalapradā || 

bahujanmārjitāt puṇyāt bahubhagyavaśād yadi | 

strīgururlabhyate nātha ! tasyā dhyānantu kīdṛśam | 

kutra vā sā gururdhyeyā śrotumicchāmi sāmpratam || 

kathayasva mahādeva ! yadyahaṃ tava vallabhā | 

śiva uvāca || 

śṛṇu pārvati ! vakṣyāmi tava snehapariplutaḥ | 

rahasyaṃ strīgurordhyānaṃ yathā dhyeyā ca sā guruḥ | 

sahasrāre mahāpadme kiñjalkagaṇaśobhite | 

praphullapadmapatrākṣī ghanapīnapayodharā | 

prasannavadanā kṣīṇamadhyā dhyāyecchivāṃ gurum | 

padmarāgasamābhāsāṃ raktavastrasuśobhanām | 

raktakaṅkaṇapāṇiñca raktanūpuraśobhitām | 

śaradindupratīkāśāṃ raktodbhāsitakuṇḍalām | 

svanāthavāmabhāgasthāṃ varābhayakarāmbujām | 

iti te kathitaṃ devi ! strīgurordhyānamuttamam | 

gopanīyaṃ prayatnena na prakāśyaṃ kadācana || 

p. 294) atha stutiḥ || 

mātṛkābhedatantre saptapaṭale || 

devyuvāca || 

stutiñca kavacaṃ nātha ! śrotumicchāmi sāmpratam | 

śrīguroḥ kavacaṃ proktaṃ tvayā nātha ! purā prabho ! | 

idānīṃ strīguroḥ strotraṃ kavacaṃ mayi kathyatām | 

yasya vijñānamātreṇa punarjanma na jāyate || 

śrīśiva uvāca || 

śṛṇu deve ! pravakṣyāmi stotraṃ paramagopanam | 

yasya śravaṇamātreṇa saṃsārānmucyate naraḥ | 

namaste devadeveśi ! namaste harapūjite ! | 

brahmavidyāsvarūpāyai tasyai nityaṃ namo namaḥ || 1 || 

ajñānatimirāndhasya jñānāñjanaśalākayā | 

yayā cakṣurunmīlitaṃ tasyai nityaṃ namo namaḥ || 2 || 

bhavabandhanapārasya tāriṇī jananī parā | 

jñānadā mokṣadā nityaṃ tasyai nityaṃ namo namaḥ || 3 || 

śrīnāthavāmabhāgasthā sadayā surapūjitā | 

sadā vijñānadātrī ca tasyai nityaṃ namo namaḥ || 4 || 

sahasrāre mahāpadme sadānandasvarūpiṇī | 

mahāmokṣapradā devī tasyai nityaṃ namo namaḥ || 5 || 

brahmaviṣṇusvarūpā ca mahārudrasvarūpiṇī | 

triguṇātmasvarūpā ca tasyai nityaṃ namo namaḥ || 6 || 

candrasūryyāgnirūpā ca madāghūrṇitalocanā | 

svanāthañca samāliṅgya tasyai nityaṃ namo namaḥ || 7 || 

brahmaviṣṇuśivatvādijīvanmuktipradāyinī | 

jñānavijñānayordātrī tasyai śrīgurave namaḥ || 8 || 

idaṃ stotraṃ maheśāni ! yaḥ paṭhedbhaktisaṃyutaḥ | 

sa siddhiṃ labhate nityaṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 

prātaḥkāle paṭhed yastu gurupūjāpuraḥsaram | 

sa eva dhanyo lokeśo devīputra iva kṣitau | 

iti mātṛkābhedatantre strīguroḥ stotraṃ sampūrṇam ||

stotraṃ samāptaṃ deveśi ! kavacaṃ śṛṇu sādaram | 

yasya smaraṇamātreṇa vāgīśasamatāṃ vrajet | 

strīguroḥ kavacasyāsya sadāśiva ṛṣiḥ smṛtaḥ | 

tārākhyā devatā khyātā caturvargaphalapradā | 

klīṃ bījaṃ cakṣuṣormadhye sarvāṅgaṃ me sadāvatu | 

aiṃ bījaṃ me mukhaṃ pātu hrīṃ jihvāṃ parirakṣatu | 

p. 295) śrīṃ bījaṃ skandhadeśaṃ me hasakhaphreṃ bhujadvayam | 

hakāraḥ kaṇṭhadeśaṃ me sakāraḥ ṣoḍaśaṃ dalam | 

kṣavarṇastadadhaḥ pātu lakāro hṛdayaṃ mama | 

vakāraḥ pṛṣṭhadeśañca rakāro dakṣapārśvakam | 

yūṅkāro vāmapārśvañca sakāro merumeva ca | 

hakāro me dakṣabhujaṃ kṣakāro vāmahastakam | 

makāraścāṅgulīṃ pātu lakāro me nakhaṃ vatu | 

vakāro me nitambañca rakāro jaṭharaṃ vatu | 

ṣīṅkāraḥ pādayugalaṃ hesauḥ sarvāṅgaṃ me'vatu | 

hesaurliṅgañca lomāni keśañca parirakṣatu | 

aiṃ bījaṃ pātu pūrve me hrīṃ bījaṃ dakṣiṇe'vatu | 

śrīṃ bījaṃ paścime pātu uttare bhūtasambhavam | 

śrīṃ pātu cāgnikoṇe ca vedākhyā nair-ṛte'vatu | 

devāmbā pātu vāyavyāṃ śambhau śrīpādukāṃ tathā | 

pūjayāmi tathā corddhvaṃ namaścādhaḥ sadā'vatu | 

iti te kathitaṃ kānte ! kavacaṃ paramādbhutam | 

gurumantraṃ japitvā tu kavacaṃ praphaṭhedyadi | 

sasiddhaḥ sagaṇaḥ so'pi śiva eva na saṃśayaḥ | 

pūjākāle paṭhed yastu kavacaṃ mantravigraham | 

pūjāphalaṃ bhavettasya satyaṃ satyaṃ sureśvari ! | 

trisandhyaṃ yaḥ paṭheddevi ! sa siddho nātra saṃśayaḥ | 

bhūrjje vilikhya gulikāṃ svarṇasthāṃ dhārayed yadi | 

tasya darśanamātreṇa vādino niṣprabhāṃ gatāḥ | 

vivāde jayamāpnoti raṇe ca nir-ṛteḥ samaḥ | 

sabhāyāṃ jayamāpnoti mama tulyo na saṃśayaḥ | 

sahasrāre bhāvayan yastrisandhyaṃ prapaṭhed yadi | 

sa eva siddho lokeśo nirvāṇapadamīyate | 

samastamaṅgalaṃ nāma kavacaṃ paramādbhutam | 

yasmai kasmai na dātavyaṃ na prakāśyaṃ kadācana | 

deyaṃ śiṣyāya śāntāya cānyathā patanaṃ bhavet | 

abhaktebhyaśca deveśi ! putrebhyo'pi na darśayet | 

idaṃ kavacamajñātvā daśavidyāśca yo japet | 

sa nāpnoti phalaṃ tasya cānte ca narakaṃ vrajet | 

samāptaṃ kavacaṃ devi ! kimanyat śrotumicchasi | 

tava snehānubandhena kiṃ mayā na prakāśitam || 

iti mātṛkābhedatantre strīgurukavacaṃ samāptam ||

p. 296) atha strīgurugītā || 

kaṅkālamālinītantre dvitīyapaṭale || 

pārvatyuvāca || 

lokeśa ! kathyatāṃ deva ! gurugītā mayi prabho ! | 

śrībhagavānuvāca || 

śṛṇu tāriṇi ! vakṣyāmi gītāṃ brahmamayīṃ parām | 

gurustvaṃ sarvaśāstrāṇāmahameva prakāśakaḥ | 

tvameva guru rūpeṇa lokānāṃ trāṇakāriṇī | 

gayā gaṅgā kāśikā ca tvameva sakalaṃ jagat | 

kāverī yamunā revā karatoyā sarasvatī | 

gomatī candrabhāgā ca tvameva kulapālike ! | 

brahmāṇḍaṃ sakalaṃ devi ! koṭibrahmāṇḍameva ca | 

na hi te vaktumarhāmi kriyājālaṃ maheśvari ! | 

uktvā coktvā bhāvayitvā bhikṣuko'haṃ nagātmaje ! | 

kathaṃ tvaṃ jananī bhūtvā badhūstvaṃ mama dehinām | 

tava cakraṃ maheśāni ! atītaṃ paramātmani | 

iti te kathitā gītā gurudevasya brahmaṇaḥ | 

saṅkṣepeṇa maheśāni ! prabhureva guruḥ svayam | 

jagat samastamāsthāya gurustveko hi kevalam | 

iti taṃ toṣayitvā ca nutibhiḥ stutibhistathā | 

nānāvidhadravyadānaiḥ siddhaḥ syāt sādhakottamaḥ || 

iti strīgurugītā samāptā ||

atra prasaṅgaprāptagurupūjanamapi likhyate | 

vṛhannīlatantre dvitīyapaṭale || 

gurupūjāvidhānañca kathayāmi varānane ! | 

ādau bhadrāsanaṃ dadyāt pādyaṃ dadyāttataḥ param | 

arghyaṃ dadyādviśeṣeṇa tathā cācamanīyakam | 

snānīyaṃ vasanaṃ dadyādgandhaṃ puṣpañca śobhanam | 

dhūpañca gugguluṃ dadyāddīpaṃ syāt suśobhanam | 

carvyaṃ coṣyaṃ tathā lehyaṃ peyaṃ dadyānmanoharam | 

nānāvidhaṃ phalaṃ dadyānnānārasasamanvitam | 

bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ viśeṣeṇa dadyāccaiva varānane ! | 

puṣpāṇāmañjaliṃ dadyāccandanena samanvitam | 

śayyāṃ dadyānmaheśāni ! vicitrāṃ sarvamohinīm | 

khaṭvāṃ dadyānmaheśāni ! vicitrāmbaradhāriṇīm | 

pādasevāṃ guroḥ kuryyādatiyatnena sundari ! | 

guruvat guruputreṣu guruvattatsutādiṣu ||

p. 297) atha sapatnīkagurupūjanam || 

puraścaraṇarasollāse daśamapaṭale | 

ānīya svaguruṃ devi ! sapatnīkaṃ prayatnataḥ | 

nānāgandhairyutaistailairañjayedaṅgayaṣṭikām | 

snāpayedvividhaiḥ śuddhairudakaiḥ surapūjitaiḥ | 

divyavastreṇa tadgātraṃ sammārjya varavarṇini ! | 

pūjayedvividhaiḥ puṣpairgandhaiśca vividhaistathā | 

dhūpadīpaistathā peyaistāmbūlādyaiḥ suvāsitaiḥ | 

prakṛtyā saha cārvaṅgi ! pūjayedgurudaivatam | 

guroraṅgaṃ maheśāni ! candanaiḥ parilepayet | 

guroḥ kaṇṭhe maheśāni ! mālāṃ dadyādvilakṣaṇām | 

nānālaṅkārasubhagaiḥ pūjayed gurudaivatam | 

paṭṭavastrādibhirdevi ! svarṇaratnādibhūṣaṇaiḥ | 

tatpatnīṃ toṣayedbhaktyā sākṣāttāṃ jagadīśvarīm | 

śaṅkhakaṅkaṇakeyūranūpurādivilakṣaṇaiḥ | 

hārairatnamayairdevi ! tatpatnīṃ pūjayet priye ! | 

tasyāstu vāmatastiṣṭhed bhaktyāpi ca śubhismite ! | 

tulyaṃ niṣkaṃ pradadyādvai bhaktyā ratnāni pārvati ! | 

tanmantraṃ śṛṇu cārvaṅgi ! atiguhyaṃ parātparam | 

kāmabījaṃ samuddhṛtya tasyāścākhyānamuddharet | 

punaḥ kāmaṃ samuddhṛtya mantrametadudīritam | 

sapatnīkaṃ guruṃ devi ! pūjayed yastu sādhakaḥ | 

anena vidhinā devi ! sampūjya gurupādayoḥ | 

sahasrāre maheśāni ! tatra koṣopari priye ! | 

bhāvayettu sapatnīkaṃ pūjayedgururājñayā | 

gurorājñāṃ mūrddhni dhṛtvā ekadhā pūjayed yadi | 

tadaiva sahasā siddhirjāyate vīravandite ! | 

ayutaṃ prajaped yastu kiṃ tasya śatakoṭibhiḥ | 

evantu pratyahaṃ kuryyād yāvallakṣaṃ samāpyate | 

divālakṣaṇa maheśāni ! paśuvat sādhakāgraṇīḥ | 

rātrau lakṣaṃ japitvā tu divyavīramataiḥ priye ! | 

samāpya vidhivadbhaktyā gurave dakṣiṇāṃ tataḥ | 

dattvā siddhimavāpnoti candrasūryyagraheṇa kim ||

atha siddhaughagurupaṃkticintanam || 

vṛhannīlatantre dvitīyapaṭale || 

siddhaughā guravo devi ! divyaughā guravastathā | 

mānavaughāḥ samāsena kathyante guravastathā | 

p. 298) urddhvakeśo vyomakeśo nīlakaṇṭho vṛṣadhvajaḥ || 

kulacūḍāmaṇau || 

mūlādibrahmarandhrāntaṃ kulaṃ dhyātvā guruṃ smaret | 

prahlādānandanāthākhyaṃ sanakānandameva ca | 

kumārānandanāthañca vaśiṣṭhānandanāthakam | 

krodhānandasukhānandau dhyānānandaṃ tataḥ param | 

bodhānandaṃ tataścaiva dhyāyet kulamukhopari | 

kulamukhopari sahasrārāsthitacandramaṇḍale ityarthaḥ | 

mahārasarasollāsahṛdayā ghūrṇalocanāḥ | 

kulāliṅganasaṃyuktāścūrṇitāśeṣatāmasāḥ | 

kulaśiṣyaiḥ parivṛtāḥ pūrṇāntaḥkaraṇodyatāḥ | 

varābhayayutāḥ sarve kulatantrārthavedinaḥ | 

kulamūlāvatārakalpasūtraṭīkāyāṃ tṛtīyakāṇḍe || 

prātaḥ prabhṛti sāyāntaṃ sāyādiprātarantataḥ | 

yat karomi jagatyarthe tadastu tava pūjanam ||

iti prāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ tṛtīye'rthakāṇḍe 
ācārasaptakādigurupaṃktinirūpaṇāntarūpagranthikathanaṃ nāma prathamaḥ paricchedaḥ |



iti sarvaṃ gurave nivedya manasā gurorājñāṃ gṛhītvā 
mūlādhārakarṇikāntaḥsthatrikoṇāntargatādhomukhasvayambhū-liṅgaveṣṭinīṃ 
prasuptabhujagākārāṃ sārddhatribalayāṃ vidyutpuñjaprabhāṃ nīvāraśūkavattanvīṃ 
kulakuṇḍalinīṃ nijeṣṭadevatārūpāṃ hūṅkāreṇa manunā haṃsa iti manunā vā 
trikoṇamaṇḍalāgninā pavanadahanayogāccaitanyaṃ vidhāya brahmavartmanā sahasrāraṃ 
nītvā tatratyaparaśive saṃyojya tayoḥ sāmañjasyaṃ vibhāvyātyantaṃ 
śyāmārahasyoktaṃ | 

tatpramāṇaṃ taddhṛtakālikāśrutiryathā || 

mūlādhāre smarennityaṃ trikoṇaṃ tejasāṃ nidhim | 

tasyāgnirekhāmānīya adha ūrddhvavyavasthitām | 

nīlatoyadamadhyasthataḍillekheva bhāsvarām | 

nīvāraśūkavattanvīṃ pītāṃ bhāsvadanupamām | 

nīvāraśūkavaditi uḍidhānyasuṅgā iti prasiddhiḥ | 

tasyāḥ śikhāyā madhye ca paramorddhvavyavasthitām | 

sa brahmā sa śivaḥ sūryyaḥ śaṅkaraḥ paramasvarāṭ | 

p. 299) sa eva viṣṇuḥ sa prāṇaḥ sa kālāgniḥ sa candramāḥ | 

iti kuṇḍalinīṃ dhyātvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate | 

iti gurorājñāṃ gṛhītveti pramāṇaśūnyalikhanam | 

kuṇḍalīdhyānānantaraṃ yogasāre || 

mūlādhāre kuṇḍalinīṃ dhyātvā sampūjayennaraḥ | 

dhyānaṃ tathā pravakṣyāmi yena yogī prajāyate | 

oṃ prasuptabhujagākārāṃ svayambhūliṅgamāśritām | 

vidyutkoṭiprabhāṃ devīṃ vicitravasanānvitām | 

śṛṅgārādirasollāsāṃ sarvadā kāraṇapriyām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā kuṇḍalinīṃ tato yajet samāhitaḥ | 

manasā gandhapuṣpādyaiḥ sampūjya vāgbhavaṃ japet | 

tato vai toṣayettāñca stavenānena sādhakaḥ | 

rudrajāmale || 

atha prātaḥ samutthāya paśuruttamapaṇḍitaḥ | 

gurūṇāṃ caraṇāmbhojamaṅgalaṃ śīrṣapaṅkaje | 

vibhāvya punarevaṃ hi śrīpadaṃ bhāvayed yadi | 

pūjayitvā ca vividhairupacārairnamet stavaiḥ || 

trailokyaṃ tejasā vyāptaṃ maṇḍalasthāṃ mahotsavām | 

taditkoṭiprabhādīptiṃ candrakoṭisuśītalām | 

sārddhatribalayākārasvayambhūliṅgaveṣṭitām | 

utthāpayenmahādevīṃ mahāvaktvāṃ manonmanīm | 

śvāsocchvāsādudgacchantīṃ dvādaśāṅgularūpiṇīm | 

yoginīṃ khecarīṃ vāyurūpāṃ mūlāmbujasthitām | 

caturvarṇasvarūpāṃ tāṃ vakārādisamāntakām | 

koṭikoṭisahasrārkakiraṇojjvalamohinīm | 

mahāsūkṣmapathaprāntarāntarāntaragāminīm | 

trailokyarakṣitāṃ vākyadevatāṃ śabdarūpiṇīm | 

mahābuddhipradāṃ devīṃ sahasradalagāminīm | 

mahāsūkṣmapathe tejomayīṃ mṛtyusvarūpiṇīm | 

kālarūpāṃ brahmarūpāṃ sarvatra sarvacinmayīm | 

dhyātvā punaḥ punaḥ śirṣe sudhābdhau viniveṣṭitām | 

sudhāpānaṃ kārayitvā punaḥ sthāne samānayet | 

samānayanakāle tu suṣumnāmadhyamadhyake | 

amṛtābhiplutaṃ kṛtvā punaḥ sthāneṣu pūjayet | 

ūrddhvodgamanakāle tu mahātejomayīṃ smaret | 

pratiprayāṇakāle tu sudhādhārābhirāplutām | 

p. 300) mahākuṇḍalinīṃ devīmamṛtānandavigrahām | 

dhyātvā dhyātvā punardhyātvā sarvasiddhīśvaro bhavet | 

tasmin sthāne mahādevīṃ vibhāvya kiraṇojjvalām | 

amṛtānandamūrtiṃ tāṃ pūjayitvā śubhāṃ mudā | 

mānasoccāravījena māyāṃ vā kāmavījakam | 

pañcāśadvarṇamālābhirjaptvānulomatastathā | 

vilomena punarjaptvā sarvatra śubhadaṃ stavam | 

paṭhitvā siddhimāpnoti tat stotraṃ śṛṇu bhairava ! ||

atha kuṇḍalinīstotram || 

janmoddhāriṇi ! rakṣiṇīha taruṇī vedādivījādimā nityaṃ cetami bhāvyate bhuvi kadā 
madvākyasañcāriṇī | 

māṃ sā tu priyadā sadā savipadaṃ saṅghātaya śrīdhare dhātrī tvaṃ 
svayamādidevavanitā dīnātidīnaṃ param || 1 || 

raktābhāmṛtacandrikā lipimayī sarpākṛtirnidritā jāgraddharmasamāśritā bhagavatī tvaṃ 
sāṃśalokāśrayaḥ | 

māṃsodgandhakagandhadoṣajaḍitaṃ vedādikāryyānvitaṃ sampālyāmalacandrakoṭikiraṇe 
nityaṃ śarīraṃ kuru || 2 || 

siddhārtho nijadoṣavit khalagatirvyādhīyate vidyayā kuṇḍalyākulamārgayuktanagarī 
sāyāhnamājñāśrayā | 

yadyevaṃ bhajati prabhātasamaye madhyāhnakāle'thavā nityaṃ yaḥ 
kulakuṇḍalīnijapadāmbhojaṃ sa siddho bhavet || 3 || 

ye vākāśacaturdale'tivimale vāñchāphalonmūlake nityaṃ samprati nityadeśaghaṭitā 
saṅketitā bhāvitā | 

vidyākuṇḍalamālinī svajananī sārakriyā bhāvyate yaistaiḥ siddhikulodbhavaiḥ praṇatibhiḥ 
kīrttyā paraṃ śambhubhiḥ || 4 || 

vācā śaṅkaramohinī tripalayācchāyāpaṭodgāminīṃ sārāmādimahāsukhapraharaṇī 
netrasthitā yoginī | 

sarvagranthivibhedinī subhujagā sūkṣmātisūkṣmā parā brahmajñānavinodinī 
kulakuṭhārāghātinī bhāvyate || 5 || 

vande śrīkulakuṇḍalīṃ tribalibhiḥ sārddhaṃ svayambhūpriyāṃ 
prāveṣṭyāsurasāracittacapalāṃ bālābalāṃ niṣkalām | 

yā devī paribhāti vedavadanā sambhāvanā bhāvanā tāmiṣṭāṃ śirasi 
svayambhuvanitāṃ sambhāvayāmi kriyām || 6 || 

p. 301) vāṇīkoṭimṛdaṅganādamadanā niḥśreṇikoṭidhvaniḥ 
prāṇeśīvaśavāmamūlakamalollāsaikapūrṇānanā | 

āṣāḍhodbhavamegharājaniyutadhvāntāntarasthāyinī mātā sā paripātu sūkṣmapathage ! 
māṃ yoginaṃ saṃskuru || 7 || 

tvāmāśritya narā vrajanti sahasā vaikuṇṭhakailāsayorānandaikavilāsinīṃ 
śaśigatānandānanāṃ kāraṇam | 

mātaḥ ! śrīkulakuṇḍalipriyakale ! kālīkuloddīpane ! bhūyastvāṃ praṇamāmi rudravanite ! 
māmuddhara tvaṃ pathi || 8 || 

kuṇḍalīśaktimārgasthaṃ stotrāṣṭakaṃ mahāphalam | 

yaḥ paṭhet prātarutthāya sa yogī bhavati dhruvam | 

kṣaṇādeva hi pāṭhena kavinātho bhavediha | 

paratra kuṇḍalīyogādbrahmalīno bhavenmahān | 

iti te kathitaṃ nātha ! kuṇḍalīkomalastavam | 

etatstotraprasādena deveṣu gurugīṣpatiḥ | 

sarve devāḥ siddhiyuktā asyāḥ stotraprasādataḥ | 

dvāparārddhacirañjīvī brahmā sarvasureśvaraḥ | 

tvañcāpi mama nikaṭe sthito bhagavatīpatiḥ | 

māṃ viddhi paramāṃ śaktiṃ sthūlasūkṣmasvarūpiṇīm | 

sarvaprakāśakaraṇīṃ vindhyaparvatavāsinīm | 

himālayasutāṃ siddhāṃ siddhamantrasvarūpiṇīm | 

vedāntaśaktitantrasthāṃ kulatantrārthagāminīm | 

rudrajāmalamabhyasthāṃ sthitisthāpakabhāvanām | 

pañcamudrāsvarūpāñca śaktijāmalamālinīm | 

ratnamālābalākāḍhyāṃ candrasūryyaprakāśinīm | 

sarvabhūtamahābuddhidāyinīṃ dānavāpahām | 

sthityutpattilayakarīṃ karuṇāsāgarasthitām | 

mahāmohanivāsāḍhyāṃ dāmodaraśarīragām | 

chatracāmararatnāḍhyāṃ mahāśūlakarāṃ parām | 

jñānadāṃ biddhidāṃ vidyāṃ ratnamālākalāpadām | 

sarvatejaḥsvarūpāṃ māmanantakoṭivigrahām | 

daridradhanadāṃ lakṣmīṃ nārāyaṇamanoramām | 

sadā bhāvaya śambho ! tvaṃ yoganāyaka ! paṇḍita ! || 

p. 302) iti rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍe kuṇḍalīstotraṃ samāptam |

kuṇḍalinyāṃ stotrāntaraṃ yogasāre tṛtīyapaṭale || 

oṃ namaste devadeveśi ! yogīśaprāṇaballabhe ! siddhide ! varade ! mātaḥ ! 
svayambhūliṅgaveṣṭite || 1 || 

oṃ prasuptabhujagākāre ! sarvadā kāraṇapriye ! | 

kāmakalānvite ! devi ! mamābhīṣṭaṃ kuruṣva ca || 2 || 

asāre ghorasaṃsāre bhavarogāt kuleśvari ! | 

sarvadā rakṣa māṃ devi ! janmasaṃsārasāgarāt || 3 || 

iti kuṇḍalinīstotraṃ dhyātvā yaḥ prapaṭhet sudhīḥ | 

mucyate sarvapāpebhyo bhavasaṃsārarūpake || 

iti kuṇḍalinīstotraṃ samāptam |

atha kuṇḍalinīkavacam || 

rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍe pañcatriṃśatpaṭale || 

śrī ānandabhairavyuvāca || 

atha vakṣye mahādeva ! kuṇḍalīkavaca śubham | 

paramānandadaṃ siddhaṃ siddhavṛndaniṣevitam | 

yaddhṛtvā yoginaḥ sarve dharmmādharmapradarśakāḥ |

jñānino mānino dhīrā vicaranti yathā narāḥ | 

siddhayo'pyaṇimādyāśca karasthāḥ sarvadevatāḥ |

etatkavacapāṭhena devendro yogirāḍbhavet | 

ṛṣayo yoginaḥ sarve jaṭilāḥ kulabhairavāḥ |

prātaḥkāle trivārañca madhyāhne vārayugmakam | 

sāyāhne vāramekastu paṭhet kavacameva ca |

pāṭhādeva mahāyogī kuṇḍalīdarśanaṃ labhet | 

kuṇḍalīkavacasya brahmā ṛṣirgāyatrīcchandaḥ kuṇḍalīdevatā sarvābhīṣṭasiddhyarthe 
viniyogaḥ | 

omīśvarī jagaddhātrī lalitā sundarī parā | 

kuṇḍalī kularūpā tu pātu māṃ kulacaṇḍikā | 

śiro me lalitā devī pātūgrākhyā kapolakam | 

brahmarandhreṇa puṭitaṃ bhrūmadhyaṃ pātu me sā | 

netratrayaṃ mahākālī kālāgnibhakṣikā śikhām | 

dantāvalīṃ viśālākṣī oṣṭhamiṣṭānnavāsinī | 

kāmavījātmikā vidyā adharaṃ pātu me sadā | 

dṛggaṇḍasthā cāṇḍayugmaṃ māyāvījā rasapriyā | 

p. 303) bhuvaneśī karṇayugmaṃ cibukaṃ krodhakālikā | 

kapilā me galaṃ pātu sarvavījasvarūpiṇī | 

mātṛkāvarṇapuṭitā kuṇḍalī kalpameva ca | 

hṛdayaṃ kālaputrī ca kaṅkālī pātu me mukham | 

bhujayugmaṃ caṇḍadurgā caṇḍadordaṇḍakhaṇḍinī | 

skandhayugmaṃ pātu devī civukaṃ krodhakālikā | 

aṅgulyagraṃ kulānandā śrīvidyā nakhamaṇḍalam | 

kālikā bhuvaneśānī pṛṣṭhadeśaṃ sadā'vatu | 

pārśvayugmaṃ mahāvīrā vīrāsanadharā'bhayā | 

mātu māṃ kuladarbhasthā nābhimudaramallikā | 

kaṭideśaṃ pṛṣṭhasaṃsthā mahāmahiṣaghātinī | 

liṅgasthānaṃ mahāmudrā bhagamālā manupriyā | 

bhagīrathapriyā dhūmrā mūlādhāraṃ gaṇeśvarī | 

caturdalaṃ kāmavidyā dalāgraṃ me vasundharā | 

dhīrdharā dhāraṇākhyā ca brahmāṇī pātu me mukham | 

bhedinī pātu kamalā vāgdevī pūrṇagaṃ dalam | 

chedinī dakṣiṇe pārśve pātu caṇḍā mahātapāḥ | 

caṇḍaghaṇṭā sadā pātu yoginī vāruṇaṃ dalam | 

uttarasthaṃ dalaṃ pātu pṛthivīmindralālitām | 

catuṣkoṇaṃ kāmavidyā brahmavidyāṣṭakoṇakam | 

aṣṭadalaṃ sadā pātu sarvavāhanavāhanā | 

caturbhujā sadā pātu ḍākinī kulacañcalā | 

meḍhrastha madanādhārā pātu me cārupaṅkajam | 

svayambhūliṅgaṃ cārvaṅgī koṭarākṣī mamāsanam | 

kadambavanagā pātu kadambavanavāsinī | 

vaiṣṇavī paramā māyā pātu me vaiṣṇavaṃ padam | 

ṣaḍdalaṃ rākiṇī pātu raṅginī kāmavāsinī | 

kāmeśvarī kāmarūpā kṛṣṇaṃ me pītavāsasam | 

dhanuḥ sā vanadurgā me śaṅkhaṃ me śaṅkhinī śivā | 

cakraṃ cakreśvarī pātu kamalākṣī gadāṃ mama | 

padmaṃ me padmagandhā ca padmamālāmanoharā | 

vādisāntākṣaraṃ pātu lākinī lokapāvanī | 

ṣaḍdalasthitadevāṃśca pātu kailāsavāsinī | 

agnivarṇā sadā pātu galaṃ me parameśvarī | 

maṇipūraṃ sadā pātu maṇimālāvibhūṣaṇā | 

daśapatraṃ daśavarṇaṃ kādiphāntaṃ trivikramā | 

pātu nīlāmahākālī bhadrā bhīmā sarasvatī | 

p. 304) ayodhyāvāsinī devī mahāpīṭhanivāsinī | 

vāgbhavādyā mahāvidyā kuṇḍalī kālarūpiṇī | 

daśacchadaśataṃ pātu rudraṃ rudrātmakaṃ mama | 

sūkṣmā sūkṣmatarā pātu sūkṣmasthānanivāsinī | 

lākinī lokajananī pātu kūṭākṣarānvitā | 

tejasaṃ pātu niyataṃ rajakī rājapūjitā | 

vijayā kulavījasthā tavargaṃ timirāpahā | 

candrātmikā maṇigranthiṃ bhedinī  pātu sarvadā | 

bhagamālā bhṛgusutā pātu māṃ nābhivāsinī |

nandinī pātu sakalaṃ kuṇḍalī kālakalpitā | 

hṛtpadmaṃ pātu kālākhyā dhūmravarṇā manoharā | 

daladvādaśavarṇañca bhāskarī bhāvasiddhidā | 

pātu me paramā vidyā kavargaṃ kāmacāriṇī | 

cavargaṃ cārurasanā vyāghrāsyā ṭaṅkadhāriṇī | 

ṭakāraṃ pātu kṛṣṇākhyā kākinīṃ pātu kālikā | 

ṭhaṅkurāṅgī ṭhakāraṃ me vījabhāṣā mahodayā | 

īśvaraṃ pātu vimalā mama hṛtpadmavāsinī | 

kālikāṃ kālasandarbhā yoginī yogamātaram | 

indrāṇī vāruṇī pātu kulamālā kulāntaram | 

tāriṇī śaktimātā ca kaṇṭhavākyaṃ sadā'vatu | 

vipracittā mahogrogrā prabhādīptā ghanāmalā | 

vākstambhinī vajradehā vaidehī vṛṣavāhinī | 

unmattā nandacittā ca kuleśī sā bhagāturā | 

mama ṣoḍaśa patrāṇi pātu mātṛtayā sthitā | 

surān rakṣatu vedajñā sarvabhāṣā ca kālikām | 

īśvarārddhāsanagatā prapāyānme sadāśivā | 

śākambharī mahāmāyā śākinī pātu sarvadā | 

bhavānī bhavamātā ca payodrumadhyapaṅkajam | 

dvidalaṃ vratakāmākhyā aṣṭāṅgasiddhidāyinī | 

pātu māmakhilānandā manorūpā japapriyā | 

lakāraṃ lakṣaṇākrāntā sarvalakṣaṇalakṣaṇā | 

kṛṣṇājinadharā devī kṣakāraṃ pātu sarvadā | 

dvidalasthaṃ sarvadevaṃ sadā pātu varānanā | 

bahurūpā viśvarūpā hākinī pātu caṇḍikā | 

harā paraśivaṃ pātu mānasaṃ pātu pañcamī | 

ṣaṭcakrasthā sadā pātu ṣaṭcakrakulavāsinī | 

p. 305) akārādikṣakārāntā bindu sargasamanvitā | 

mātṛkārṇā sadā pātu kuṇḍalī jñānakuṇḍalī | 

pūrṇakālī gatipretā pūrṇagiritaṭaṃ śivā | 

uḍḍīyāneśvarī devī sakalaṃ pātu sarvadā | 

kailāsaparvataṃ pātu kailāsagirivāsinī | 

ḍākinī rākiṇī śaktirlākinī kākinī kalā | 

sākinī hākinī devī ṣaṭcakrādīn prapātu me | 

kailāsākhyaṃ sadā pātu pañcānanatanūdbhavā | 

hiraṇyavarṇā rajanī candrasūryyāgnibhakṣiṇī | 

sahasradalapadmaṃ me sadā pātu kulākulā | 

sahasradalapadmasthā daivataṃ pātu bhairavī | 

kālī tārā ṣoḍaśākhyā mātaṅgī padmavāsinī | 

śaśikoṭigaladrūpā pātu me sakalāṃ tanum | 

raṇe ghore jale doṣe yudve vāde śmaśānake | 

sarvatra gamane jñāne sadā māṃ pātu śailajā | 

parvate vividhāvāse vināśe pātu kuṇḍalī | 

padādibrahmarandhrāntaṃ sarvākāśā sureśvarī | 

sadā pātu sarvavidyā sarvajñānaṃ sadā mama | 

navalakṣamahāvidyā daśadikṣu prapātu mām | 

ityetat kavacaṃ devi ! kuṇḍalinyāḥ prasiddhidam | 

ye paṭhanti dhyānayoge yogamārgavyavasthitāḥ | 

te yānti mokṣapadavīmaihikenātra saṃśayaḥ | 

mūlapadme manoyogaṃ kṛtvā dṛḍhāsanasthitaḥ | 

mantrī dhyāyet kuṇḍalinīṃ sūkṣmapadmaprakāśinīm | 

dharmodayāṃ dayārūḍhāmākāśasthānavāsinīm | 

amṛtānandarasikāṃ vikalāṃ sakalāṃ sitām | 

asitāṃ raktarahitāṃ viraktāṃ raktavigrahām | 

raktanetrāṃ kulakṣiptāṃ jñānākulajalojjvalām | 

viprakārāṃ manorūpāṃ mūle dhyātvā prapūjayet | 

yo yogī kurute evaṃ sa siddho nātra saṃśayaḥ | 

rogī rogāt pramucyeta baddho mucyeta bandhanāt | 

vastu priyamavāpnoti vastuhīnaḥ paṭhed yadi | 

putrahīno labhet putraṃ yogahīno bhavedvaśī | 

kavacaṃ dhārayedyastu śikhāyāṃ dakṣiṇe bhuje | 

vāmā vāmakare dhṛtvā sarvābhīṣṭamavāpnuyāt | 

svarṇe raupye tathā tāmre sthāpayitvā prapūjayet | 

p. 306) sarvadeśe sarvakāle paṭhitvā siddhimāpnuyāt | 

sa bhūyāt kuṇḍalīputro nātra kāryyā vicāraṇā || 

iti rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍe kandavāsinīkavacaṃ samāptam ||

tata ācārāt sveṣṭadevatāpraṇāmamantreṇa kuṇḍalinīṃ natvā daśasu dvāreṣu 
cauragaṇeśamantraṃ kavāṭavan nyaset taduktaṃ gaṇeśavimarṣiṇyāṃ || 

cakṣurdvayaṃ tathā karṇadvayaṃ nāsāpuṭadvayam | 

mukhaṃ nābhiṃ liṅgamūlaṃ gudasthānaṃ tathaiva ca | 

manodvāraṃ bhruvormadhye daśaiva dvārasaṃjñitāḥ | 

aṅkuśaṃ prathamaṃ bījaṃ hṛdaye daśadhā japet | 

prajapānte tato mātaḥ ! kavāṭaṃ nikṣipettataḥ | 

karṇayośca tathā kūrccaṃ kālīṃ nāsāpuṭe tataḥ | 

mukhe strīṃ dvividhaṃ bījaṃ nābhau vāṇīṃ tato japet | 

hesauḥ bījaṃ liṅgamūle bluṃ mūle parikīrttitam | 

oṃkārañca bhruvormadhye manaḥsthāne tathaiva ca | 

etadekādaśaṃ bījaṃ pratidvāre kavāṭavat || 

cauragaṇeśamantraṃ na japtvā karmmamātraṃ na kuryyāt | 

yadi kuryyāttadā doṣa ukto varṇavilāsatantre || 

gaṇeśa uvāca || 

adhunāhaṃ pravakṣyāmi cauramantramataḥ śṛṇu | 

cauramantraparijñānaṃ vinā he brāhmaṇīśvari ! | 

purāṇaṃ prapaṭhedyastu sa eva mūrttimān kaliḥ | 

parajanmani pāpiṣṭhaḥ sa bhaveccaurakukkuraḥ | 

śivapūjā viṣṇupūjā śaktipūjā tathaiva ca | 

sarvapūjāsu yattejo harate gaṇapaḥ svayam | 

pañcāśadgaṇadevānāṃ jyotīṃṣi munipuṅgavāḥ | 

pratidvārapathe gatvā pratipadmeṣu jṛmbhate | 

haranti japatejāṃsi pratipadmeṣu saṃsthitāḥ | 

japapūjāsu yattejastatra cauragaṇādhipaḥ | 

tasmāccauraprabodhārthaṃ cauramantrān japeddaśa | 

tatastu pūjayeddhīmān yasya yā iṣṭadevatāḥ | 

tataḥ phalamavāpnoti brahmāditridivaukasaḥ | 

cauramantraṃ mahāmantraṃ pañcāśadgaṇatoṣaṇam | 

cauramantraṃ vinā bhadre ! śāntisvastyayanaṃ kutaḥ | 

karṇadvayaṃ tathā cakṣurdvayaṃ nāsāmukhaṃ tataḥ | 

p. 307) nābhisthāne liṅgamūle gudasthāne tathaiva ca | 

manodvāre bhruvormadhye daśaikaṃ dvāramīritam | 

pratidvāre nyasenmantraṃ caurākhyaṃ brāhmaṇīśvari ! | 

cauramantrañcāha bhadre ! pratidvāre kavāṭavat | 

rahasyaṃ te pravakṣyāmi pañcāśadgaṇatoṣaṇam | 

aṅkuśaṃ pañcamaṃ bījaṃ prathame daśadhā japet | 

prajapya subhage ! mātaḥ ! kavāṭaṃ nikṣipettataḥ | 

anyathā aṅkuśairvījaiḥ kavāṭaṃ bhedire gaṇāḥ | 

candrikāntargatā nityaṃ śaṅkaraṃ varasundaram | 

candrikā suṣamā sītā śiñjinī aṇimāguṇā | 

candravindvātmikā nityā gaṇeśaparipūjitā | 

hrīṃ hrīṃ vījadvayamiti vinyasennayanadvaye |

karṇayośca tathā hrīṃ hrīṃ huṃ huṃ nāsādvaye tathā | 

mukhe strīṃ dvividhaṃ bījaṃ nābhau klīṃ subhageśvari ! |

japakālo'pi tatraiva ||

yamasya nādhikāro'pi cauramantrajapāt priye ! |

tattejoharaṇe śaktirgaṇeśasya na caiva hi ||

p. 310) ṣaṭśatāni sahasrāṇi sahasrañca tadeva hi |

parāparebhyastadvacca tathā parātparāya ca | 

kṣetrapālaṃ namasmṛtya dvāradevān vidhānataḥ | 

lakṣmīṃ sarasvatīṃ durgāṃ gaṅgāṃ godāvarīṃ tathā | 

bhogavatīṃ namaskṛtya vāstudevānnamettadā | 

vighnarājañca vāstvīśaṃ kṣetreśaṃ praṇamāmyaham | 

nair-ṛtañcaiva brahmāṇaṃ brahmakarmātmakaṃ priye ! | 

vighnān praṇamya vidhinā tān devāṃśca namettataḥ | 

ādhāraśaktiṃ tāṃ natvā praṅkajadvayadhāriṇīm | 

nīlavarṇāṃ mahāmāyāṃ dvādaśākṣararūpiṇīm | 

tato dharmagaṇaiḥ sevyāṃ tāṃ devīṃ śaktirūpiṇīm | 

nīlavarṇaṃ mahākāyaṃ kūrmaṃ himādrisaṃsthitam | 

sarvaśaktitanuṃ viṣṇuṃ kūrmākāraṃ namāmyaham | 

tasya pṛṣṭhe sthitaṃ devamanantaṃ taṃ namāmyaham | 

sahasraphaṇasaṃyuktaṃ sarpākāraṃ hariṃ namaḥ | 

himādrisadṛśaṃ viṣṇuṃ ekadaṇḍadharaṃ vibhum | 

phaṇāsahasrairmaṇibhirbhūṣitaṃ taṃ namāmyaham | 

tasya kukṣagataṃ dhyāyellāṅgalañcāśubhāvaham | 

mudgaraṃ vāmabhāge ca tatraikādaśasaṃyutam | 

tasya mūrddhnisthitaṃ dhyāyedvarāhaṃ viṣṇumavyayam | 

ekadantaṃ mahākāyaṃ kālameghasamaprabham | 

tasya dante sadā dhyāyet pṛthivīṃ padmapāyinīm | 

nīlavarṇāṃ mahādevīṃ śāliśūkavibhūṣitām || 

aṇimādiguṇairyuktāṃ sarvatattvavibhūṣitām | 

tasyā mūrddhni sadā dhyāyet samudrān ratnasaṃyutān | 

nānāparvatasaṃyuktāṃ pṛthivīṃ cintayeddhiyā | 

parvatāṃścintayettasyāṃ sarvatra kulabhūṣitān | 

diggajebhyo namaskṛtya yogebhyaḥ praṇamet sadā | 

munīndrebhya ṛṣibhyaśca yogaśāstradharāya ca | 

śāktebhyo vaiṣṇavebhyaśca śaivebhyaḥ praṇamet sadā | 

yantrebhyo mantradevebhyaḥ sarvaśāstraparāya ca | 

candrāya candravaṃśebhyaḥ sūryyavaṃśebhya eva ca |

p. 316) manuvaṃśamanuṣyebhyaḥ pañcabhūtebhya eva ca | 

mahābhūtebhyo bhūtebhyaḥ kalābhyaḥ praṇamet sadā | 

rasebhyo'pi namaskṛtya gandhebhyaṃ praṇamet sadā | 

puṣpebhyaḥ puṣpahāsebhyo gandharvebhyo namo namaḥ | 

yakṣebhyo bhūtavargebhyaḥ pretebhyaṃ sarvakarmasu |

śabdabrahmasvarūpebhyaḥ praṇamāmi sadā priye ! | 

rudrebhyo rudravaṃśebhyo bhairavebhyo namo namaḥ | 

liṅgebhyo liṅgadevebhyo vedaśāstrebhya eva ca | 

vedebhyo'pi ca śāstrebhyastebhyo nityaṃ namo namaḥ | 

sthāvarebhyo namaskṛtya ṛṣibhyaḥ praṇamet sadā | 

praṇamāmi sadārthebhyo veśebhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

mantrebhyo'pi namaskṛtya mantrārthebhyo namo namaḥ | 

pūjopakaraṇebhyaśca brāhmaṇebhyo namo namaḥ | 

yoginībhyo namaskṛtya śaktibhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

ratnebhyo'pi namaskṛtya nadībhyaḥ praṇamet sadā | 

pīṭhebhyo'pi namaskṛtya mātṛbhyaḥ praṇamet sadā | 

annebhyo'pi namaskṛtya indrebhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

kṣetrebhyo'pi namaskṛtya kṣetrapālebhya eva ca | 

kulācalebhyo natvā tu prādurbhāvebhya eva ca | 

uktānuktañca yat kiñcittebhyo nityaṃ namo namaḥ | 

oṃ namāmi kālikāṃ devīṃ kalikalmaṣanāśinīm | 

namāmi śambhupatnīñca namāmi bhavasundarīm | 

ādyāṃ devīṃ namaskṛtya namastrailokyamohinīm | 

namāmi satyasaṅkalpāṃ sarvaparvatavāsinīm | 

pārvatīñca namaskṛtya namo nityaṃ nagātmaje ! | 

mātastvadīyacaraṇaṃ śaraṇaṃ surāṇāṃ dhyānāspadairdiśati vāñchitavāñchanīyam | 

yeṣāṃ hṛdi sphurati taccaraṇāravindaṃ dhanyāsta eva niyataṃ suralokapūjyāḥ | 

gandhaiḥ śubhaiḥ kuṅkumapaṅkalepairmātastvadīyaṃ caraṇaṃ hi bhaktāḥ | 

smaranti śṛṇvanti luṭhanti dhīrāsteṣāṃ jarā naiva bhavedbhavāni ! | 

tavāṅghripadmaṃ śaraṇaṃ surāṇāṃ parāparā tvaṃ paramā prakṛṣṭiḥ | 

dine dine devi ! bhavet karasthaḥ kimanyamuccaiḥ kathayanti santaḥ | 

kavīndrāṇāṃ darpaṃ karakamalaśobhāparicitaṃ vidhunvajjaṅghā me 
sakalagaṇametadgirisute ! | 

p. 317) atastvatpādābjaṃ janani ! cetasi mama hitaṃ nārībhūtaṃ praṇihitapadaṃ 
śāṅkaramapi | 

ye te daridrāḥ satataḥ hi mātastvadīyapādaṃ manasā namanti | 

devāsurāḥ siddhavarāśca sarve tava prasādāt satataṃ luṭhanti || 

haristvatpādābjaṃ nikhilajagatāṃ bhūtirabhavat śivo dhyātvā dhyātvā kimapi paramaṃ 
tatparataram | 

prajānāṃ nātho'yaṃ tadanu jagatāṃ sṛṣṭivihitaṃ kimanyatte mātastava caraṇayugmasya 
phalatā || 

indraḥ surāṇāṃ śaraṇaṃ śaraṇye ! prajāpatiḥ kāśyapa eva nānyaḥ | 

varaḥ patirviṣṇubhavaḥ pareśi ! tvadīyapādābjaphalaṃ samastam || 

tvadīyanābhīnavapallave vā navāṅkurairlomavaraiḥ praphullam | 

sadā vareṇye ! śaraṇaṃ vidhehi kiṃvāparaṃ cittavarairvibhāvyam | 

tvadīyapādārcitavastusambhavaḥ surāsuraiḥ pūjyamavāpa śambhuḥ | 

tvadīyapādārccanatatparo hariḥ sudarśanādhīśvaratāmupālabhat | 

dharitrī gandharūpeṇa rasena ca jalaṃ dhṛtam | 

tejo vahnisvarūpena praṇave brahmarūpadhṛk | 

mukhaṃ candrākāraṃ tribhuvanapade yāmasahitaṃ trinetraṃ me mātaḥ ! pariharati yaḥ 
syāt sa tu paśuḥ | 

na siddhistasya syāt suratasatataṃ viśvamakhilaṃ kaṭākṣaiste mātaḥ ! 
saphalapadapadmaṃ sa labhate || 

kratustvaṃ havistvaṃ svadhā tvaṃ murāreḥ purā tvaṃ parā tvaṃ sadāśīrmurāreḥ | 

harastvaṃ haristvaṃ śivastvaṃ śivānāṃ gatistvaṃ gatistvaṃ gatistvaṃ bhavāni ! || 

navāhaṃ navā tvaṃ navā vā kriyāyā varastvaṃ carustvaṃ śaraṇyaṃ dharāyāḥ | 

nadastvaṃ nadī tvaṃ gatistvaṃ nidhīnāṃ sutastvaṃ sutā tvaṃ pitā tvaṃ gṛhiṇām || 

tvadīyamuṇḍākhya bhavāni ! mālāṃ vidhāya citte bhavapadmajādayaḥ | 

surādhipatvaṃ labhate munīndraḥ śaraṇyametat kimapīha cānyat | 

narasya muṇḍañca tathā hi khaḍgaṃ bhujadvaye ye manasā japanti | 

savyetare devi ! varābhayañca bhavanti te siddhajanā munīndrāḥ | 

śivopari tvāṃ hṛdaye nidhāya japanti vidyāṃ hṛdaye kadācit | 

p. 318) sadā bhavet kāvyarasasya vettā ante padadvandvamupāśrayeta || 

digambarīṃ tvāṃ manasā vicintya japet parākhyāṃ jagatāṃ janīti | 

japetparākhyāṃ jagatāṃ matiśca kiṃvā parākhyāśaraṇaṃ bhavāmaḥ | 

śivāvirāvaiḥ pariveṣṭitāṃ tvāṃ nidhāya citte satataṃ japanti | 

bhaveya deveśi ! parāparādinirīśatāṃ devi ! parā vadandi || 

tvadīyaśṛṅgārarasaṃ nidhāya japanti mantraṃ yadi vedamukhyāḥ | 

bhavanti te devi ! janāpavādaṃ kaviḥ kavīnāmapi cāgrajanmā || 

vikīrṇaveśāṃ manasā nidhāya japanti vidyāṃ cakitaṃ kadācit | 

sudhādhipatyaṃ labhate naraḥ sa kimasti bhūmyāṃ sṛṇu kālakāli ! || 

tvadīyavījatrayamātaretajjapanti siddhāstu vimuktihetoḥ | 

tadeva mātastava pādapadmagā bhavanti siddhiśca dinatraye'pi || 

tvadīyakūrccadvayajāpakatvāt surāsurebhyo'pi bhavecca varṇaḥ | 

dhanitvapāṇḍityamayanti sarve kiṃ vā parāṃ devi ! parāparākhyā || 

tvadīyalajjādvayajāpakatvādbhavenmaheśāni ! caturthasiddhiḥ | 

tvadīyasatsiddhivaraprasādāttavādhipatyaṃ labhate nareśaḥ || 

tataḥ svanāmnaḥ śṛṇu mātaretat phalaṃ caturvarga vadanti santaḥ | 

vījatrayaṃ vai punarapyu pāsya surādhipatyaṃ labhate munīndraḥ || 

punastathā kūrccayugaṃ japanti namanti siddhā narasiṃharūpāḥ | 

tato'pi lajjādvayajāpakatvāllabhanti siddhiṃ manaso janāste | 

ante padaṃ kṣipya vibhā vibhāvasostvanmantramuddhāramidaṃ vadanti || 

tripañcāre cakre janani ! satataṃ siddhisahitāṃ vicinvan sañcinvan paramamamṛtaṃ 
dakṣiṇapadam | 

sadā kālīṃ dhyātvā vidhivihitapūjāparikarā viśeṣākhyānaṃ te na khalu 
bhavabhaṅgābhayapadaḥ || 

tvaṃ śrīstvamīśvarī kālī tvaṃ hrīstvañca karālikā | 

lajjā lakṣmīḥ satī gaurī nityācintyā citiḥ kriyā || 

parā ādyā parākhyā ca paramā paramā vasuḥ | 

akulyādyaiścitte pracayapadapadyaiḥ padayutaiḥ sadā japtvā stutvā japati hṛdi mantraṃ 
manuvidā | 

p. 319) na teṣāṃ saṃsāre vibhavaparibhaṅgapramathane kṣaṇaṃ cittaṃ devi ! prabhavati 
viruddhe parikaram || 

trayastriṃśaiḥ ślokairyadi japati mantraṃ stavati ca nameccaitānetān paramamṛtakalpaṃ 
suravaram | 

bhavet siddhiḥ śuddhau jagati śirasā tvatpadayugaṃ praṇamya prākāmyaṃ varasurajanaiḥ 
pūjyavitatim || 

iti viśvasāratantre dakṣiṇakālikākramastotraṃ nāma caturthaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

yāsāṃ yāsāṃ kramastotraṃ tāsāṃ tāsāṃ dakṣiṇāprakṛtikatvenāpavādaviṣayam 
parityajyotsargasya pravṛttitvāt dakṣiṇakālikākramastotrapāṭhenaiva tatphalanirvāhaḥ | 

samprati guhyakālyā api viśeṣa uktastatraiva ṣaṣṭhapaṭale tatkavacāt param || 

kramastavaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva kamalānane ! | 

brāhmye muhūrte utthāya gurudevānnamet sadā | 

vāruṇīmukhamāśritya namaskuryāduparyyadhaḥ | 

gurubhyo'pi namo nityaṃ namastatpādukāya ca | 

paraṃ parebhyaśca parāparebhyaḥ parātparebhyo'pi namo'stu te ca | 

ādhāraśaktiṃ namaskṛtya kulācalānnamaskuryyāt | 

dharmmakarmmaṇe namaskṛtya kūrmmarājāya vai namaḥ | 

anantāya namo nityaṃ varāhāya namo namaḥ | 

pṛthivyai ca namo nityaṃ kulācalebhyo namo namaḥ | 

samudrebhyo namo nityaṃ saratnairamṛtairjalaiḥ | 

kulavṛkṣebhyo namo nityaṃ kalpavṛkṣebhyo namo namaḥ | 

svarṇasiṃhāsanebhyaśca maṇivedyai namo namaḥ | 

caturdiśi namaskuryyāt  sarvadevān namastataḥ | 

vedebhyo'pi namo nityaṃ vedārthebhyo namo namaḥ | 

śākhābhyastasya vai nityaṃ tatpādebhyo namo namaḥ | 

tadvarṇebhyo namo nityaṃ varṇārthebhyo namo namaḥ | 

asaṃskārapadebhyaśca namo vai paramātmane | 

vidyābhyo'pi namo nityamavidyābhyo namo namaḥ | 

śāstrebhyo'pi namo nityamaśāstrebhyo namo namaḥ | 

dharmebhyo'pi namo nityamadharmebhyo namo namaḥ | 

bhāgavatebhyo namo nityaṃ vidyābhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

p. 320) śrutibhyo'pi namo nityaṃ śrutyarthebhyo namo namaḥ | 

śrīrāmabhāgavatebhyaśca daṇḍavat praṇamet sadā | 

bhāgavatebhyo namo nityaṃ bhāgavatārthebhyo namo namaḥ | 

tattvārthebhyo namo nityaṃ tattvebhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

yogebhyo'pi namo nityaṃ yogārthebhyo namo namaḥ | 

saṃhitābhyo namo nityaṃ tadarthebhyo namo namaḥ | 

mātṛkābhyo namo nityaṃ tadarthebhyo namo namaḥ | 

kalābhyo'pi namo nityaṃ śaktibhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

svarebhyo'pi namo nityaṃ rāgebhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

rāgiṇībhyo namo nityaṃ tadarthebhyo namo namaḥ | 

astrebhyo'pi namo nityaṃ śastrebhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

jīvebhyo'pi namo nityaṃ vījebhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

bhūcarebhyo namo nityaṃ khecarebhyo namo namaḥ | 

yogebhyo'pi namo nityaṃ karaṇebhyo namo namaḥ | 

muhūrtebhyo namo nityaṃ yāmebhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

ṛtubhyo'pi namo nityaṃ vatsarebhyo namo namaḥ | 

ḍākinībhyo namo nityaṃ rudrebhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

bhūtebhyo'pi namo nityaṃ pretebhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

yakṣebhyo'pi namo nityaṃ vighnebhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

bhūtebhyo'pi namo nityaṃ mahābhūtebhyo namo namaḥ | 

ācāryebhyo namo nityaṃ śaṅkarebhyo namo namaḥ | 

vaiṣṇavebhyo namo nityaṃ ye ca māyāvimohitāḥ | 

māyāyai praṇamennityaṃ prattvebhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

śarīrebhyo namo nityaṃ vyāpakebhyo namo namaḥ | 

mantrebhyo'pi namo nityaṃ yantrārthebhyo namo namaḥ | 

namo varṇātmikābhyaśca devatābhyo namo namaḥ | 

tatpadebhyo namo nityaṃ satpadebhyo namo namaḥ | 

bhāvanābhyo namo nityaṃ bhāvaśāstrebhya eva ca | 

stavebhyaḥ kavacebhyaśca nāmabhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

āgamebhyo namo nityaṃ tadarthebhyo namo namaḥ | 

tatkriyābhyo namo nityaṃ śreṣṭhaśāstreti kīrttyate | 

rasebhyo'pi namo nityaṃ gandhebhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

p. 321) pīṭhebhyo'pi namo nityaṃ tatsthebhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

sattvebhyo'pi namo nityaṃ rājasebhyo namo namaḥ | 

tāmasebhyo namo nityaṃ guṇebhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

sarvavidyāvatārebhyaḥ siddhebhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

ānandakandāya namaskṛtya kandanāya namo namaḥ | 

sarvatattvātmakamalāya prakṛtipatrebhya eva ca | 

siddhapīṭhāya sattvasya śavarūpaśivāya ca | 

tripañcārāya cakrāya namo nityaṃ namo namaḥ | 

tatrasthāyai kālikāyai guhyāyai kāmarūpiṇyai | 

gurukulādiśaktibhyaḥ kālikāyai namo namaḥ | 

namo namo namaste'stu parāparābhyo namo namaḥ | 

viśālanetrāṃ surasaṅghavanditāṃ surāsurābhyarccitapādapadmām | 

nṛmuṇḍamālākalitāśatāṅgaiḥ parāvare te prabhavanti siddhāḥ || 

sthūle kecit pravadanti siddhāḥ sūkṣmeti kecinmunayo vadanti | 

tāmambikāṃ naumi viśālanetrasumaṅgalaṃ dhehi madīyadehe || 

tvadīyapādāmbujamevametat niṣevya siddhāḥ prabhavaṃ prayānti | 

mātastavāyaṃ pāpāt sarvaśokaduḥkhājjagajjanaramye | 

ye tvā dhyāyeyurmanasi vidhānāḥ sarve te sakalaphalāḥ || 

iti viśvasāratantre guhyakālyāḥ kramastavo nāma ṣaṣṭhapaṭale ||

atha tripurāsundaryyāḥ kramastavaḥ saptamapaṭale || 

kramastavaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva kamalānane ! | 

oṃ gurubhyo namo nityaṃ parātparatarāya ca | 

parāparebhyaḥ parebhyaśca namo nityaṃ namo namaḥ | 

gobhyo namo brāhmaṇebhyo brahmaputrebhyo vai namaḥ | 

munibhyo munipatnībhyo muniputrebhyo vai namaḥ | 

digbhyo namo dikpatibhyo digambarebhyaśca vai namaḥ | 

bhramaṇebhyo namo nityaṃ rasanābhyaśca vai namaḥ | 

vedebhyo vedaśāstrebhyo vedārthebhyo namo namaḥ |

bhāgavatebhyo namo nityaṃ śrībhāgavatebhyo namo namaḥ | 

śrīrāmabhāgatebhyo namo nityaṃ bhāgavatebhyo namo namaḥ | 

tīrthebhyaḥ parvatebhyaśca pīṭhebhyaḥ satataṃ namaḥ | 

yogibhyo yoginībhyaśca yogādibhyaśca vai namaḥ | 

p. 322) dhyānebhyo mantravarṇebhyo mātṛkābhyo namo namaḥ | 

ācāryyebhyo namo nityaṃ bhairavebhyo namo namaḥ | 

rudrebhyo rudrapatnībhyo gandharvebhyo namo namaḥ | 

kinnarebhyaḥ piśācebhyaḥ kuṣmāṇḍebhyo namo namaḥ | 

avatārebhyo mūrttibhya ādityebhyo namo namaḥ | 

namaḥ saṃvatsarebhyaśca divasebhyo namo namaḥ | 

nakṣatragrahayogebhyaḥ karaṇebhyaśca vai namaḥ | 

tithibhyaḥ sarvakālebhya ṛtubhyo'pi namo namaḥ | 

kakārādyairmahādevyai kālikāyai namo namaḥ | 

ekārasthāṃ mahādevīṃ sarvādhārasvarūpiṇīm | 

īkārasthāṃ mahādevīṃ ratiṃ kāmāṅgasundarīm | 

lakārasthamahādevyai indrāṇyai sarvasiddhaye | 

hakārastha mahādevyai śivāyai sarvato namaḥ | 

etairvarṇairmahāmāyā pātu māṃ sundarī śubhā | 

sakārasthā mahāmāyā sāvitrī pātu māṃ sadā | 

śiva māṃ pātu deveśī hakārādyā sureśvarī | 

śivānī sarvadā pātu hakārasthā mahodayā | 

vakārasthā mahādevī pātu māṃ kāmadāyinī | 

lakārasthā makudevī indrāṇī pātu sarvadā | 

sarvāṅgaṃ pātu māṃ devī sarvāṇī sarvasundarī | 

etairvarṇairmahāmāyā pātu māṃ sundarī sadā | 

kakārasthā mahāmāyā brahmāṇī pātu māṃ sadā | 

hakārasthā śivā pātu sarvatra śivadūtikā | 

kakārasthā śivā pātu hakārasthā sureśvarī | 

sakārasthā sadā pātu sāvitrī śarmadāyinī | 

lakārasthā mahādevī lakṣmīrmāṃ sarvadāvatu | 

bhuvaneśī sadā pātu sarvāṅge bhavasundarī | 

anantā sarvadā pātu akārasthā sureśvarī | 

akārādyā sadā pātu śivadūtī sureśvarī | 

sakārasthā sadā pātu kāminī kāmadāyinī | 

lakāre pātu māṃ sarvakṣetre tribhuvaneśvarī | 

lakārasthā sadā pātu indrāṇī sarvadā mama | 

makārasthā sadā pātu sarvatra māṃ sureśvarī | 

ebhirvarṇaiḥ sthitā devī pātu māṃ bhuvaneśvarī | 

śivānī māṃ sadā pātu sarvatra harasundarī | 

candravījasthitā vidyā sāvitrī śambhurūpiṇī | 

p. 323) pātu māṃ sarvakṣetreṣu devatā bhuvaneśvarī | 

etairvarṇairmahāmāyā pañcaviṃśativarṇikā | 

bhuvaneśī sadā pātu sarvāṅge tripurāmbikā | 

sarvavījeṣu sarvatra yojayedbhuvaneśvarīm | 

sarvāṅge māṃ sadā pātu devī tribhuvaneśvarī | 

śikhāyāṃ bhrūyuge cākṣṇoroṣṭhayorgaṇḍayornasi | 

tathā ca civuke dante jihvāyāṃ tālumūlake | 

hṛdaye pārśvayugale pṛṣṭhe nābhau tathodare | 

nitambe ca tathopasthe guhye ca jānuyugmake | 

jānucakre ca jaṅghāyāṃ kaṭike pādake punaḥ | 

sarvāṅge pātu māṃ devī śrīmattripurasundarī | 

śāstre vāde ca saṃgrāme rājadvāre surārccane | 

prāntare giridurge ca śatrumadhye tathāpare | 

caure vyāghre ca siṃhe ca viṣamādibhayādiṣu | 

atyantarogayukteṣu pātu māṃ tripurāmbikā | 

sundarī pātu sarvatra sundarī parirakṣatu | 

idaṃ tripurasundaryyāḥ kramastotramudāhṛtam | 

yatra tatra na vaktavyaṃ yadīcchedātmano hitam | 

śaṭhāya bhaktiśūnyāya nindakāya maheśvari ! | 

nyūnāṅge cātiriktāṅge darśayenna kadācana | 

śūdrāya na ca dadyācca stavarājaṃ maheśvari ! | 

vadedvā darśayedvāpi śaṅkareṇa ca bhāṣitam | 

ślokaṃ vā stavamekaṃ vā pūjayedvā paṭhannapi | 

brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ steyaṃ gurvaṅganāgamaḥ | 

sarvamāśu taratyeva stavasyāsya prasādataḥ | 

aputro labhate putraṃ daridro labhate dhanam | 

arogī balavāṃścaiva jāyate sparśanādapi | 

kimanyadbahunā devi ! yadyadvāñchitamātmanaḥ | 

tatsarvaṃ labhate devi ! stavasyāsya prasādataḥ || 

iti viśvasāratantre tripurāsundarīkramastavaḥ samāptaḥ |

iti śrīprāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ tṛtīye'rthakāṇḍe prātaḥkṛtyavidhirūpaśākhākathanaṃ nāma 
dvitīyaḥ paricchedaḥ |



jāmale || 

snānamūlāḥ kriyāḥ sarvāḥ śrutismṛtyuditā nṛṇām | 

tasmāt snānaṃ niṣeveta śrīpuṣṭyārogyavarddhanam | 

etadvacanaṃ matsyasūkte daśamapaṭale'pi || 

snānākaraṇe doṣa ukto rāghavabhaṭṭena || 

p. 324) yataścāśucivastramasnānamanalaṅkāraṃ puruṣaṃ devatā nādhitiṣṭhantīti || 

snānabhedamāha rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍe ekaviṃśatipaṭale || 

snānantu dvividhaṃ proktaṃ majjanaṃ gātramārjjanam | 

mantrajalādibhiḥ snānamuttamaṃ parikīrttitam || 

tatprakāraṃ śṛṇu prāṇavallabha ! priyakāraṇam || 

viśvasāratantre dvitīyapaṭale || 

māntraṃ bhaumaṃ tathāgneyaṃ vāyavyaṃ divyameva ca || 

vāruṇaṃ mānasañcaiva saptasnānaṃ prakīrttitam | 

evaṃ snātvā vidhānena śucirbhavati nānyathā || 

tatra māntrasnānādikaṃ viśeṣayati tatraiva || 

āpo hiṣṭhādibhirmāntraṃ bhaumaṃ dehapramārjjanam | 

āgneyaṃ bhasmanā snānaṃ vāyavyaṃ gorajaḥ smṛtam | 

yattadātapavarṣeṇa snānaṃ divyamihocyate | 

vāruṇañcāvagāhaḥ syānmānasaṃ viṣṇucintanam | 

tatra mantrasnānaṃ dvividhamāntaraṃ vāhyañca | 

tatra vaiṣṇavasnānamāntaramuktam vaśiṣṭhasaṃhitāyām | 

anantādityasaṅkāśaṃ vāsudevaṃ caturbhujam | 

śaṅkhacakragadāpadmamukuṭaṃ vanamālinam | 

dvādaśārṇāmbujagataṃ dhyāyennārāyaṇaṃ vibhum || 

viśvasāratantre dvitīyapaṭale || 

khasthitaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ mūrttimantaṃ prabhuṃ smaran | 

tatpādodakajāṃ dhārāṃ nipatantīṃ svamūrddhani | 

cintayedbrahmarandhreṇa praviśantaṃ svakāṃ tanum | 

tayā saṃkṣālayet sarvametaddehagataṃ malam | 

tatkṣaṇādvirajo mantrī jāyate sphaṭikopamaḥ | 

idaṃ snānavaraṃ mantrāt sahasramadhikaṃ smṛtamiti || 

iti vaiṣṇavāntarasnānam || 

śāktamabhyantarasnānamuktaṃ śrīpañcamīmate || 

snānaprakāro dvividho vāhyābhyantarabhedataḥ | 

āntaraṃ snānamatyantaṃ rahasyamapi sādarāt | 

kathayāmi bhavadhvastyai caturvargaphalāptaye | 

saṃvittrayamanusmṛtya caraṇatrayamadhyataḥ | 

sravantaṃ saccidānandapravāhaṃ bhāvagocaram | 

vimuktisādhanaṃ puṃsāṃ smaraṇādeva yoginām | 

tena plāvitamātmānaṃ bhāvayedbhavaśāntaye || 

iti śākte āntarasnānam | 

śaivāntarasnānaṃ śaivāgame uktam | 

manasā mūlamantreṇa prāṇāyāmapuraḥsaram | 

p. 325) kurvīta mānasasnānaṃ sarvatra vihitañca yat || 

gandharvatantre ṣaṣṭhapaṭale || 

snānañca trividhaṃ proktaṃ sandhyā ca trividhā smṛtā | 

āntarañca bhaveddevi ! vāhyaṃ mānasameva ca | 

mānasaṃ vai bhavet snānaṃ yaḥ sadā śuddhamānasaḥ | 

sparśalepādike vāpi nirvikalpaḥ sadaiva hi | 

vāhyamantraiśca samproktamāntaraṃ śṛṇu sāmpratam | 

prāguktakramayogena prāṇāyāmaparo budhaḥ | 

śaktiṃ paraśivenaiva saṅgamayya vidhanataḥ | 

tadudbhavāmṛte śaśvannimajjya punarevahīti || 

atha nityasnānaphalam matsyasūkte daśamapaṭale || 

yāmyaṃ hi yātanāduḥkhaṃ nityasnāyī na paśyati | 

nityasnānena pūjyante ye'pi pāpakṛto janāḥ | 

agamyāgamanāt pāpāt pāpibhyaśca pratigrahāt | 

rahasyācaritāt pāpānmucyate snānamācaran || 

atha vāhyasnānam || 

rudrajāmale pūrvakhaṇḍe dvitīyapaṭale || 

nadīsarastaḍāgeṣu kūpavāpīṣu vā punaḥ | 

prātarmadhyāhnasāyāhne naraḥ snāyād yathāvidhi || 

viśvasāratantre dvitīyapaṭale || 

vidhinā snānamuddiśya kuryyāt pūrvāhṇikīṃ kriyām | 

nadīṃ vā devakhātaṃ vā dīrghikāṃ sāgaraṃ tathā || 

etān prāpya snānamuddiśya paurvāhṇikīṃ kriyāṃ kuryyādityanvayaḥ | 

nadyādīnāmuddhṛtajalāpekṣayā sthānasthajalasya prāśastyamuktam tatraiva || 

gaṅgādyāḥ saritaḥ sarvāḥ śasyante sthānato budhaiḥ | 

kāśyādisarvatīrthāni bhārate yāni santi vai | 

tāni sthāne praśasyante sarvapāpaharāṇi vai || 

iti vakṣyamāṇavacane yojanaparyyantadeśasya gaṅgātvāt kaimutikanyāyasiddhaṃ 
gaṅgājalasyoddhṛtasyāpi prāśastyamiti | 

evañca gaṅgādyā ityatra tadguṇasaṃvijñānabahubrīhiṇā gaṅgābhinnā iti jñeyam || 

nīlatantre dvitīyapaṭale || 

mṛtkuśānapi saṃgṛhya gatvā jalāntikaṃ tataḥ | 

malāpakarṣaṇaṃ snātvā mantrasnānaṃ samācaret || 

śākte kuśān viśeṣayati śyāmārahasyadhṛtam || 

tarjjanyā rajataṃ dhāryyaṃ svarṇaṃ dhāryyamanāmayā | 

p. 326) eṣa eva kuśaḥ śākte na darbho vanasambhavaḥ || 

iti śāktakuśaḥ | 

mahānirvāṇatantre pañcamollāse || 

ādāvapa upaspṛśya praviśet salile tataḥ | 

nābhimātrajale sthitvā malānāmapanuttaye | 

sakṛt snātvā tathonmajjya mantrasnānamathācaret || 

etena malāpakarṣaṇaṃ vinā saṅkalpapūrvakasnānamātramanuśāsanaviruddham | 

kecittu prāguktaviśvasāravacane 
paurvāhṇikakriyānantarasnānadarśanādakṛtatatkriyāstailābhyaktā api snānāt pūrvaṃ 
paurvāhṇikakṛtyamācaranti | 

tanna manoramam |

mantrasnānaṃ kṛtvā devagṛhamāgatya sammārjanādītyādyuktam || 

nīlatantre || 

vidyayā trirnimajjyaivamācaret pāthasā tathā | 

punarnimajjya payasi saṅkalpañca samāpayet | 

iṣṭadevyāḥ pūjanārthaṃ kuryyāt snānaṃ jalāśaye || 

mantratantraprakāśe || 

aruṇe'nudite mantrī tīrthe vā vimale jale | 

vaidikaṃ snānamācaryya tāntrikaṃ snānamācaret | 

iti vaidikasnānānantaraṃ tāntrikasnānam || 

gandharvatantre prathamapaṭale || 

praṇavaṃ pūrvamuddhṛtya taditi paratastataḥ |

kulacūḍāmaṇau || 

kṛṣṇaraktaharinnīlā vividhā mama mūrttayaḥ | 

tatra yaḥ kulagaḥ śiṣyaḥ sa tadrūpaṃ parāmṛṣan |

nitye naimittike kāmye pitṛdaivatakarmaṇi | 

p. 327) saṅkalpapūrvakaṃ karma anyathā naṃ phalaṃ smṛtam || 

iti saṅkalpākaraṇe doṣaḥ || 

saṅkalpārthaṃ niṣiddhavihitapātramapi tatraiva || 

śuktiśaṅkhāśmahastaiśca kāṃsyaraupyādibhistathā | 

saṅkalpo naiva karttavyo mṛṇmayaiśca kadācana || 

iti saṅkalpe niṣiddhapātram || 

gṛhītvauḍumbaraṃ pātraṃ sauvarṇañca viśeṣataḥ | 

saṅkalpantu budhaḥ kuryyānmedhyapatrapuṭena vā || 

iti saṅkalpe vihitapātraṃ tatraiva || 

snānakāle tu hastena tāmrapātreṇa vā tataḥ | 

paitṛke śrāddhapātreṇa daive śaṅkhaḥ praśasyate || 

iti karma viśeṣe pātraviśeṣaḥ || 

majjanaprakāramāha tatraiva || 

srotaso'bhimukho majjet sūryyābhimukhato'tha vā | 

smarennārāyaṇaṃ gaṅgāmiṣṭadevaṃ tataḥ param | 

majjanaṃ muktakeśena badhnīyācca śikhāṃ punaḥ | 

tataḥ kṛtāñjalirbhūtvā tīrthānyāvāhayettataḥ | 

oṃ gaṅge ca yamune caiva godāvari sarasvati | 

narmade sindhu kāveri jale'smin sannidhiṃ kuru | 

brahmāṇḍe devatīrthāni karaiḥ spṛṣṭāni te raveḥ | 

tena satyena me deva ! tīrtthaṃ dehi divākara ! | 

priyavratāni tīrthāni sūryyaraśmisthitāni ca | 

āgatyārghyaṃ gṛhītvā ca sarvasiddhiṃ prayaccha me | 

caturasraṃ vinirmāya tatra snānaṃ diśet sudhīḥ || 

rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtam | 

āvāhayāmi tvāṃ devi ! snānārthamiha sundari ! | 

ehi gaṅge ! namastubhyaṃ sarvatīrthasamanvitā | 

iti mantrairaṅkuśamudrayā ādityamaṇḍalāttīrthamāvāhya vamityambhasi saṃyojya 
somasūryyāgnimaṇḍalāni tatra sañcintya vamityamṛtavījena dvādaśadhā abhimantrya 
kavacenāvaguṇṭhya astreṇa saṃrakṣya mūlamantreṇaikādaśadhā abhimantrayet | 

tatpramāṇaṃ vaśiṣṭhasaṃhitāyām || 

hṛnmantro'ṅkuśamudrābhyāṃ tīrthamākṛṣya maṇḍalāt | 

āvāhyāmbhasi saṃyojya somasūryyāgnimaṇḍalam | 

sañcintya mantrī tanmadhye nimajjet susamāhitaḥ | 

mūlamantraṃ samāvarttya manasollikhyam cākṛti || 

jñānārṇave || 

p. 328) vinyasyāṅge ṣaḍaṅgāni prāṇāyāmapuraḥsaram | 

śrīsūryyamaṇḍalāttīrthamākṛṣyāṅkuśamudrayā |

matsyasūkte'pi | 

rudrasaṃkhyaṃ japenmantramācchādya mīnamudrayā |

ravisaṃkhyayā dvādaśasaṃkhyāyā |

caraṇānniḥsṛte toye trirnimajjya japenmanum |

tathaiva bhāgāntareṇa parilepayedadhobhāgamiti śeṣaḥ || 

lepanaprakāro matsyasūkte ||

viniyogaḥ |

gātralepanamantrastu viśvasāre || 

oṃ aśvakrānte rathakrānte viṣṇukrānte vasundhare ! | 

mṛttike ! hara me pāpaṃ yanmayā duṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam | 

oṃ uddhṛtāsi varāheṇa kṛṣṇena śatabāhunā |

p. 329) vāmahaste kṛtā muṣṭirdakṣahastasya pārvati ! | 

kalasākhyā bhavenmudrā sarvapāpaharā śubhā || 

vaśiṣṭhasaṃhitāyām ||

pīḍayitvā svakaṃ coru prakṣālyācamya yatnataḥ || 

iti saṃkṣepatarpaṇam || 

parakhāte tu viśeṣo'sti viśvasāre dvitīyapaṭale || 

parakhāte tu karttavyaḥ paṅkapiṇḍoddharaḥ sadā | 

uttiṣṭhottiṣṭha paṅka ! tvaṃ tyaja puṇyaṃ parasya ca | 

pāpāni vināśaya me śāntiṃ dehi sadā mama || 

iti parakhātetikarttavyatā || 

atha ayanādau tailasnānaniṣedhaḥ || 

yoginītantre uttarakhaṇḍe navamapaṭale || 

ayane viṣuve caiva bhaumavāre dinakṣaye | 

dvādaśyāṃ rāhugraste ca tailasnānaṃ na kārayet || 

atha gātramārjjanaṃ tatraiva || 

vastreṇa mārjayeddevi ! kārpāsenātha vālkalaiḥ | 

raktavastrairbhavet kuṣṭhī pāṇḍarairvyādhimāpnuyāt | 

na kuryyāt kusumaiścaiva kṛtvā nāśamavāpnuyāt | 

paṭaiścakṣuḥ samāpnoti nīlairaktakṣayaṃ vrajet | 

madhyāhnasnāne tu gātramārjananiṣedho matsyasūkte daśamapaṭale || 

madhyāhnasnānaṃ deveśi ! vastreṇa vātha pāṇinā | 

na ca pramārjayedgātraṃ prātarbhogyaṃ na duṣyati | 

pretasnāne ca tīrthe ca gaṅgāyāṃ sārvakālikam | 

na tūlikasya vījena gātraṃ naiva pramārjayet | 

eṣu gātraṃ naiva mārjayet | 

adhikadoṣadarśanārthaṃ na tūlikasya vījenetyuktam | 

madhyāhnegātramārjananiṣedhakāraṇamapi tatraiva || 

devāḥ pibanti śiraso mukhasya pitarastathā | 

urasaḥ pitṛgandharvā adhare sarvajantavaḥ | 

tasmānna mārjayed gātraṃ madhyāhne tu viśeṣataḥ | 

puraścaraṇarasollāse || 

gaṅgāsnānaṃ vinā devi ! pūjayed yastu kālikām | 

daśavidyā maheśāni ! pūjayed yastu bhaktitaḥ | 

sarvaṃ tasya vṛthā devi ! gaṅgāsnānaṃ vinā priye ! | 

p. 330) gaṅgāmantraṃ samuccāryya kṣetre nadyāntu pārvati ! | 

snāpayed yastu pāpātmā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate | 

dhyānañcaiva tathā mantraṃ gaṅgāyāḥ śṛṇu sundari ! | 

tantroktaṃ dhyānamantrañca praśastaṃ bhārate kalau || 

atha dhyānam || 

śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśāṃ śuklāmbaravibhūṣitām | 

śuklamuktāvalīmālāṃ hṛdayopariśobhitām | 

śvetamālādharāṃ devīṃ śvetābharaṇabhūṣitām | 

sadā ṣoḍaśavarṣīyāṃ brahmādipariṣevitām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā japedvidyāṃ gaṅgāyāḥ śṛṇu śaṅkari ! | 

praṇavaṃ pūrvamuccāryya māyābījaṃ samuddharet | 

gaṅgāyai parameśāni | 

tataḥ paramudīrayet | 

pūrvavījadvayañcoktvā vahnijāyāṃ samuddharet | 

eṣā navākṣarī vidyā suṣumnāmārgasaṃsthitā || 1 || 

aparaikaṃ pravakṣyāmi mantraṃ paramagopanam | 

praṇavaṃ pūrvamuddhṛtya gaṅgāyai tadanantaram | 

punaḥ praṇavamuddhṛtya mantrametadudīrayet || 2 || 

aparaikaṃ pravakṣyāmi suṣumnāmārgasaṃsthitam | 

māyābījaṃ samuddhṛtya punaḥ praṇavamuddharet | 

tato gaṅgāpadaṃ devi ! caturthyantaṃ kuru priye ! | 

pūrvavījadvayaṃ proktvā mantrametaṃ japaṃ kuru | 

eṣā saptākṣarī vidyā sadā me śirasi sthitā || 3 || 

aparaikaṃ pravakṣyāmi mantraṃ paramadurlabham | 

māyābījaṃ samuddhṛtya gaṅgāyai tadanantaram | 

punarmāyāṃ samuddhṛtya mantrametadudīrayet || 4 || 

anena dhyānamārgeṇa mantramārgeṇa pārvati ! | 

pūjayet satataṃ gaṅgāṃ tasya siddhiradūrataḥ | 

dine dine ca japtvā vai gaṅgāsnānaphalaṃ labhet | 

tathā | 

tasmādādau japedvidyāṃ gaṅgāyāḥ surapūjite ! | 

gaṅgāpi svayamāyāti pūtārthaṃ sādhakasya ca || 

iti  gaṅgāmantraphalam || 

yā gaṅgā mahatī māyā kuṇḍalī parakuṇḍalī | 

sā gaṅgā parameśāni ! brahmāṇḍaṃ vyāpya tiṣṭhati |
