mahānadanadīrūpā sarvaṃ vyāpya hi tiṣṭhati || 

gaṅgākṣetramāhātmyamapi tatraiva || 

yatra kṣetre nadī devī ! āyāme yojanāt priye ! | 

sā gaṅgā cañcalāpāṅgi ! kiñcinnāstyatra saṃśayaḥ | 

p. 331) na hi gaṅgāsamaṃ puṇyaṃ mama jñāne hi vidyate | 

yoginītantre pūrvakhaṇḍe aṣṭādaśapaṭale || 

māhātmyaṃ kimu vakṣyāmi gaṅgāyāśca sureśvari ! | 

yannāmasmaraṇādeva pāpino muktibhāginaḥ | 

gaṅgā gaṅgeti yo brūyāt pāpināmapi pātakī | 

teṣāñca pātakaṃ hitvā tairgacchedvaiṣṇavīṃ purīm || 

iti gaṅgānāmamāhātmyam | 

yāni kāni ca pāpāni proktāni te maheśvari ! | 

prāyaścittavihīnāni prāyaścittaparāṇyapi | 

tāni sarvāṇi naśyanti gaṅgānīrābhiṣekataḥ || 

iti gaṅgājalamāhāmtyam || 

nagarī ghargharādyambu patedgaṅgā pravāhayet | 

sarvaṃ gaṅgā bhavatyeva mantramāhātmyataḥ śive ! | 

yatra deśe vahed gaṅgā sa deśaḥ puṇyabhājanaḥ | 

puṇyakṣetraṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ pavitraṃ yojanadvayam | 

tatra yat kriyate karmma gaṅgāyāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ | 

gaṅgāyāṃ yat kṛtaṃ devi ! tadakṣayaphalaṃ labhet | 

saṅgamaṃ vā vihīnaṃ vā kathitaṃ śambhuvallabhe ! | 

tatrasthāḥ prāṇinaḥ sarve devalokaviniḥsṛtā | 

bhuktvā ca vividhān bhogān kṛtvā ca sukṛtaṃ sadā | 

anāyāsena yāsyanti sthānaṃ paramadurlabham | 

iti sagaṅgadeśaphalam || 

yatra gaṅgā na śocanti devarṣigaṇasaṃstutāḥ | 

gaṅgāyāṃ tyajate prāṇān yastu puṇyaprabhāvataḥ | 

jñānato mokṣamāpnoti vaikuṇṭhaṃ tadabhāvataḥ | 

svarlokādyāmaheśāni ! gaṅgā pāpavivarjitaḥ || 

iti gaṅgāyāṃ prāṇatyāgaphalam || 

gaṅgāmuddiśya vrajataḥ pathi prāṇān vimuñcati | 

viṣṇulokaṃ bahuguṇaṃ pāpī cetso'pi gacchati || 

iti gaṅgāyātrikasya pathi mṛtyuphalam | 

tattīre yastyajet prāṇān nyāyato'nyāyato'pi vā | 

so'pi svargamavāpnoti sarvasambhārasaṃyutam | 

iti gaṅgātīramṛtyuphalam | 

yāvadasthīni gaṅgāyāṃ nikṣipyante mṛtasya ca | 

tāvadabdasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate | 

iti gaṅgāyāṃ mṛtāsthinikṣepaphalam || 

gaṅgājalasamāyogān mriyate yatra kutracit | 

sarvapāpavinirmukto viṣṇuloke mahīyate | 

p. 332) ityanyatra gaṅgājalasamāyogamaraṇaphalam || 

gaṅgātīre caturhaste piṇḍaṃ dadyāt samāhitaḥ | 

pitṝṇāṃ niṣkṛtiṃ kṛtvā viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 

iti caturhastamadhye piṇḍadānaphalam || 

gaṅgāyāṃ tarpaṇaṃ devi ! puṇyavān yaḥ samācaret | 

mahātṛptirbhavet satyaṃ pitṝṇāñca śatābdikī | 

ṛṣīṇāṃ devatānāñca tathaiva samudāhṛtam | 

iti gaṅgāyāṃ tarpaṇaphalam || 

divā vā yadi vā ratrau sandhyāyāṃ vā mahāniśi | 

snānaṃ dānaṃ tapo homastarpaṇaṃ pūjanaṃ śive ! | 

sarvaṃ kuryyāttu gaṅgāyāṃ kālabhedaṃ na cācaret | 

iti gaṅgāyāṃ paryyudastakālābhāvaḥ | 

kālabhedaṃ samācaryya yadi kāryyaṃ tyajet śive ! | 

tatastu sthāvaro bhūyādaraṇye tadanantaram | 

dāvāgninā śikhā tasya dagdhāśu bhasmanaikadhā | 

tadante parameśāni ! cāṇḍālo nityaduḥkhitaḥ | 

jāyate saptajanmāni tadante rajako bhavet | 

janmatrayaṃ maheśāni ! tadante śūdrayoniṣu | 

daśajanma maheśāni ! tato vaiśyatvamāpnuyāt | 

caturjanmavyatīte tu kṣatriyatvaṃ trijanmataḥ | 

brāhmaṇatvaṃ tataḥ prāpya labhet puṇyagatiṃ naraḥ | 

iti gaṅgāyāṃ kālākālavicāranindā || 

gaṅgāyāṃ harate yo hi yatkiñcit parameśvari ! | 

tasya mohāndhatamaso rauravānnāsti niṣkṛtiḥ | 

ābhūtasaṃplavaṃ devi ! kathitaṃ te sureśvari ! | 

iti gaṅgāyāṃ kiñciddharaṇadoṣaḥ || 

gaṅgātīre ca gaṅgāyāṃ pratigṛhṇāti yo naraḥ | 

śvapaco jāyate nityaṃ daśajanmani kāmini ! | 

tato dāridryadoṣeṇa paribhramati medinīm | 

saptajanma mahādevi ! tadante niṣkṛtiṃ labhet || 

iti gaṅgādau pratigrahaṇadoṣaḥ || 

evante kathitaṃ tasya mantramāhātmyamuttamam | 

kālikāyā maheśāni ! gaṅgāmāhātmyakāraṇam || 

iti yoginītantre gaṅgāmāhātmyam ||

oṃ sisṛkṣornikhilaṃ viśvaṃ muhuḥ śukraṃ prajāyate | 

mātaraḥ sarvabhūtānāmāpo devyaḥ punantu mām | 

p. 333) tāravāruṇavījena puṭitaṃ tat samuccaret | 

alakṣmīṃ malarūpāṃ yāṃ sarvabhūteṣu saṃsthitām | 

kṣālayanti nijasparśādāpo nityaṃ punantu mām | 

iti mantrābhyāṃ rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtābhyāmātmānamabhiṣicya | 

oṃ yanme keśeṣu daurbhāgyaṃ sīmante yacca mūrddhani | 

lalāṭe karṇayorakṣṇorāpastattvaṃ tu vo namaḥ | 

āyurārogyamaiśvaryamaripakṣakṣayaṃ sukham | 

santoṣakṣāntirāstikyaṃ vidyā bhavatu vo namaḥ | 

ityanena rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtena jalaṃ namet || 

atha mānasasnānam || 

tatra prathamaṃ bandhamokṣādau manasaḥ kāraṇatvaṃ darśayati || 

yoginītantre tṛtīyabhāge aṣṭamapaṭale || 

mana eva tu vai nityaṃ mana eva tu kāraṇam | 

mana eva manuṣyāṇāṃ kāraṇaṃ bandhamokṣayoḥ | 

tasya niyantraṇādeva saṃsārī na bhavet kvacit | 

vadatyanyaṃ karotyanyaṃ yattu vāṅmanasi sthitam | 

darśayati tathā cānyaṃ vṛthā tasya kriyā bhavet | 

ityadhikaṃ matsyasūkte ṣaḍviṃśatipaṭale || 

cintayed yaḥ kṛtaṃ duṣṭaṃ tīrthasnānena tasya kim | 

śataśo'pi jalairdhautaṃ surābhāṇḍamivāśucirityapi matsyasūkte || 

yoginītantre || 

tanneṣṭaṃ tatparaṃ jātaṃ tanmukhyaṃ duḥkhakāraṇam | 

cittamantargataṃ duṣṭaṃ tīrthasnāne niṣiddhyate | 

sarvadāpi jalairdhautaṃ surābhāṇḍamivāśuciḥ | 

yadi vasati guhāyāṃ parvatāgre ciraṃ vā yadi vasati trikhaṇḍaṃ tasya piṇḍaṃ paṭaṃ 
vā | 

yadi paṭhati purāṇaṃ vedasiddhāntatattvaṃ yadi hṛdayamaśuddhaṃ sarvametadviruddham | 

na tīrthāni na dānāni na vratāni na cāśramāḥ | 

duṣṭāśayaṃ duṣṭamatiṃ pāvayanti kadācana | 

iti duṣṭamanaso doṣakathanam || 

tathaiva || 

itiṃ ceto vaśīkṛtya yatra tatra vasennaraḥ | 

tatra tasya kurukṣetraṃ prayāgaṃ puṣkaraṃ gayā || 

iti śuddhacetasaḥ phalakathanam | 

jñānasaṅkulyām || 

idaṃ tīrthamidaṃ tīrthaṃ bhramanti tāmasā janāḥ | 

ātmatīrthaṃ na jānanti kathaṃ muktā varānane ! || 

p. 334) rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍe ekaviṃśatipaṭale || 

snānamātreṇa muktaḥ syāt pāpaśailādanantagaḥ | 

snāyācca vimale tīrthe hṛdayāmbhojapuṣkare | 

bindutīrthe'thavā snāyāt sarvajanmāghamuktaye | 

iḍāsuṣumne śivatīrthake'smin jñānāmbupūrṇe vahataḥ śarīre | 

brahmāmbubhiḥ snāti tayoḥ sadā yaḥ kiṃ tasya gāṅgairapi puṣkarairvā || 

etadvacanadvayaṃ kalpasūtraṭīkāyāmudayākarapaddhatyāmiti kṛtvā likhitam || 

jñānasaṅkulyām || 

iḍā bhāgīrathī gaṅgā piṅgalā yamunā nadī | 

tayormadhyagatā nāḍī suṣumnākhyā sarasvatī | 

triveṇīsaṅgamo yatra tīrtharājaḥ sa ucyate | 

tatra snānaṃ prakurvīta sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 

iti mānasaprayogaḥ || 

rudrajāmale || 

iḍā malasthānanivāsinī yā sūryyātmikā yā yamunā pravāhikā | 

tathā suṣumnā maladeśagāminī sarasvatī rakṣati majjanātmakam | 

manogatasnānaparo manuṣyo mantrakriyāyogaviśiṣṭatattvavit | 

mahīsthatīrthe vimale jale mudā mūlāmbuje snāti sumuktibhāg bhavet | 

sarvāṇi tīrthe suratīrthapāvanī gaṅgā mahāsattvavinirgatā satī | 

karoti pāpakṣayameva muktiṃ dadāti sākṣādamalārthapuṇyadā | 

iti mūlādhārasthatīrtham || 

svargasthaṃ yāvatā tīrthaṃ svādhiṣṭhāne supaṅkaje || 

mano nidhāya yogīndraḥ snāti gaṅgājale yathā | 

iti svādhiṣṭhānasthatīrtham | 

maṇipūre devatīrthaṃ pañcakuṇḍaṃ sarovaram | 

tatra śrīkāmanātīrthaṃ snāti yo muktimicchati | 

iti maṇipūratīrtham || 

anāhate sarvatīrthaṃ sūryyamaṇḍalamadhyagam | 

vibhāvya sarvatīrthāni snāti yo muktimicchati | 

ityanāhatatīrtham || 

viśuddhākhye mahāpadme aṣṭatīrthasamudbhavaḥ | 

kaivalyamuktidaṃ dhyātvā snāti vīro vimuktaye | 

iti viśuddhasthatīrtham | 

mānasaṃ bindutīrthañca kālīkuṇḍaṃ kalādharam | 

ājñācakre sadā dhyātvā snāti nirvāṇasiddhaye | 

ityājñāsthatīrtham || 

etat kulapriyasnānaṃ kurvanti yogino mudā | 

p. 335) ato vīrāḥ sattvayuktāḥ sarvasiddhiyutāḥ surāḥ | 

nānāpāpaṃ sadā kṛtvā brahmahatyāvinirgatam | 

kṛtvā snānaṃ mahātīrthe siddhāḥ syuraṇimādigāḥ | 

snānamātreṇa niṣpāpo'śaktaḥ syādvāyusaṃgrahe | 

tīrthānāṃ darśanādeṣāṃ mukto yogī bhaved dhruvam || 

iti mānasasnānaphalam || 

matsyasūkte daśamapaṭale || 

jalādutthāya karttavyaṃ jale caivopaviśya yat | 

anayorviparītena yatkaroti na tatkṛtam | 

yajjale śuṣkavastreṇa sthale caivārdravāsasā | 

na kuryyādāhnikaṃ karma jale cāpi sthaleṣu ca | 

iti jale śuṣkavāsasā sthale cārdravāsasā karmmaniṣedhaḥ || 

gautamīye || 

pīḍayitvāmbaraṃ coru prakṣālyācamya vāgyataḥ | 

dhārayedvāsasī śuddhe paridhānottarīyake | 

acchinne sadaśe śukle ācāmet pīṭhasaṃsthitaḥ ||

matsyasūkte ||

uttīrya vāsasī dhaute paridhāya vicakṣaṇaḥ | 

na raktaṃ malinañcaiva na nīlañca praśasyate | 

aṣṭau sapta tathā ṣaṣṭha caturhastānyanukramāt | 

brāhmaṇādikrameṇaiva pidhānāmbaramīritam | 

iti paridheyavastraparimāṇam || 

mātsye || 

śvetaṃ raktaṃ tathā pītaṃ kṛṣṇañcetyanupūrvaśaḥ | 

anupūrvaśa iti brāhmaṇādyanukrameṇa | 

vastramanyadhṛtañcaiva sadaiva parivarjayet |

vastramūlaṃ samuddhṛtya pṛṣṭhavaṃśe ṣaḍaṅgulam | 

daśāyāṃ tryaṅgulaṃ tyaktvā nābhimātre vinirdiśet | 

daśāhīnaṃ na karttavyaṃ parivarttaṃ na kārayet | 

iti vastraparidhānaprakāraḥ || 

atha karmaviśeṣe vastraviśeṣaḥ ||

udayabhānunāthakṛtakṛṣṇārcanacandrikāyām || 

śyāmārahasyadhṛtasvatantratantre || 

mokṣārthī raktavastre dve bhogārthī śvetavāsasī | 

māraṇe kṛṣṇavāsastu vaśye raktaṃ sadā gṛhī | 

evañca pūrvoktamatsyasūktavacane yadraktavastrasyāpraśastatva-muktaṃ 
tanmokṣādiviśeṣakarmmātiriktakarmmaparaṃ sāmānyaviśeṣanyāyāt | 

ata-eva annadākalpoktavakṣyamāṇavacane raktavastraparidhānaṃ vakṣyate | 

p. 336) uccāṭe vyāghracarmmāṇi vṛkṣatvak stambhakarmmaṇi | 

paridhāya tato mantrī yāgabhūmimathāviśet | 

matsyasūkte || 

na caivaṃ dhunayedvastraṃ na keśān dhunayet kvacit || 

nīlīraktena vastreṇa yatkarmma kurute dvijaḥ | 

snānaṃ dānaṃ japaṃ homaṃ tatsarvaṃ viphalaṃ bhavet || 

iti nīlādivastreṇa karmmakaraṇaniṣedhaḥ || 

kalau kārpāsavastrañca paṭṭaṃ kṣaumaṃ praśasyate | 

nityaṃ kāpacitaṃ kuryyādaśuddhaṃ śuddhatāniyāt | 

kena jalenāpacitaṃ prakṣālitabhityarthaḥ | 

iti kalau kārpāsādivastrapraśaṃsā || 

sūcīviddhena vastreṇa ākhudaṣṭena vā dvijaḥ | 

kaṭiśūnyena deveśi ! sarvaṃ snānaphalaṃ haret || 

iti sūcīviddhādivastraniṣedhaḥ | 

vastrāntareṇa yadvastraṃ kṛmijaṃ vā pradṛśyate | 

na deyaṃ daive paitrye ca koṣṭhajañca praśasyate || 

iti vijātīyavastraniṣedhaḥ | 

kṛmijena tu vastreṇa yaḥ snānaṃ kurute naraḥ | 

sarvakālakṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ snānamātreṇa naśyati || 

iti kṛmijavastreṇa snānaniṣedhaḥ | 

etadeva śvetajātavṛkṣajāte na duṣyati | 

śvetakṛmeśca sambhūtaṃ praśastaṃ daivapaitryayoḥ || 

iti kṛmijaśvetavastrapraśaṃsā || 

athottarīyam || 

matsyasūkte || 

śatadvayena sūtreṇa uttīrayasya mānakam || 

tadarddhenātha vā kāryyamabhāve sarvataḥ svayam | 

yogapadādīnāṃ pūjādāvupayogāttallakṣaṇaṃ vīramitrodayadhṛtasiddhāntaśekhare || 

trividhaṃ yogapadakamādyaṃ vyāghrājinodbhavam | 

dvitīyaṃ mṛgacarmmāḍhyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tantunirmmitam | 

caturmmātrapravistāraṃ dairghyeṇa yajñasūtravat | 

caturmātraṃ caturaṅgulamātram || 

yajñopavītamārjanasya snānāt parataḥ karttavyatvaṃ tadatra likhyate || 

gāyatrītantre caturthabrāhmaṇapaṭale || 

sammārjya dadhidugdhena ghṛtena bahuyatnataḥ | 

ghṛtādyabhāve cārvaṅgi ! mārjayettaṇḍulapiṣṭakaiḥ | 

tadabhāve sārṣapeṇa tilatailaṃ parityajet | 

vilvasya phalaniryāsairmārjayet dvijasattamaḥ | 

śuddhasattvātmakaṃ sūtraṃ tadā bhavati pratyaham |

p. 337) iti yajñopavītamārjanadravyāṇi || 

yajñopavītamārjanaprakārastu tatraiva || 

vāmaskandhāt samuttāryya vāmāṅguṣṭhe niyojayet | 

vāmāṅguṣṭhaṃ vāmaskandhamevameva na cānyathā | 

vāmāṅguṣṭhayutaṃ sūtraṃ kṛtvā sammārjayed dvijaḥ || 

iti yajñopavītamārjanaprakāraḥ | 

kaṇṭhādau yajñopavītakaraṇadoṣaśca tatraiva || 

kaṇṭhe śirasi dakṣakare kṛtvā śūdratvamarhati | 

gāyatryā ayutaṃ japtvā tasmāt pāpāt pramucyate | 

tathā | 

varṇavipre tathā bhaṭṭe mārjanaṃ na hitaṃ sadā | 

dhāraṇaṃ yajñasūtrasya jātibhede pṛthak pṛthak |

raktacandanatoyena mārjayet kṣatrajātayaḥ | 

hāridrairmārjayedvaiśya iti śāstrasya nirṇayaḥ ||

atha tilakaṃ matsyasūkte daśamapaṭale || 

athopaviśya pūrvāśāmukho bhūtvā susaṃyutaḥ | 

prakuryyāttilakaṃ mantrī uttarāśāmukhena vā | 

gopīcandanakenātha rocanākuṅkumena vā | 

mṛttikayā malayajena tulasīvilvajena vā || 

yoginītantre tṛtīyabhāge navamapaṭale || 

malayajena gandhena gopīcandanakena vā | 

vilvakāṣṭhodbhavenātha tulasīkāṣṭhakena vā | 

padmakena tamālena tathā rocanayāthavā | 

pidhāya tilakaṃ śreṣṭhameṣāmekatamena vā || 

vilvakāṣṭhodbhavenetyudāsīnaparam | 

tatkāṣṭhacandanaṃ bhāle yo dhārayati sambhramādityādi yoginītantrīyapūrvakhaṇḍe 
pañcamapaṭaloktavacanāt || 

mṛdādiphalaṃ matsyasūkte || 

mṛdā tu muktimāpnoti āyuṣyaṃ candanena tu | 

kuṅkumena bhavetkāryyaṃ rocanā vaśyamīritam | 

jalasthāmṛttikā varjyā tīrasthaṃ puṇyadāyakam | 

tasmāttīrodbhavaṃ grāhyamaśvakrānteti sampaṭhan | 

notthāya tilakaṃ kuryyānmudā strīsarito budhaḥ | 

śastañca jāhnavītīrodbhavairvā gopicandanaiḥ || 

iti tilakasya mṛttikāvidhiniṣedhau | 

tilakakaraṇamantrastu matsyasūkte | 

keśavānanta ! govinda ! varāha ! puruṣottama ! puṇyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ tilakaṃ me 
prasīdatu || 

candanadhāraṇamantro'pi tatraiva || 

kāntiṃ lakṣmīṃ dhṛtiṃ saukhyaṃ saubhāgyamatulaṃ mama | 

p. 338) dadātu candanaṃ nityaṃ satataṃ dhārayāmyaham | 

ityanena ca mantreṇa pradadhyānmantravittamaḥ || 

tilakadhāraṇasthānāni matsyasūkte || 

lalāṭe prathamaṃ dadyānmūrddhni dadyāttato hṛdi | 

kaṇṭhe ca śrotrayorbāhvorbāhumūladvaye'pi ca | 

nābhau pṛṣṭhe ca sandadhyāt pārśvayośca yugaṃ yugam | 

tilakāni likheccaiva mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ | 

bhāle dīpaśikhākāraṃ bāhubhyāṃ vilvapatravat | 

hṛdaye kamalākāraṃ grīvāyāṃ candramuddiśet || 

vaiṣṇavatilake tu viśeṣaḥ || 

lalāṭe tu gadā kāryyā mūrddhni cāpaṃ śaraṃ tathā | 

nandakañcaiva hṛnmadhye śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ bhujadvaye | 

śaṅkhacakrāṅkito vipraḥ śmaśāne mriyate yadi | 

prayāge yā gatiḥ proktā sā gatistasya nārada ! | 

śyāmāpradīpe dvitīyaparicchede | 

yajñapārśve mā tilakī karmma kurvīteti | 

tasmānmṛdā jalenāpi karttavyaṃ tilakaṃ budhaiḥ | 

anyathā niṣphalaṃ jñeyaṃ vījoptirūṣare yathā | 

tilakākaraṇanindāpi tatraiva matsyasūkte || 

akṛtvā tilakaṃ bhāle caṇḍālaṃ yastu paśyati | 

punaḥ snānantu karttavyaṃ viṇmūtrotsāraṇe tathā | 

jale ca ghaṭe ca chāyāyāṃ saṃvīkṣya tilakaṃ caret | 

darpaṇe vīkṣayedyatnādamāvāsyāṃ vivarjayet || 

ityamāvāsyāyāṃ tilakaniṣedhaḥ | 

tathā | 

tilake nijadevāstraṃ vilikheddakṣiṇāditaḥ | 

bījaṃ bāhūdarotsaṅge bhāle vā praṇavādikam | 

utsaṅge kroḍe || 

rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtabrahmāṇḍapurāṇe || 

ūrddhvapuṇḍramṛjuṃ saumyaṃ lalāṭe yasya dṛśyate | 

sa caṇḍālo'pi śuddhātmā pūjya eva na saṃśayaḥ | 

ūrddhapuṇḍraṃ mṛdā saumyeti padmapurāṇe prathamacaraṇe pāṭhaḥ | 

aśuciścāmyanācāro manasā pāpamācaret | 

śucireva bhavennityamūrddhvapuṇḍrānvito naraḥ | 

matpriyārthaṃ śubhārthaṃ vā rakṣārthaṃ caturānana ! | 

madbhakto dhārayennityamūrddhvapuṇḍramatandritaḥ | 

matpūjāhomakāle ca sāyaṃ prātaḥ samāhitaḥ | 

madbhakto dhārayennityamūrddhvapuṇḍraṃ bhavāpaham | 

p. 339) iti bhagavadbhaktivilāsadhṛtapadmapurāṇe adhikam | 

taddhṛtaskandapurāṇe kārttikaprasaṅge || 

ūrddhvapuṇḍro mṛdā śubhro lalāṭe yasya dṛśyate | 

caṇḍālo'pi viśuddhātmā yāti brahma sanātanam | 

ūrdhva puṇḍre sthitā lakṣmīḥ ūrddhvapuṇḍre sthitaṃ yaśaḥ | 

ūrddhvapuṇḍre sthitā muktirūrddhvapuṇḍre sthito hariḥ | 

uttarakhaṇḍe śivapārvatīsaṃvāde || 

ūrddhvapuṇḍrasya madhye tu viśāle sumanohare | 

lakṣmyā sārddhaṃ samāsīno devadevo janārdanaḥ | 

tasmād yasya śarīre tu ūrddhvapuṇḍraṃ dhṛtaṃ bhavet | 

tasya deha bhagavato vimalaṃ mandiraṃ smṛtam | 

ūrddhvapuṇḍradhṛto vipraḥ sarvalokeṣu pūjitaḥ | 

vimānavaramāruhya yāti viṣṇoḥ paraṃ padam | 

ūrddhvapuṇḍradharaṃ vipraṃ dṛṣṭvā pāpaiḥ pramucyate | 

nāmasmṛtyā tathā bhaktyā sarvadānaphalaṃ labhet | 

ūrddhvapuṇḍradharaṃ vipraṃ yaḥ śrāddhe bhojayiṣyati | 

ākalpakoṭipitarastasya tṛptā na saṃśayaḥ | 

ūrddhvapuṇḍradharo yastu kuryyāt śrāddhaṃ śubhānane ! | 

kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi vaikuṇṭhe vāsamāpnuyāt | 

yajñadānatapaścaryyājapahomādikañcaret | 

ūrddhvapuṇḍradharaḥ kuryyāttasya puṇyamanantakam || 

brahmāṇḍapurāṇe viṣṇuvākyam || 

ūrddhvapuṇḍradharo martyo mriyate yatra kutracit | 

śvapāko'pi vimānastho mama loke mahīyate | 

ūrddhvapuṇḍradharo martyo gṛhe yasyānnamaśnute | 

tadā viṃśatkulaṃ tasya narakāduddharāmyaham || 

ityūrddhva puṇḍramāhātmyam || 

padmapurāṇe nāradoktau || 

yajño dānaṃ tapo homaḥ svādhyāyaḥ pitṛtarpaṇam | 

vyarthaṃ bhavati tat sarvamūrddhvapuṇḍraṃ vinā kṛtam || 

uttarakhaṇḍe || 

ūrddhvapuṇḍravihīnastu kiñcit karma karoti yaḥ | 

iṣṭāpūrttādikaṃ sarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ syānna cānyathā | 

ūrddhvapuṇḍravihīnastu sandhyākarmādikañcaret | 

tatsarvaṃ rākṣasaṃ jñeyaṃ narakañcādhigacchati | 

yaccharīraṃ manuṣyāṇāmūrddhvapuṇḍra vinā kṛtam | 

draṣṭavyaṃ naiva tattāvaccāṇḍālasadṛśaṃ bhavet || 

p. 340) skānde || 

yasyorddhvapuṇḍraṃ dṛśyeta lalāṭe na narasya hi | 

taddarśanaṃ na karttavyaṃ dṛṣṭvā sūryyaṃ nirīkṣayet || 

ityūrddhvapuṇḍrākaraṇe doṣaḥ || 

uttarakhaṇḍe || 

ūrddhvapuṇḍraṃ dharedvipro mṛdā śubhreṇa vaidikaḥ | 

na tiryyagdhārayedvidvānāpadyapi kadācana | 

bhagavadbhaktivilāsadhṛtam || 

ūrddhvapuṇḍre tripuṇḍraṃ yaḥ kurute sa narādhamaḥ | 

bhuṅktvā viṣṇugṛhaṃ puṇyaṃ sa yāti narakaṃ dhruvam || 

skandapurāṇe || 

tiryyak puṇḍraṃ na kurvīta samprāpte maraṇe'pi ca | 

naivānyanāma ca brūyāt pumānnārāyaṇādṛte || 

ityūrddhvapuṇḍre tripuṇḍrakaraṇaniṣedhaḥ || 

śāktānandataraṅgiṇīdhṛtam || 

ūrddhvapuṇḍraṃ tripuṇḍraṃ vā kṛtvā sandhyāṃ samācaret | 

tadeva vyavasthāpitam skandapurāṇe || 

vaiṣṇavānāṃ brāhmaṇānāmūrddhvapuṇḍraṃ vidhīyate | 

anyeṣāntu tripuṇḍraṃ syāditi brahmavido viduḥ || 

padmapurāṇe || 

tripuṇḍraṃ yasya viprasya ūrddhvapuṇḍre na dṛśyate | 

taṃ dṛṣṭvāpyathavā spṛṣṭvā sacelaḥ snānamācaret || 

ūrddhvapuṇḍre na kurvīta vaiṣṇavānāṃ tripuṇḍrakam | 

kṛtatripuṇḍramartyasya kriyā na prītaye hareḥ || 

athorddhvapuṇḍranirmāṇavidhiḥ brahmāṇḍapurāṇe || 

vīkṣya deśe jala vāpi yo vidadhyāt prayatnantaḥ |

ūrddhvapuṇḍraṃ mahābhāga ! sa yāti paramāṃ gatim | 

daśāṅgulapramāṇantu uttamottamamucyate | 

navāṅgulaṃ madhyamaṃ syādaṣṭāṅgulamataḥ param | 

etairaṅgulibhedaistu kārayenna nakhaiḥ spṛśet || 

urddhvapuṇḍralakṣaṇantu matsyasūkte || 

nāsikākeśaparyyantamūrddhvapuṇḍraṃ vidhīyate | 

madhye chidrantu karttavyaṃ tacchidraṃ harimandiram || 

padmapurāṇe uttarakhaṇḍe || 

ekāntena mahābhāgāḥ sarvabhūtahite ratāḥ | 

sāntarālaṃ prakurvanti puṇḍraṃ haripadākṛtim | 

śyāmaṃ śāntikaraṃ proktaṃ raktaṃ vaśyakaraṃ tathā | 

śrīkaraṃ pītamityāhuḥ śvetaṃ mokṣapradaṃ śubham || 

iti karmaviśeṣe varṇaviśeṣatripuṇḍram | 

varttulaṃ tiryyagacchidraṃ hrasvaṃ dīrghataraṃ tanum || 

p. 341) vabhrūṃ virūpaṃ bahvagraṃ bhinnamūlaṃ padacyutam | 

aśubhraṃ rūkṣamāsaktaṃ tathā nāṅgulikalpitam | 

vigandhamapasavyañca puṇḍramāhuranarthakam || 

iti ninditapuṇḍram || 

nāṅgulikalpitamiti vihitakāṣṭhikābhinnopalakṣaṇam || 

anyathā matsyasūkte daśamapaṭaloktavacanamanarthakaṃ syāt | 

tadvacanantu || 

kuśenaiva suvarṇena na kāṣṭhenāyasena ca | 

na śaṅkhena na tāmreṇa na kāṃsyena kadācana || 

tulasyā vilvakāṣṭhena rajatena samullikhet || 

ūrddhvapuṇḍrākārastu uttarakhaṇḍe || 

aśvatthapatrasaṅkāśo veṇupatrākṛtistathā | 

padmakuṭmalasaṅkāśo mohanaṃ tritayaṃ smṛtam | 

ārabhya nāsikāmūlaṃ lalāṭāntaṃ likhenmudā | 

lalāṭāntamiti nyūnatvavyavacchedakamanyathā daśāṅgulasyottamatvakīrttanaṃ vyarthaṃ 
syāt | 

daśāṅgulaparimāṇantu nāsikātṛtīyabhāgāvadhi brahmarandhraparyyantameva | 

mṛdeti vihitadravyopalakṣaṇam | 

mṛdamiti pāṭhe vyatyayo bahulamiti sūtrādvibhaktivyatyayena sa evārthaḥ || 

nāsikāyāstrayo bhāgā nāsāmūlaṃ pracakṣate | 

samārabhya bhruvormadhyamantarālaṃ prakalpayet || 

trayo bhāgā iti nirdeśabalāt pūraṇapratyayārthāvagatestṛtīyo bhāgo labhyate | 

sa ca bhāgo yatra mastakāvasthito nāsikā nirgatā tatraiva || 

tasmācchidrānvitaṃ puṇḍraṃ daṇḍākāraṃ suśobhanam | 

viprāṇāṃ satataṃ kāryyaṃ strīṇāñca śubhadarśane ! | 

nāsikākeśaparyyantaṃ ūrddhvapuṇḍraṃ suśobhanam || 

madhye chidrasamāyuktaṃ tadvidyāddharimandiram || 

iti harimandiralakṣaṇam || 

vāmabhāge sthito brahmā dakṣabhāge sadāśivaḥ | 

madhye viṣṇuṃ vijānīyāt tasmānmadhyaṃ na lepayet || 

yajurvedīyasya hiraṇyakeśīyaśākhāyām || 

hareḥ padākrāntim ātmano nidhāya yanmadhye chidramūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ yo dhārayati sa 
parasya priyo bhavati sa puṇyavān bhavati sa muktibhāgbhavatīti | 

tilakaracanāyāmaṅguliniyamamāha bhagavadbhaktivilāsadhṛtā smṛtiḥ || 

anāmikā kāmadoktā madhyamāyuṣkarī bhavet | 

p. 342) aṅguṣṭhaḥ puṣṭidaḥ proktastarjanī mokṣasādhanī || 

ūrdhvapuṇḍrasya mṛttikā tu padmapurāṇe || 

parvatāgre nadītīre vilvamūle jalāśaye | 

sindhutīre ca valmīke harikṣetre viśeṣataḥ || 

viṣṇoḥ snānodakaṃ yatra pravāhayati nityaśaḥ | 

puṇḍrāṇāṃ dhāraṇārthāya gṛhṇīyāttasya mṛttikām | 

śrīraṅge veṅkaṭādrau ca śrīkurmmadvārake śubhe | 

prayāge nārasiṃhādrau vārāhe tulasīvane | 

gṛhītvā mṛttikāṃ bhaktyā viṣṇupādajalaiḥ saha | 

dhṛtvā puṇḍrāṇi cāṅgeṣu viṣṇusāyujyamāpnuyāt || 

parvatāgre ityādau ṣaṣṭhīsaptabhyorarthaṃ prati bhedābhāva iti nyāyāt 
parvatāgrasyetyādi labhyate | 

gṛhītvā mṛttikāmiti pareṇānvayaḥ | 

vyatyayo bahulamiti ṣaṣṭhyarthe saptamī vā | 

nadītīra iti gaṅgāṃtīrasyeti jñeyam | 

prāguktamatsyasūktavacanena strīsarito mṛttikāgrahaṇaniṣedhāditi | 

śrīraṅgaḥ kāverīmadhyasthaḥ kṣetraviśeṣo dakṣiṇadeśeṣu prasiddhaḥ | 

veṅkaṭādriḥ śeṣācalanāmnā tailaṅgadeśe prasiddhaḥ | 

śrīkūrmmaḥ kaliṅgadeśe prasiddhaḥ kṣetraviśeṣaḥ || 

gopīcandanamāhātmyantu matsyasūkte | 

durlabhaṃ candanaṃ loke gopīnāṃ viṣṇunirmitam | 

yat kṛtvā tilakaṃ sadyo brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati || 

sthitasyetyadhyāhārādekakarttṛkatā | 

padmapurāṇe nāradavākye'pi || 

brahmaghno vātha goghno vā haitukaḥ sarvapāpakṛt | 

gopīcandanasamparkāt pūto bhavati tatkṣaṇāt | 

gopīcandanakhaṇḍantu yo dadātīha vaiṣṇave | 

kulamekottaraṃ tena svaṃ bhavettāritaṃ śatam || 

iti vaiṣṇave gopīcandanadānaphalam || 

skandapurāṇe dhruvavākyam || 

śaṅkhacakrāṅkitatanuḥ śirasā mañjarīdharaḥ | 

gopīcandanaliptāṅgo dṛṣṭaścettadaghaṃ kutaḥ | 

gopīmṛttulasī śaṅkhaḥ śālagrāmaḥ sacakrakaḥ | 

gṛhe'pi yasya pañcaite tasya pāpabhayaṃ kutaḥ || 

sacakrakaṃ saśrīcakrakamityarthaḥ | 

kāśīkhaṇḍe || 

śrīkhaṇḍe kva sa āmodaḥ svarṇe varṇaḥ kva tādṛśaḥ | 

p. 343) tatpavitraṃ kva vai tīrthe śrīgopīcandane yathā || 

garuḍapurāṇe śrīnāradavākyam | 

yo mṛttikāṃ dvāravatīsamudbhavāṃ kare samādāya lalāṭapaṭṭe | 

karoti nityantvatha corddhvapuṇḍra kriyāphalaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ sadā bhavet | 

kriyāvihīnaṃ yadi mantrahīnaṃ śraddhāvihīnaṃ yadi kālavarjitam | 

kṛtvā lalāṭe yadi gopicandanaṃ prāpnoti tatkarmmaphalaṃ sadākṣayam | 

gopīcandanasambhavaṃ suruciraṃ puṇḍraṃ lalāṭe dvijo nityaṃ dhārayate yadi dvijapate ! 
rātrau divā sarvadā | 

yat puṇyaṃ kurujāṅgale ravigṛhe māghyāṃ prayāge tathā tatprāpnoti khagendra ! 
viṣṇusadane santiṣṭhate devavat || 

yasmin gṛhe tiṣṭhati gopicandanaṃ bhaktyā lalāṭe manujo bibharti | 

tasmin gṛhe tiṣṭhati sarvadā hariḥ śraddhānvitaḥ kaṃsanihā vihaṅgama ! || 

yo dhārayet kṛṣṇapurīsamudbhavāṃ sadā pavitrāṃ kalikalmaṣāpahām | 

nityaṃ lalāṭe yadi mantrasaṃyutāṃ yamaṃ na paśyed yadi pāpasaṃvṛtaḥ | 

yasyāntakāle khaga ! gopicandanaṃ bāhvorlalāṭe hṛdi mastake ca | 

prayāti lokaṃ kamalālayāprabhorgobālaghātī yadi brahmahā bhavet || 

grahā na pīḍanti na rakṣasāṃ gaṇo yakṣāḥ piśācoragabhūtadānavāḥ | 

lalāṭapaṭṭe khaga ! gopicandanam santiṣṭhate yasya hareḥ prasādataḥ || 

padmapurāṇe || 

ambarīṣa ! mahāghasya kṣayārthe kuru vīkṣaṇam | 

lalāṭe yaiḥ kṛtaṃ nityaṃ gopīcandanapuṇḍrakam || 

kāśīkhaṇḍe yamoktiḥ | 

dūtāḥ ! śṛṇuta yadbhālaṃ gopīcandanalāñchitam | 

jvaladindhanavat so'pi dūre tyājyaḥ prayatnataḥ || 

iti gopīcandanamāhātmyam ||

bhagavadbhaktivilāsadhṛtakāśīkhaṇḍe || 

atha tasyopari śrīmattulasīmūlamṛtsnayā | 

tathaiva vaiṣṇavaiḥ kāryyamūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ manoramam | 

skandapurāṇe tulasīmūlamṛttikāmupakramya || 

tanmṛdaṃ gṛhya yaiḥ puṇḍraṃ lalāṭe dhāritaṃ naraiḥ | 

prayāṇakaṃ kṛtaṃ taistu mokṣāya gamanaṃ prati ||

p. 344) tulasīmṛttikāpuṇḍraṃ lalāṭe | 

yasya dṛśyate | 

dehaṃ na spṛśate pāpaṃ kriyamāṇantu nārada ! || 

garuḍapurāṇe || 

tulasīmṛttikāpuṇḍraṃ yaḥ karoti dine dine | 

tasyāvalokanāt pāpāṃ yāti varṣakṛtaṃ nṛṇām || 

tulasīmūlamṛttikāmāhātmyam gāruḍe | 

tasyopariṣṭādbhavagannirmālyamanulepanam | 

tathaiva dhāryyamevaṃ hi trividhaṃ tilakaṃ smṛtamiti || 

pūrvoktyā atha tasyopari gopīcandanatilakopari | 

tatra tu tasyopariṣṭāttulasīmūlamṛttikā tilakopariṣṭādityarthaḥ || 

tena prathamaṃ gopīcandanatilakaṃ kṛtvā tadupari tulasīmūlamṛttikātilakaṃ karaṇīyam || 

tadupari ca viṣṇunirmālyānulepane tilakaṃ kāryyamiti trividhaṃ tilakam || 

atha dvādaśatilakavidhiḥ || 

brahmapurāṇe || 

tato dvādaśabhiḥ kuryyānnāmabhiḥ keśavādibhiḥ | 

dvādaśāṅgeṣu vidhivadūrdhvapuṇḍrāṇi vaiṣṇavaḥ || 

padmapurāṇe uttarakhaṇḍe || 

lalāṭe keśavaṃ dhyāyennārāyaṇamathodare | 

vakṣaḥsthale mādhavantu govindaṃ kaṇṭhakūpake | 

viṣṇuñca dakṣiṇe kukṣau bāhau ca madhusūdanam | 

trivikramaṃ skandhadeśe vāmanaṃ vāmapārśvake | 

śrīdharaṃ vāmabāhau tu hṛsīkeśantu kandhare | 

pṛṣṭhe tu padmanābhañca kaṭyāṃ dāmodaraṃ nyaset | 

tatprakṣālanatoyantu vāsudeveti mūrddhani | 

yoginītantre || 

pidhāya tilakaṃ śreṣṭhameṣāmekatamena vā | 

catuḥsamañca trisamaṃ dvisamañca sureśvari ! | 

athālipya tato dehamayane tu vivarjayet | 

catuḥsamamityādipūrvoktamalayajagopīcandanavilvatulasīkāṣṭha-padmakṛtamālagorocanānām | 

śīte rātrau punaḥ snāne nānulipya sugandhibhiḥ | 

lalāṭe tu viśeṣeṇa varāṅge na kadācana || 

matsyasūkte || 

na rātrau tilakaṃ kuryyād bhaktvā naiva ca naiva ca | 

mahāgurunipāte ca tilakaṃ chedayennaraḥ | 

bahvṛcapariśiṣṭam | 

tatrādāvanulepena bhagavaccaraṇābjayoḥ | 

nirmmālyena prasādena sarvāṇyaṅgāni mārjayet || 

brahmapurāṇe || 

śālagrāmaśilālagnaṃ candanaṃ dhārayet sadā | 

p. 345) sarvāṅgeṣu mahāśuddhisiddhaye kamalāsanam || 

ityūrdhva puṇḍraprakaraṇam |
	
atha tripuṇḍradhāraṇavidhiḥ || 

rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtam || 

bhasmāgnihotrasambhūtamānayecchodhinaṃ budhaḥ | 

yadvā dharāmasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ satyenānīya gomayam || 

satyena praṇavena || 

vāmena pātre saṃśodhya aghoreṇa vinirdahet || 

tatpuruṣeṇa samuddhṛtya īśānena viśodhayet | 

itthantu saṃskṛtaṃ bhasma agnirityādimantrataḥ | 

vimṛjyāṅgāni saṃspṛśya punarādāya mantrataḥ | 

tasmādbrahmeti yajuṣā mantrayedrudrasaṃkhyayā | 

praṇavādyaiścaturthī hṛdantaistvaghora īśakaiḥ | 

pañcavarṇākṣarādyaiśca bhālāṃśodarahṛtsu ca | 

tripuṇḍradhāraṇaṃ kuryyānmūrddhniṃ pañcākṣareṇa ca | 

tripuṇḍraṃ dhārayenmantrī sākṣācchiva ivāparaḥ || 

vāyavīsaṃhitāyām bhasmagrahaṇamanyadapyuktam || 

yathā | 

śivāgnerbhasma saṃgrāhyamagnihotrodbhavantu vā | 

vaivāhyāgnyudbhavaṃ vāpi pakvaṃ śuci sugandhi ca | 

kapilāyāḥ śakṛcchuddhaṃ gṛhītaṃ gagane patat | 

na klinnaṃ nāpi kaṭhinaṃ na durgandhi na coṣitam | 

uṣitaṃ paryyuṣitamityarthaḥ || 

uparyyadhaḥ parityajya gṛhṇīyāt patitaṃ yadi | 

piṇḍīkṛtya śivāgnau tu prakṣipenmūlamanvataḥ | 

apakvamatipakvañca santyajya bhasitaṃ sitam | 

ādāya yasmācchuddhaṃ tad yathā bhasma prajāyata iti kālāgnirudropaniṣadā 
vṛṣagomayasyāpyevamprakāraḥ pradarśitaḥ || 

vāmeneti || 

oṃ vāmadevāya namo jyeṣṭhāya namo rudrāya namaḥ | 

kālāya namaḥ kalavikaraṇāya namo balavikaraṇāya namo balapramathanāya namaḥ 
sarvabhūtadamanāya namo manonmanāya namaḥ || 

aghoreṇeti mantrastu oṃ aghorebhyo'tha ghorebhyo ghoraghoratarebhyaḥ sarvataḥ 
sarvasarvebhyo namaste rudrarūpebhyaḥ || 

tatpuruṣeṇeti mantrastu oṃ tatpuruṣāya vidmahe mahādevāya dhīmahi tanno rudraḥ 
pracodayāt || 

īśāneneti mantrastu | 

p. 346) oṃ īśānaḥ sarvavidyānāmīśvaraḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ 
brahmādhipatirbrahmaṇo'dhipatirbrahmā śivo me'stu sadāśivaum | 

agnirityādimantrastu | 

agniriti bhasmavāyuriti bhasmajalamiti bhasmasthalamiti bhasmavyometi bhasmasarvaharā idam | 

bhasmamana etāni sakṣūṃṣi tasmādvratametat pāśupataṃ yat |

bhasmanāṅgāni saṃspṛśet | 

tasmādbrahma tadetat pāśupataṃ paśupataṃ paśupāśavimokṣāya | 

yajuṣā yajurmantreṇa pañcākṣareṇa namaḥ śivāyeti mantreṇa vā 
kulamūlāvatārakalpasūtratṛtīyakāṇḍaṭīkādhṛtā śrutiḥ || 

śuddhadravyaṃ tadāgneyabhasmatripuṇḍrakaṃ 
trirekhātmakamarddhacandrarūpātmakamarddhacandrarūpam || 

atha sanatkumārapramāṇasya tridhā lalāṭādācakṣuḥ paścād bhrūvormadhyataḥ | 

yasyeha prathamā rekhā sā āhavanīyo'rka ṛgvedaḥ | 

prātaḥ savanaṃ maheśvaraḥ | 

yasyeha dvitīyā rekhā sāvasathya ukāro yajurvedaḥ sadāśivaḥ | 

yasyeha tṛtīyā rekhā sā gārhapatyaḥ paramātmajñānaśaktirmakārādyairiti || 

atha kālāgnirudropaniṣat || 

saṃbartto'gnir-ṛṣirvirāḍviśvarūpī rudro devatā anuṣṭupchandaḥ śrīviśvarūpaprītyarthe 
viniyogaḥ | 

atha kālāgnirudraṃ bhagavantaṃ sanatkumāraḥ papraccha adhīhi bhostripuṇḍravidhiṃ 
satattvaṃ kiṃ dravyaṃ kiñca sthānaṃ kati pramāṇaṃ kā rekhāḥ ke mantrāḥ kiṃ 
daivataṃ kaḥ karttā kiṃ phalamiti taṃ hovāca bhagavān kālāgnirudraḥ | 

satyamuktaṃ yaddravyaṃ yadāgneyaṃ yadbhasma sadyojātādi pañcarudrabrahmamantraiḥ 
parigṛhya agniriti bhasma anenābhimantrya mānastoka iti saṃkṣubhya tryāyuṣamityādinā 
śirolalāṭaskandhakaṇṭhahṛdbāhudvayeṣu tribhirāyuṣaistryambakaistiryak tisro rekhāḥ 
prakurvīta etadeva tacchāmbhavaṃ sarvavedeṣu vedavidbhiruktam | 

tat samācaran mumukṣurna punarbhavāya | 

bhedamālalāṭādācakṣuṣorābhruvormadhyataḥ | 

karṇādākarṇaparyyantaṃ tiryyak tisro rekhāḥ prakurvīta | 

yā syāt prathamā rekhā sā gārhapatyāgniścākārā bhūrloka ātmakriyāśaktiśca 
ṛgvedaḥ | 

p. 347) prathamaṃ savanaṃ maheśvaraḥ yā syād dvitīyā rekhā sā 
dakṣiṇāgnirukāraḥ | 

satyamantarīkṣamantarātmā cecchāśaktiryajurvedaḥ | 

madhyandinaṃ dvitīyasavanaṃ sadāśivadeveteti | 

yā syāt tṛtīyā rekhā sā āhavanīyo'gnirmakārastamo dyauḥ paramātmā jñānaśaktiḥ 
sāmavedastṛtīyasavanaṃ śivadeveti | 

tripuṇḍrakaṃ bhasmanā karoti yo vidvān brahmacārī gṛhī vānaprastho yatirvā sa 
samastamahāpātakopapātakebhyaḥ pūto bhavati sarvān vedān paṭhitā bhavati 
sarvatīrtheṣu snāto bhavati sa santataṃ sarvamantrajāpī bhavati | 

sakalabhogabhugdehaṃ tyaktvā śivasāyujyameti na punarāvarttate | 

sa vaiṣṇavaḥ śivaḥ so'pi sa ca brahmā prajāpatiḥ | 

sa muktaḥ sarvapāpebhyo na punarbhavamiṣyate | 

ityasti bhagavān kālāgnirudraḥ punarāha | 

agniriti bhasmajalamiti bhasmavāyumiti bhasmasthalamiti bhasmasarvaṃ viśvaṃ tadāgneyaṃ 
yājñyaṃ vṛṣapurīṣabhasma tena tripuṇḍrakaṃ tena tadvidvān kiñcidyājñikaṃ karmma 
ityāha bhagavān bhavaḥ || 

iti kālāgnirudropaniṣat samāptā ||
	
vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāṃ trayodaśasu sthāneṣu tripuṇḍradhāraṇamuktam || 

yathā rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtam || 

lalāṭe brahma vijñeyaṃ hṛdaye havyavāhanaḥ | 

nābhau skandhe gale pūṣā rudro dakṣiṇabāhuke | 

ādityo bāhumadhye ca śaśī ca maṇibandhake | 

vāmadevo vāmabāhau bāhumadhye prabhañjanaḥ | 

maṇibandhe ca vasabaḥ pṛṣṭhadeśe haraḥ smṛtaḥ | 

śambhuḥ kakudi samproktaḥ paramātmā śivaḥ smṛtaḥ | 

tasmāt kālāgnirudropaniṣadvāyavīyasaṃhitoktasthānānāmicchā-vikalpaḥ || 

puraścaraṇalaharītantre dvitīyapaṭale || 

āgneyamucyate bhasma dagdhagomayasambhavam | 

śodhyaṃ ṣaḍakṣarairmantrairvedamantravivarjitaiḥ | 

śirodeśe lalāṭe ca skandhe ca bhrūpradeśake | 

madhyamānāmikāṅgulyaṅguṣṭhena tilakaṃ tataḥ |

p. 348) tilakastisro rekhā syādrekhāṇāṃ nava devatāḥ | 

pṛthivyagnistathā ātmā kriyāśaktirmaheśvaraḥ | 

devatā ādyarekhāyā bhaktyā te parikīrttitāḥ | 

labhasva tvañca subhage ! dvitīyāyāśca devatāḥ | 

paramātmā śivo devastṛtīyāyāstu devate | 

etānnityaṃ namaskṛtya tripuṇḍraṃ dhārayed yadi | 

maheśvaravratamidaṃ kṛtvā siddhīśvaro bhavet | 

brahmacārī gṛhastho vā vanastho yatireva vā | 

mahāpātakasaṅghātairmucyate sarvapātakāt | 

tathānye kṣatraviṭśūdrastrīhatyādikapātakaiḥ | 

vīrabrāhmaṇahatyābhyāṃ mucyate subhageśvari ! | 

amantreṇāpi yat kuryyāt kṛtvā caiva mahonnatim | 

tripuṇḍrabhālatilako mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ | 

paradravyāpaharaṇaṃ paradārābhimarṣaṇam | 

paranindā parakṣetraharaṇaṃ parapīḍanam | 

asatyavākyaṃ paiśunyaṃ pāruṣyaṃ devavikrayam | 

kūṭasākṣyaṃ vratatyāgaṃ kaitavaṃ nīcasevanam | 

gobhūhiraṇyamahiṣītilakambalavāsasām | 

annadhānyajalādīnāṃ nīcebhyaśca pratigrahaḥ | 

dāsīveśyāsu kṛṣṭāsu vṛṣalīṣu naṭīṣu ca | 

rajaḥsvalāsu kanyāsu vidhavāsu ca saṅgamaḥ | 

māṃsacarmmavasādīnāṃ lavaṇasya ca vikrayaḥ | 

evaṃrūpāṇyasaṃkhyāni pāpāni vividhāni ca | 

sadya eva vinaśyanti tripuṇḍrasya ca dhāraṇāt | 

śivadravyāpaharaṇāttannindāyāśca kutracit | 

nindāyāḥ śivabhaktānāṃ prāyaścittairna śudhyati | 

devadravyāpaharaṇe brahmasvaharaṇena ca | 

kulānyāśu vinaśyanti brāhmaṇātikrameṇa ca | 

rudrākṣā yasya deheṣu lalāṭe ca tripuṇḍrakam | 

yadi syāt sa ca cāṇḍālaḥ sarvavarṇottamottamaḥ | 

yāni tīrthāni loke'smin gaṅgādyāḥ saritaśca yāḥ | 

snāto bhavati sarvatra yallalāṭe tripuṇḍrakam | 

saptakoṭirmahāmantrā upamantrāstathaiva ca | 

śrīviṣṇoḥ koṭimantrāśca koṭimantrāḥ śivasya ca | 

te sarve tena japtāḥ syuryo bibhartti tripuṇḍrakam | 

sahasraṃ pūrvajātānāṃ sahasrañca janiṣyatām | 

p. 349) svavaṃśajānāṃ martyānāmuddharedyastripuṇḍradhṛk | 

aṣṭaiśvaryyaguṇopetaḥ prāpya divyatripuṇḍrataḥ | 

divyaṃ vimānamāruhya divyastrīśatasevitaḥ | 

vidyādharāṇāṃ siddhānāṃ gandharvāṇāṃ mahaujasām | 

indrādilokapālānāṃ lokeṣu ca yathākramam | 

bhuktvā bhogān suvipulān prāktanānāṃ pureṣu ca | 

brahmaṇaḥ padamāsādya tatra kalpāyutāvadhi | 

viṣṇorloke ca ramate ābrahmaṇaḥ śatāyuṣam | 

śivalokaṃ tataḥ prāpya ramate kālamakṣayam | 

śivasāyujyamāpnoti na sa bhūyo vijāyate || 

iti tripuṇḍradhāraṇaphalam ||

śāktānandataraṅgiṇīdhṛtabhaviṣyapurāṇe || 

tripuṇḍreṇa vinā kuryyād yāṃ kāñcidvaidikīṃ kriyām | 

sā niṣphalā bhaveddevi ! brahmaṇāpi kṛtā yadi || 

śyāmāpradīpe tu skandapurāṇīyamiti kṛtvā likhitam || 

kūrmapurāṇe || 

vaiṣṇavo vātha śaivo vā śākto vā saura eva vā | 

tripuṇḍreṇa vinā pūjāṃ kurvāṇo yātyadhogatim || 

śivadharme | 

sitena bhasmanā kuryyāt lalāṭe ca tripuṇḍrakam | 

sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ śivaloke mahīyate || 

bhasmetyupalakṣaṇaṃ dravyāntareṇāpi tripuṇḍraṃ kāryyam || 

sarvastripuṇḍrakaṃ kuryyāt sarvadā yajñabhasmanā | 

tadabhāve candanena mṛdā vā vāriṇāpi vā | 

iti bhaviṣyapurāṇavacanāt | 

yatkiñcit kurute karma vinā viprastripuṇḍrakam | 

vyarthameva bhavet sarvaṃ bandhyāstrīsaṅgamo yathā | 

sacchidraṃ kurute yastripuṇḍraṃ paśumatirdvijaḥ | 

dharmārthakāmamokṣeṣu tasya chidraṃ prajāyate | 

iti sacchidratripuṇḍradhāraṇanindā | 

nanūrddhvapuṇḍre tripuṇḍrakaraṇamuktavacanajātāt niṣiddhaṃ 
pūrvoktakūrmapurāṇavacanena vaiṣṇavādīnāṃ tripuṇḍreṇa vinā pūjākaraṇe 
doṣaśrutiśceti lambodarasya ratikāle priyāmukhacumbanamivaikaṃ sandhitsato'nyat pracyavata 
iti ceti naivam | 

ūrdhvapuṇḍratripuṇḍrayoḥ sacchidrakaraṇaniṣedho vaidhetarapara ityavehi | 

sarvatraiva niṣedhasya vaidhabhinnaparatvād yathā viśikhanindā 
prāyaścittādyanāghrātaparā | 

p. 350) ata-eva śaivaistūrdhva puṇḍradhāraṇānantarameva bhasmanā 
tripuṇḍradhāraṇamapi kāryyaṃ yato dvijānāmūrdhvapuṇḍrasthāvaśyakatvamiti 
rāghavabhaṭṭaḥ || 

atha śaktitilakam || 

śāktānandataraṅgiṇīdhṛtagautamīye || 

tilakaṃ raktagandhena candanenātha vā priye ! | 

devyastraṃ vilikhedbhāle tārābījaṃ tato hṛdi | 

śaktimadhyagatāṃ kuryyādityetacchaktitilakam || 

tilake nijadevāstramiti prāguktenaikavākyatayā devyastraṃ svīyeṣṭadevatāvījamiti || 

annadākalpe pañcamapaṭale || 

prakṣālya pāṇipādau ca raktavastrayugaṃ tataḥ | 

paridhāyārddhacandrābhaṃ sabinduṃ tilakaṃ caret | 

candanena suraktena kuṅkumenātha vā punaḥ | 

sindūrabindusaṃyuktaṃ rekhātrayavirājitam | 

devīpādāmbujarajo dhyātvā mūlena sādhakaḥ | 

pūjādhikārī durgāyā yathāsau candraśekharaḥ || 

kṛṣṇārccnacandrikādhṛtamālinīvijaye'pi || 

bhāle candrāddharekhā tritayaviracitaṃ vidyudābhāvilāsaṃ 
tasyādhaścāntarāle'ruṇakiraṇanibhaṃ bhrūyugasyātha bindum | 

hṛṇmadhye padmarūpaṃ śaśadharaviśadaṃ doryuge vīraṇābhaṃ sthāneṣvanyeṣu 
bindūn racayati tilakaṃ so hi viśveśarūpaḥ || 

bhrūyugasyāntarāle madhye raktavarṇaṃ binduṃ racayatītyanvayaḥ | 

hṛṇmadhye śvetapadmarūpamityarthaḥ | 

doryuge bāhudvaye | 

vīraṇābhaṃ vīraṇapatrākāram | 

anyeṣu sthāneṣu purokteṣu iti etacchabdasya prayogastacchabdārthamabhidhatte | 

uktañca sarvanāmasūtraṭīkāyāṃ tyattadāvekārthau yadi nirdiṣṭārthau | 

itare trayaḥ sābhinayapratyakṣe sābhinayo hastabhaṅgimādiriti kulacandraḥ | 

anyathā vimargalope satītyanena cchandobhaṅgaḥ syāt | 

prakrāntavācinastasya na yacchabdāpekṣā |

uktañca kāvyaprakāśe || 

evaṃ prakrāntaprasiddhānubhūtārthaviṣayakastacchabdo'pi yacchabdasyopādanaṃ 
nāpekṣate | 

ṛjavastu tantre'paśabdavicāraniṣedhāt so hīti paṭhanti | 

p. 351) jale yadi sandhyādi kriyate tadā yāgamaṇḍapaṃ gatvā upaviśya tilakaṃ 
kāryyam || 

annadākalpe tathoktatvāt ||

iti śrīprāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ tṛtīye arthakāṇḍe snānāditilakāntapraśākhākathanarūpastṛtīyaḥ 
paricchedaḥ ||



atha sandhyā || 

vaidikasandhyānantaraṃ tāntrikī sandhyā kāryyā || 

tathāca puraścaraṇarasollāse prathamapaṭale | 

prātaḥsnānaṃ samāsādya sandhyāṃ paramadurlabhām | 

upāsya cañcalāpāṅgi ! gāyatrīṃ prajapettataḥ | 

tatastu tāntrikīṃ sandhyāṃ gāyitrīṃ tāntrikīṃ tathā | 

sūryyārghyañca tato dattvā pūjārthaṃ gṛhamāviśet || 

vṛhannīlatantre prathamapaṭale'pi || 

ādau ca vaidikīṃ sandhyāṃ kṛtvā cāgamasammatām | 

sandhyāṃ kṛtvā tato vīraḥ kulakoṭīḥ samuddharet | 

tathā | 

etatsandhyātrayeṇaiva divārātrau ca vandaneti || 

gandharvatantre  ṣaṣṭhapaṭale || 

aparāṃ kathayāmyatra sarvapāpavināśinīm | 

upaviśya śucau deśe tritattvena tridhācamet | 

prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā tridhāvījaistridhāvidham | 

vāmahaste jalaṃ gṛhya galitodakabindubhiḥ | 

saptadhā prokṣaṇaṃ kuryyānmūrddhni mantramanusmaran | 

avaśiṣṭodakaṃ dakṣahaste saṃgṛhya sādhakaḥ | 

nāsāmāśliṣya toyena tatastenāghamarṣaṇam | 

iḍayākṛṣya dehāntaḥ kṣālitaiḥ pāpasañcayaiḥ | 

kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ tadodakaṃ vāmanāḍyā virecitam | 

dakṣahaste tu tanmantrī pāparūpaṃ vicintya ca | 

purato vajrapāṣāṇe nikṣipedastramantrataḥ | 

ityaghamarṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā punarācamanaṃ tathā | 

abhiṣiñcettato mūrdhni vidhinā sādhakottamaḥ | 

oṃ āpo devī raso'mṛtaṃ pūtaṃ brahma punīmahe | 

jalāñjalitrayaṃ bhūmau mārttaṇḍabhairavāya ca | 

ūrdhvabāhustato mantrī mārttaṇḍaṃ samupāśrayet | 

oṃ udutyaṃ jātavedasaṃ devaṃ vahanti ketavaḥ dṛśe viśvāya sūryyam | 

mārttaṇḍaśeṣe deveśi ! bhairavaṃ padamīrayet | 

etādṛk sandhyā tu gauḍa deśe na pracaradrūpā | 

p. 352) gauḍadeśīyā tu toḍalatantre tṛtīyapaṭale || 

ātmavidyāśivaistattvairācāmet pāthasā tathā | 

vahni jāyānvitā mantrā vijñeyāḥ praṇavādikāḥ | 

gaṅge cetyādinā devi ! tīrthāvāhanamācaret | 

mūlena manunā bhūmau trivāraṃ nikṣitet sudhīḥ | 

tajjalena saptavāramātmābhiṣekamācaret | 

aṅganyāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā vāmahaste sureśvari ! | 

gaganaṃ vāyuvījañca varuṇaṃ bhūmivījakam | 

vahnibījaṃ binduyutaṃ trivāraṃ japamācaret | 

anena manunā devi ! tajjalañcābhimantritam | 

mūlamantraṃ samuccāryya saptadhā tattvamudrayā | 

ātmābhiṣekamātreṇa mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ |

śeṣaṃ jalaṃ maheśāni ! dakṣahaste samānayet | 

iḍayā pūrayettoyaṃ kṣālayeddehamadhyagam | 

tataḥ piṅgalayā devi ! tattoyantu virecayet | 

kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ tadudakaṃ pāparūpaṃ vicintayet | 

purato vajrapāṣāṇe phaṭkāreṇa vinikṣipet | 

hastaṃ prakṣālya cācamya sūryyāyārghyaṃ nivedayet | 

devatārghyaṃ tataḥ paścāt gāyatrīṃ paramākṣarīm | 

prapaṭhettu tato dhīmān trivāraṃ jalamutkṣipet | 

tato japenmahāmantraṃ gāyatrīṃparamākṣarīm | 

aṅganyāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā ācamya parameśvari ! | 

iṣṭadevīṃ maheśāni ! dhyātvā tu ravimaṇḍale | 

prāṇāyāmādikaṃ kṛtvā cāṣṭottaraśataṃ japet || 

kalpasūtraṭīkātṛtīyakāṇḍadhṛtalakṣmīkulārṇave || 

sandhyayā tu vihīno yo na dīkṣāphalamāpnuyāt | 

mantrasandhyāpūrvikā sandhyā kāryyā taduktaṃ navaratneśvare || 

vaidikī tāntrikī sandhyā yathānukramayogata iti || 

mahānirvāṇatantre pañcamollāse'pi || 

sandhyāṃ samācarenmantrī tāntrikīṃ śṛṇu kathyate | 

ācamya pūrvavattoye tīrthānyavāhayecchive ! | 

gaṅge cetyādimantreṇa mudrayāṅkuśasaṃjñayā | 

āvāhya tīrthaṃ salile mūlaṃ dvādaśadhā japet | 

tatastattoyato bindūn tridhā bhūmau vinikṣipet | 

aṅguṣṭhānāmikāyogānmūloccāraṇapūrvakam | 

saptavāraṃ svamūrddhānamabhiṣicya tato jalam | 

p. 353) vāmahaste samādāya chādayeddakṣapāṇinā | 

īśānavāyuvaruṇavahnīndravījapañcakam | 

prajapya vedadhā toyaṃ dakṣahaste samānayet | 

vīkṣya tejomayaṃ dhyātvā ceḍayākṛṣya sādhakaḥ | 

dehāntaḥ kalaṣaṃ tena recayet piṅgalākhyayā | 

niṣkṛṣya purato vajraśilāyāṃ vajramuccaran | 

trivāraṃ tāḍayenmantrī hastau prakṣālayettataḥ | 

ācamyoktena mantreṇa sūryyāyārghyaṃ nivedayet | 

tāramāyā haṃsa iti ghṛṇisūryyastataḥ param | 

idamarghyaṃ tubhyamuktvā dadyāt svāhetyudīrayan | 

tato dhyāyenmahādevīṃ gāyatrīṃ paradevatām | 

prātarmadhyāhnasāyāhne trirūpaṃ guṇabhedataḥ | 

prātarvahniraktavarṇāṃ dvibhujāñca kumārikām | 

kamaṇḍaluṃ tīrthapūrṇamakṣamālāñca bibhratīm | 

kṛṣṇājināmbaradharāṃ haṃsārūḍhāṃ śucismitām || 1 || 

śyāmavarṇāṃ vaiṣṇavīñca caturbhujāṃ maheśvari ! | 

śaṅkhacakragadāpadmadhāriṇīṃ garuḍāsanām | 

pīnatuṅgakucadvandvāṃ vanamālāvibhūṣitām | 

yuvatīṃ satataṃ dhyāyenmadhye mārttaṇḍamaṇḍale || 2 || 

sāyāhne varadāṃ devīṃ gāyatrīṃ saṃsmaredyatiḥ | 

śuklāṃśuklāmbaradharāṃ vṛṣāsanakṛtāśrayām | 

trinetrāṃ varadāṃ pāśaṃ śūlañca nṛkaroṭikām | 

bibhratīṃ karapadmaiśca vṛddhāṃ galitayauvanām || 3 || 

evaṃ dhyātvā mahādevyai jalānāmañjalitrayam | 

dattvā japettu gāyatrīṃ daśadhā śatadhāpi vā | 

trisandhyametāṃ prajapan sandhyāyāḥ phalamāpnuyāt | 

tatastu tarpayeddevi ! devarṣipitṛdevatāḥ | 

praṇavaḥ sa dvitīyākhyastarpayāmi namaḥ padam | 

śaktau tu praṇavo māyāṃ namaḥ sthāne dviṭhaṃ vadet | 

tataścārghyaṃ mahādevye dattvā mūlaṃ japet sudhīḥ | 

yathāśakti japaṃ kṛtvā devyā vāmakare'rpayet | 

praṇamya devīpūjārthaṃ jalamādāya sādhakaḥ | 

natvā tīrthe paṭhan stotraṃ devatādhyānatatparaḥ | 

yāgamaṇḍapamāgatya pāṇipādau viśodhayet || 

vṛhannīlatantre prathamapaṭale || 

sandhyāṃ sāyantanīṃ kuryyād dvādaśyādiṣvapi priye ! | 

p. 354) akurvannirayaṃ yāti yato nityāgamakriyā | 

etadvacanamavijñāya vaidikī tāntrikī sandhyā yathānukramayogata iti tantrasāradhṛtavacane 
anukramapadasvarasāt yatra vaidikī sandhyā na vidyate tatra tāntrikyapi na karaṇīyā | 

tena dvādaśyādau sāyam evamaśaucādau tāntrikasandhyā na kāryyeti kaścidvadati 
tanna | 

aśaucasya vaidikaviṣayatvāditi || 

tathā ca viśvasāratantre dvitīyapaṭale || 

mahāgurunipātādau na pūjāyā vikalpanā | 

mohādvā yadi vā daivāt pūjayenna kadācana | 

tasya sarvavināśaḥ syādityuktantu sadāśivairiti | 

pūjayediti tāntrikanityakarmopalakṣaṇam | 

yathā kāmadatantre navamapaṭale || 

upāsakā ye śrīkālyāste śuddhāḥ sarvadaiva hi | 

janurmaraṇajāśaucaṃ tathānyad yadaśaucakam | 

sarvaṃ na bādhate kvāpi kālikāyā upāsake | 

gaṅgeva śuddhaḥ satataṃ kimaśaucaṃ sureśvari ! | 

kālīmuddiśya pūjādijapahomamaśaucake | 

na tyājyaṃ tena śāktena sarvaṃ kāryyaṃ sadaiva hi | 

kālīmuddiśya dānādi na ca tyājyamaśaucake | 

sarvadā paramaḥ śuddhaḥ śākta eva maheśvari ! | 

atra śākta iti sāmānyenopadānāt pūrvavacane kālyā iti śaktimātropalakṣaṇam || 

atha yogināṃ sandhyā kalpasūtraṭīkāyāṃ tṛtīyakāṇḍe || 

yā gururūpiṇī mṛṇālasūtrāntaragā svaprakāśā kuṇḍalinīśaktiḥ 
kulākulasamarasabhāvena sādhanāntardhyeyā | 

tathā madhyāhnasamaye taruṇāvayavāmatibhāsvarāṃ kāmarājarūpiṇīṃ 
mūlādibrahmarandhrāntaṃ brahmarandhrādimūlāntaṃ dhyāyet | 

tathā sāyaṃsamaye śvetavarṇāṃ śaktivījasvarūpāṃ mṛṇālatantunibhāṃ 
kulākulayogenānusandadhyāt | 

arddharātre parāparakuṇḍalinīrūpāṃ padmarāgavarṇāṃ mūlādihṛdayārvyantaṃ 
vāgbhavavījarūpiṇīṃ hṛdayādbhrūmadhyaparyyantaṃ kāmavījarūpāṃ 
bhrūmadhyādbrahmarandhrāntaṃ śaktivījarūpāṃ dhyāyet | 

taduktamudayākarapaddhatyām || 

ādyā mṛṇālasūtrābhā pītā raktā tu madhyamā | 

p. 355) śvetā tvantargatā śaktiḥ sandhyācobhayarūpiṇī | 

tarpaṇantu mūlādhārāt somasūryyāgnirūpiṇīm | 

kuṇḍalinīṃ samutthāpya parabinduṃ nirbhidya tadudbhavāmṛtena sveṣṭadevatāṃ tarpayet | 

taduktaṃ navacakreśvare || 

candrārkānalasaṅghaṭṭād galitaṃ yatparāmṛtam | 

tenāmṛtena divyena tarpayet sundarīṃ parām || 

atha sūryyapūjā || 

mūlādhārāt kulakuṇḍalinīmutthāpya hṛdayārkamaṇḍalaṃ nītvā 
tatkarṇikāntargatasudhādhārayā mūlamantraṃ smaran siñcediti yogināmantaryāgaḥ || 

atha kaulānāṃ sandhyā gandharvatantre ṣaṣṭhapaṭale || 

ātmānaṃ sarvadā samyak dhyāyed dhyānaparāyaṇaḥ | 

devatābhedataḥ sandhyā mānasīyaṃ parā priye ! | 

śivaśaktyoḥ samāyogo yasmin kāle prajāyate | 

sā sandhyā kulaniṣṭhānāṃ samādhisthaiḥ pratīyate | 

bhūtaśuddhitantre prathamapaṭale'pi || 

antaḥ sandhyāṃ maheśāni ! śṛṇu yatnena pārvati ! | 

śivaśaktisamāyogo yasmin kāle prajāyate | 

sā sandhyā kulasādhūnāṃ samādhisthaiḥ pratīyate || 

vaidikasandhyādiprayogastu brāhmaṇasarvasvādāvanusandheyaḥ | 

kevalamārjanasya viśeṣo nirvaṇatantre tṛtīyapaṭale yathā | 

āpo mārjanamantrasya prakāraṃ śṛṇu yatnataḥ | 

bhūmau śirasi cākāśe ākāśe bhūmimastake | 

mūrdhnibhūmau tathākāśe yajurvede sureśvari ! | 

sāmātharvairidaṃ devi ! ṛgvede śṛṇu śailaje ! | 

śūnye śirasi cākāśe bhūmau śūnye śire tathā | 

bhūmau śūnye tathā mūrdhni cāpo mārjanamācaret | 

āpohiṣṭheti mantrasya aṣṭākṣarakrameṇa tu | 

mārjanaṃ tatkrameṇaiva sarvapāpavināśanam | 

vyatikrameṇa cārvaṅgi ! brahmahatyā pade pade | 

varathastrīn mūrdhni dadyād ye savo vyomni cocyate | 

bhūmau dadyānna trayeṇa iti sammārjanaṃ viduḥ | 

ata-eva kramaṃ sarvaṃ tava snehāt prakāśitam | 

stutiñca kavacaṃ devi ! paṭhitvā praṇamet sudhīḥ | 

atha gāyatrī || 

p. 356) nirvāṇatantre tṛtīyabrāhmaṇapaṭale || 

gāyatrīṃ śṛṇu cārvaṅgi ! caturvedaprapūjitām | 

vedamāteti vikhyātāṃ trivargaphaladāyinīm | 

hālāhalaṃ samuddhṛtya ityādi gāyatrī jñeyā || 

iti japtvā maheśāni ! mukto bhavati nānyathā | 

antyayakārayoḥ sthāne ya iti ca yaḥ paṭhet | 

sa caṇḍāla iti khyāto brahmahatyā dine dine | 

ata-eva maheśāni ! tava snehāt prakāśitam | 

sāvitrī paramā vidyā trailokye ca sudurlabhā | 

asyā grahaṇa mātreṇābhūdbrahmā nātra saṃśayaḥ || 

yattu gāyatrītantrasya prathamabrāhmaṇapaṭale || 

praṇavatrayasaṃyuktaṃ brāhmaṇeṣu prakīrttitam | 

kṣetrādau parameśāni ! śasyate praṇavadvayam || 

dvitīyabrāhmaṇapaṭale'pi || 

praṇavatrayasaṃyuktāṃ gāyatrīṃ prajapettu yaḥ | 

gāyatryāḥ phalamāpnoti anyathāraṇyarodanamiti | 

praṇavatrayayuktagāyatrīkathanam | 

tatprabalakalau tatpuṭitatvābhiprāyeṇa tathā ca tatraiva || 

vidyā vā upavidyā vā praṇavaiḥ puṭitaṃ kalau | 

japtvā siddhimavāpnoti tadṛte viphalaṃ bhavet || 

asyārthaḥ | 

rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtam || 

tatra praṇavena brahma pratipādyate vyāhṛtibhirapi tadeva pratipādyate | 

bhūriti sanmātramucyate sarvaṃ bhāvayati prakāśayati iti vyutpattyā bhuvamiti 
cidrūpamucyate | 

sarvapreraṇāt svariti tejorūpatvāt pūjanīyatvāt maha iti | 

sarvajananatvājjana iti | 

jñānasvarūpatayā sarvatāpanatayā vā tapa iti | 

paratvāt sarvātmakatvān anantajñānatvāt satyamiti saptavyāhṛtibhiḥ paraṃ brahmaivocyate 
| 

praṇavena saha āsāmāntaraḥ sambandho yathā | 

praṇavasya akārādisaptamātrāḥ saptavyāhṛtaya iti yadvā bhūrādisaptalokāḥ 
praṇavavācyabrahmātmakāstadatiriktaṃ na kiñcidastītyarthaḥ | 

tatpadena brahmocyate | 

oṃ tat saditi nirdeśo brahmaṇastrividho mata iti vacanāt | 

suñ prāṇiprasava iti dhātorjātatvāt sarvaprapañcopadeśakatvena savituriti | 

niratiśayānandarūpatvāt sarvavaraṇīyatvāt sarvasevanīyamiti vareṇyam | 

p. 357) pāpatarjanādbhaktavartmanā'bhayādbhargaḥ | 

sarvaprakāśakatvena devasyeti saviturdevasyetyatra ṣaṣṭhīprayogo rāhoḥ 
śiravadaupacārikatrayaṃ bhūtam | 

brahmadhīmahi dhyāyemahi no'smākaṃ dhiyo buddhīryaḥ pracodayāt prerayet | 

atra ya iti liṅgavyatyayaḥ chāndasatvāt | 

tena sarvāntaḥkaraṇaprakāśakaḥ sarvasākṣī paramātmoktaḥ | 

vyāpnotītyāpaḥ sarvaprakāśakamiti jyotiḥ sarvātiśāyitvāt hrasvaḥ | 

yadvā | 

āpojyotīrasa iti somāgnyosteja ucyate | 

yatastadātmakaṃ jagat | 

anāśitvādamṛtaṃ vṛhattvādvṛṃhaṇatvād brahma satyajñānānandalakṣaṇam | 

bhūrbhuvaḥsvaḥpadavācyaṃ so'hamityāspadavācyamiti | 

evaṃ sarvātmakabrahmabodhako gāyatrīmantraḥ | 

praṇavādyā iti vyāhṛtiḥ sambadhyate | 

tatpadādiketyagrimeṇa sambadhyate || 

atra sapta vyāhṛtayaḥ || 

oṃ bhūḥ oṃ bhuvaḥ oṃ svaḥ oṃ mahaḥ oṃ janaḥ oṃ tapaḥ oṃ satyam | 

etadagre tat savituriti ṛk tadante oṃ āpojyotīraso'mṛtaṃ brahmabhūrbhuvaḥ svaromidaṃ 
śivah | 

asyāstantrāntaroktaṃ vījādiryathā | 

re ityetattu bījaṃ syād yavarṇaṃ śaktirucyate | 

nītyetat kīlakaṃ proktaṃ gāyatryāstripadātmana iti | 

patsandhiṣviti pādadvayasandhicatuṣke akṣaracatuṣkaṃ taduktaṃ paddvayasandhicatuṣkamiti | 

evaṃ bhujadvayasandhicatuṣke akṣaracatuṣkam | 

anyatraikaikamakṣaram evaṃ caturviṃśatyakṣarāṇi nyasanīyāni | 

nīyetyasya pṛthak tena caturviṃśatyakṣarātmakaṃ pratyekamakṣarāṇām 
ṛṣicchandodevatādhyānamucyate | 

akṣarāṇāñca sarveṣāṃ prajāpatir-ṛṣistathā | 

viniyogo'ṅgavinyāse gāyatrīcchanda ucyate | 

akṣarāṇāntu sarveṣāṃ vakṣyante devatāḥ kramāt | 

agnivāyusūryyavidyudyamā varuṇa eva ca | 

vṛhaspatiśca parjanya indro gandharva eva ca | 

pūṣā śivaśca tvaṣṭā ca vāsavaśca maruttathā | 

somo'ṅgirā viśvedevā aśvinī ca prajāpatiḥ | 

p. 358) sarvadevaśca rudraśca brahmā viṣṇustu devatāḥ | 

japakāle cintanīyāstāsāṃ sāyujyamāpnuyāt | 

tatkāraṃ campakāpītaṃ brahmaviṣṇuśivātmakam | 

śatapatrāsanārūḍhaṃ dhyāyet susthāsanasthitam | 

sakāraṃ cintayedevamatasīpuṣpasannibham | 

padma madhyasthitaṃ saumyamupapātakanāśanam | 

atasīkusumābhā te-uḍīti yasya bhāṣā natvatasī śoṇapuṣpaṃ 
vakṣyamāṇagāyatrītantroktavacane śyāmavarṇadarśanāt || 

vikaraṃ kapilaṃ nityaṃ kamalāsanasaṃsthitam | 

dhyāyecchāntaḥ dvijaśreṣṭho mahāpātakanāśanam | 

tukāraṃ cintayet prājña indranīlasamaprabham | 

nirdahet sarvapāpāṇi graharogasamudbhavam | 

rvakāraṃ vahnidīptābhaṃ cintayecca vicakṣaṇaḥ | 

bhrūṇahatyākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇādeva naśyati | 

rekāraṃ vimalaṃ dhyāyet śuddhasphaṭikasannibham | 

pāpaṃ naśyati tat kṣipramagamyāgamanodbhavam | 

ṇīkāraṃ cintayed yogī śuddhasphaṭikasannibham | 

abhakṣyabhakṣaṇaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇādeva naśyati | 

yakāraṃ tārakāvarṇaminduśeṣavibhūṣitam | 

yogināṃ varadaṃ dhyāyed brahmahatyāvināśanam | 

bhakāraṃ kṛṣṇavarṇantu nīlameghasamaprabham | 

dhyātvā puruṣa ityādi pāpaṃ nāśayati dvijaḥ | 

rgokāraṃ raktavarṇañca kamalāsanasaṃsthitam | 

gohatyādikṛtaṃ pāpaṃ dhyātvā naśyati tatkṣaṇāt | 

dekāraṃ raktasaṅkāśaṃ kamalāsanasaṃsthitam | 

cintayet satataṃ yogī strīhatyā dahanaṃ param | 

vakāraṃ cintayecchuddhaṃ jātīpuṣpasamaprabham | 

guruhatyākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ dhyātvā naśyati tatkṣaṇāt | 

syakārantu tathā pītaṃ suvarṇasadṛśaprabham | 

manasā cintitaṃ pāpaṃ dhyātvā nudati cānaghaḥ | 

dhīkāraṃ cintayecchuklaṃ kundapuṣpasamaprabham | 

pitṛmātṛbadhāt pāpānmucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ | 

makāraṃ padmarāgābhaṃ cintayeddīptatejasam | 

pūrvajanmārjjitaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇādeva naśyati | 

hikāraṃ śaṅkhavarṇantu pūrṇacandrasamaprabham | 

aśeṣapāpadahanaṃ dhyāyennityaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ | 

p. 359) dhikāraṃ pāṇḍavaṃ dhyāyet padmasyoparisaṃsthitam | 

pratigrahakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ smaraṇādeva naśyati | 

yokāraṃ raktavarṇantu indragopasamaprabham | 

dhyātvā prāṇibadhe pāpaṃ nirdahenmunipuṅgavaḥ | 

dvitīyaścaiva yaḥ prokto yokāro raktasannibhaḥ | 

nirdahet sarvapāpāṇi nānyaiḥ pāpaiḥ pralipyate  | 

naḥkārantu mukhaḥpūrvamādityodayasannibham | 

sakṛddhyātvā dvijaśreṣṭhaḥ sa gacchet paramaṃ padam | 

nīlotpaladalaśyābhaṃ prakāraṃ dakṣiṇāyanam | 

sakṛddhyātvā dvijaśreṣṭhaḥ sa gacchedaiśvaraṃ padam | 

saumyaṃ gorocanāpītaṃ cokāraṃ caturānanam | 

sakṛddhyātvā dvijaśreṣṭhaḥ sa gacchedvaiṣṇavaṃ padam | 

śuklavarṇendusaṅkāśaṃ dakāraṃ paścimānanam | 

sakṛddhyātvā dvijaśreṣṭhaḥ sa gacchedbrahmaṇaḥ padam | 

yātkārantu śivaṃ proktaṃ caturvadanasaprabham | 

pratyakṣaphalado brahmaviṣṇurudra iti smṛtiḥ | 

etaddhyātvā tu medhāvī japaṃ homaṃ karoti yaḥ | 

na bhavet sūtakaṃ tasya mṛtakañca na vidyate | 

yastvevaṃ na vijānāti gāyatrīñca tathāvidhām | 

kathitaṃ sūtakaṃ tasya mṛtakañca mayānagha ! | 

naiva dānaphalaṃ tasya na ca yajñaphalaṃ bhavet | 

na ca tīrthaphalaṃ proktaṃ tasyaiva sūtake satīti || 

atha varṇakalpaḥ | 

tathā gāyatrītantre || 

takāraṃ campakāpītaṃ brahmaviṣṇuśivātmakam | 

tsakāraṃ cintayet śyāmaṃ vikāraṃ kapilaṃ tathā | 

tukāramindranīlābhaṃ rvakāraṃ vahnisannibham | 

rekāraṃ vahnisaṅkāśaṃ ṇikāraṃ raktavarṇakam | 

yaṅkāraṃ dhūmrasaṅkāśaṃ bhakāraṃ kṛṣṇameva ca | 

rgokāraṃ raktavarṇañca dekāraṃ śyāmalaṃ tathā | 

vakāraṃ śuklasaṅkāśaṃ syakāraṃ nīlaśobhitam | 

dhīkāraṃ kundapuṣpābhaṃ makāraṃ śuklameva ca | 

hikāraṃ candrasaṅkāśaṃ dhikāraṃ pītameva ca | 

yokāraṃ taḍidākāraṃ yokāraṃ dhūmravarṇakam | 

aśeṣaṃ śoṣayet pāpaṃ vāyubījaṃ yathā tathā | 

nakārantu mukhaṃ proktaṃ prataptakanakaprabham | 

nakārasya samīpe tu bindudvayaṃ viśeṣataḥ | 

ūrdhvabinduraktavarṇamadhaḥ śyāmaṃ prakīrttitam | 

p. 360) nīlotpaladalaśyāmaṃ prakāraṃ dakṣiṇāmukham | 

saumyaṃ gorocanāpītaṃ cokāraṃ paścimāmukham | 

śuklaśaṅkhasamākāraṃ dakāraṃ caturāmukham | 

yātkāraṃ mūrdhni vijñeyaṃ ūrdhvāsyaṃ brahmamandiram || 

pūrvoktagāyatrīvarṇadhyānoktavarṇena yatra yatra viśeṣastatra tatrāmnāyabhedena jñeyaḥ 
|| 

atha gāyatrīyantram || 

ādau trikoṇaṃ vinyasya ṣaṭkoṇaṃ tadvahirnyaset | 

vṛttañcāṣṭadalaṃ padmaṃ tadvahiścaturasrakam | 

caturdvārasamāyuktaṃ sāvitrīyantramīritam || 

śāradāyām || 

vidhāya maṇḍalaṃ vidvān trikoṇojjvalakarṇikam | 

maṇḍalamiti sarvatobhadramaṇḍalam || 

trikoṇojjvalamiti tatkarṇikāyāṃ trikoṇaṃ likhet ||

atha gāyatryā ṛṣyādinyāsaḥ || 

śāradātilake ekaviṃśatipaṭale || 

ṛṣyādyāḥ praṇavasyaite munibhiḥ parikīrttitāḥ | 

jamadagnibharadvājabhṛgugautamakāśyapān | 

viśvāmitravaśiṣṭhākhyau vyāhṛtīnāmṛṣīn viduḥ | 

gāyatryuṣṇigathānuṣṭuvvṛhatīpaṅktayaḥ punaḥ | 

triṣṭuvjagatyau chandāṃsi kathitāni manīṣibhiḥ | 

saptārcirbalinaḥ sūryyo vākpatirvaruṇo vṛṣā | 

viśvedevāḥ kramādāsāṃ devatāḥ parikīrttitāḥ | 

gāyatryā munirākhyāto viśvāmitro mahādyutiḥ | 

gāyatrīcchanda ityuktaṃ devatā savitā smṛtā | 

śiraso'sya munirbrahmā chandodevyādikā smṛtā | 

gāyatrī paramātmā syāddevatā kathitā budhaiḥ | 

vyāhṛtīḥ sapta tārādyā hṛṇmukhāṃsoruyugmake | 

jaṭhare nyasya mantrajño gāyatryarṇān tanau nyaset | 

patsandhiṣu dhvaje nābhau hṛtkaṇṭhabhujasandhiṣu | 

āsyanāsākapolākṣikarṇabhrūmastake punaḥ | 

pāścātyottarayāmyagrāmūrdhva vaktreṣu sādhakaḥ | 

padāni daśa vinyasyedeṣu sthāneṣu mantravit | 

śirobhrūmadhyahṛdvaktrakaṇṭhahṛnnābhiguhyake | 

jānunoḥ pādayoryugme tacchiraḥ śirasi nyaset ||

p. 361) atha ṣaḍaṅganyāsaḥ || 

nirvāṇatantre || 

ṣaḍaṅganyāsamantraṃ yat tat śṛṇuṣva priyaṃvade ! | 

praṇavadvayaṃ hṛtpadme bhūḥkāraṃ śīrṣadeśake | 

bhuvaḥ śikhāyāṃ svaḥkāraṃ kavace vinyase sudhīḥ | 

netratraye bhūrbhuvaḥsvaḥ svaḥkāraṃ karayugmake | 

namaḥ svāhā vaṣaṭ kūrccaṃ vauṣaṭ phaṭ kramato nyaset || 

śāradāyāntu || 

brahmaṇe hṛdayaṃ proktaṃ viṣṇave śira īritam | 

śikhā rudrāya kavacamīśvarāya samīritam | 

netraṃ sadāśivāyoktamastraṃ sarvātmane smṛtam | 

ṣaḍaṅgānyevamuktāni yathāsthānaṃ pravinyaset || 

atha dhyānam || 

nirvāṇatantre || 

dhyānaṃ śṛṇu varārohe ! yathā dhyātvā japennaraḥ | 

śvetavarṇā samuddiṣṭā kauśeyavasanā tathā | 

śvetairvilepanaiḥ puṣpairalaṅkāraiśca bhūṣitā |

ādityamaṇḍalāntaḥsthā brahmalokagatātha vā | 

akṣasūtradharā devī padmāsanagatā tathā | 

oṃ tejo'si śukramasyamṛtamasi dhāmanāmāsi priyaṃ devānāmanādhṛṣṭaṃ 
devayajanamasi | 

gāyatryasi ekapadī dvipadī tripadī catuṣpadyapadasi na hi padyase namaste turīyāya 
darśitāya parāya parorajase | 

śāradoktadhyānaṃ yathā | 

muktāvidrumahemanīladhavalacchāyairmukhaistrīkṣaṇairyuktā-mindunibaddharatnamukuṭāṃ 
tattvārṇavarṇātmikām | 

gāyatrīṃ varadābhayāṅkuśakarāṃ śubhraṃ kapālaṃ guṇaṃ śaṅkhaṃ 
cakramathāravindayugalaṃ hastairvahantīṃ bhaje || 

gāyatrītantraprathamabrāhmaṇapaṭale || 

caturbhujāṃ śaśikalāṃ jaṭājūṭasamanvitām | 

ṛksāmayajuṣāṃ nāthāṃ praphullakamalekṣaṇām | 

pañcāśadvarṇagrathitamālādyotitahṛtsthalām | 

anekaratnanirmāṇakaṇṭhadeśavirājitām | 

divyagandhapraliptāṅgīṃ śuklavastrapariṣkṛtām | 

śuklapadmasamāsīnāṃ śuklavastrottarīyiṇīm | 

brahmādidevatāvṛndaiḥ saṃstutāṃ nityanūtanām | 

evaṃ dhyātvā nyasedvarṇaṃ paṭhitvā chanda uttamam | 

asyāgnimukhīgāyatryāḥ paraṃbrahma ṛṣir-ṛgyajuḥsāmātharvacchandaḥ savitā devatā 
paraṃbrahmamāturnyāse viniyogaḥ | 

p. 362) pādādimastakāntañca nyāsaṃ kuryyādviśāladhīḥ | 

vinā nyāsaṃ japed yastu gāyatrī vipalā bhavet | 

vinā mudrāṃ nyased yastu nyāso bhavati niṣphalaḥ | 

yatistu pañcamudrābhirnyāsaṃ kuryāt prayatnataḥ | 

gṛhī ca yatnataḥ kuryyāt trisandhyaṃ tattvamudrayā | 

kaniṣṭhānāmikāṅguṣṭhā aṅguṣṭhā tarjjanī tathā | 

aṅguṣṭhāmadhyamābhyāñca aṅguṣṭhānāmikā tathā | 

kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhasaṃyogāt pañcamudrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ | 

pādādi mastakāntañca bhūrbhuvaḥ svarnyaset sudhīḥ | 

saptadhā vinyasedvidvān gāyatrīṃ kramayogataḥ | 

pādāṅguṣṭhe nyasedyogī takāraṃ bhāvayet param | 

tsakāraṃ gulphayormadhye vikāraṃ jaṅghayostathā | 

tukāraṃ jānunormadhye rvakāramūrumadhyataḥ | 

rekāraṃ guhyadeśe ca ṇikāraṃ vṛṣaṇe nyaset | 

yaṅkāraṃ kaṭideśe ca bhakāraṃ nābhimaṇḍale | 

rgokāraṃ jaṭhare nyaseddekāraṃ stanayornyaset | 

vakāraṃ hṛdi madhye ca syakāraṃ kaṇṭhato nyaset | 

dhīkāraṃ vadane bhadre makāraṃ tāluni nyaset | 

hikāraṃ nāsikāgre ca dhikāraṃ cakṣuṣornyaset | 

yokāraṃ prathamaṃ yogī bhruvormadhye ca vinyaset | 

dvitīyañca tato jñeyaṃ lalāṭe ca na saṃśayaḥ | 

naḥkārantu mukhe jñeyaṃ prakāraṃ dakṣiṇāmukham | 

cokāraṃ paścime tadvaddakāraṃ cottare tathā | 

yātkāraṃ mūrdhni vijñeyaṃ sarvaṃ vyāpya sthitaṃ sadā || 

gāyatrītantre daśamabrāhmaṇapaṭale || 

adhunā sampravakṣyāmi gāyatryāḥ pūjanaṃ priye ! | 

upāsitasya mantrasya yathā pūjā vidhīyate |

gāyatryāḥ pūjanaṃ bhadre ! tathaiva kathayāmi te | 

śivapūjā śaktipūjā viṣṇupūjādikā ca yā | 

sarvā pūjā vṛthā bhadre ! gāyatrīpūjanaṃ vinā | 

pūjāmaṇḍapamāgatya gāyatryācamanaṃ caret | 

gāyatryāḥ prathamaṃ bījaṃ pūjāsthāne nyaset sudhīḥ | 

kuśāsane copaviśya prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret | 

pūrakādikrameṇaiva gāyatryāḥ prāṇadhāraṇam | 

sāmānyārghyaṃ tataḥ kuryyāt pūrvoktakramavartmanā | 

p. 363) gāyatrīṃ saṃkṛduccāryya tāmrapātre jalaṃ kṣipet | 

tīrthamāvāhya yatnena gāyatrīṃ prajapettataḥ | 

tato dhenvāmṛtīkṛtya prathamāṣṭākṣareṇa vai | 

dvitīyāṣṭākṣare vipro jalaṃ vīkṣya praśāntaye | 

tṛtīyāṣṭākṣare devi ! jalairātmaviśodhanam | 

anenaiva vidhānena sṛṣṭisthitilayātmakam | 

pūjanaṃ parameśāni ! tadaiva phaladāyakam | 

yantranyāsadhyānādikantu pūrvameva likhitam || 

sauraṃ pīṭhaṃ yajettatra dīptādinavaśaktibhiḥ | 

tatra sarvatobhadramaṇḍale dīptādayastu caturdaśapaṭale || 

yathā || 

dīptā sūkṣmā jayā bhadrā vibhūtirvimalā punaḥ | 

aghorā vidyutā sarvatomukhī pīṭhaśaktayaḥ | 

saurapīṭhaṃ sūryyaprakaraṇe'nusandheyam || 

mūlamantreṇa kḷptāyāṃ mūrttau devīṃ prapūjayet || 

nirvāṇatantre || 

jīvanyāsādikaṃ kṛtvā pūjayettāṃ trivargadām | 

hālāhalādikaṃ mantraṃ samastaṃ parameśvari ! | 

samuccāryya vadet pādyaṃ sāvitrīṃ ṅeyutāṃ tataḥ | 

tyāgātmakamanuṃ paścād yathā vibhavavistaraiḥ | 

pūjayedbahuyatnena cānyathā brāhmaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ | 

śāradāyām || 

koṇeṣu triṣu sampūjyāḥ brāhmyādyāḥ śaktayo vahiḥ | 

ādityādyāstataḥ pūjyā ūṣādisahitāḥ kramāt | 

ādityādyāścoṣādayaśca sūryyaprakaraṇe veditavyāḥ | 

tataḥ ṣaḍaṅgānyabhyarcet keśareṣu yathāvidhi | 

prahlādinīṃ prabhāṃ paścānnityāṃ viśvambharāṃ punaḥ | 

vilāsinīprabhāvatyāvūṣāṃ śāntāṃ yajet punaḥ | 

kānti durgāsarasvatyau vidyārūpāstataḥ param | 

viśālāsahitāmīśāṃ vyāpinīṃ vimalāṃ japet | 

tamo'pahāriṇīṃ sūkṣmāṃ viśvayoniṃ jayāvahām | 

padmālayāṃ parāṃ śobhāṃ padmarūpāṃ tato'rccayet | 

brāhmyādyāḥ sāruṇā vāhye pūjayet proktalakṣaṇāḥ | 

tato'bhyarcced grahān vāhye śakrāṃdīnāyudhaiḥ saha | 

itthamāvaraṇairdevīṃ daśabhiḥ paripūjayet | 

dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ bhoktā syāddvijasattamaḥ || 

nirvāṇatantre || 

dravyābhāve varārohe ! pādyādyairudakātmakaiḥ | 

pūjayitvā japeddevīṃ gāyatrīṃ paramākṣarīm | 

p. 364) etena dravyābhāve jalādinā pūjāyāṃ karttavyāyāmapi 
etatpādyamityādivaktavyamata-eva smṛtiḥ | 

tailaṃ pratinidhiṃ kuryyād yajñārthe yājñiko yadi | 

prakṛtyaiva tadā hetorbrūyād ghṛtavatīmiti | 

tenaitatpādyārthaṃ jalamiti vākyamanuśāsanaviruddhamiti | 

japaphalamapi tatraiva || 

daśabhirjanmajanitaṃ śatenāpi purākṛtam | 

trijanmajaṃ sahasreṇa gāyatrī hanti pātakam | 

jape tu viśeṣa ukto gāyatrītantre || 

gāyatryā puṭitaṃ kṛtvā iṣṭamantraṃ japecchatam | 

iṣṭamantreṇa puṭitaṃ gāyatrīṃ prajapecchatam | 

etajjapaṃ maheśāni ! ādhārādheyamuttamam | 

vinādhāraṃ maheśāni ! ādheyena vinā tathā | 

nādhāraṃ sidhyate bhadre ! ādheyañca na sidhyati | 

sarveṣu viṣṇumantreṣu sauragāṇapatau tathā || 

ādhārādheyabhāvastu sarvatra karavarṇini ! | 

sāṅganāllocanād yastu jhaṭiti praviśed gṛham | 

na paśyeccakṣuṣā kiñcid gṛhamadhyeṣu yat sthitam | 

evamādhāramādheyaṃ yaḥ karoti dvijātikaḥ | 

aṅkuro jāyate bhadre ! lakṣajāpānnaṃ saṃśayaḥ | 

naca jīvātmanorbhinnā gāyatrī brahmarūpiṇī | 

gāyatrī na hi bhinnā syājjīvātmā tatsvarūpiṇī | 

sarvadā ubhayoraikyaṃ nārikele yathodakam | 

dvijātiḥ kathyate devabhāva eva hi brāhmaṇaḥ | 

yathā sparśamaṇisparśāt tāmro'pi kāñcanaṃ sadā | 

gāyatrīsahita ātmā dvijātmā tena īritaḥ || 

gayatrītantre || 

japet sūryyamukho vipro nāsti diṅniyamaḥ kvacit | 

gāyatrīṃ prajapennityaṃ trisandhyaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ | 

aśauce tu maheśāni ! gāyatrīṃ manasā smaret | 

gāyatrī bahurūpā ca trisandhyā dhyānameva || 

tatraiva || 

oṃ maheśvaravadanotpannā viṣṇorhṛdayasaṃsthitā | 

brahmaṇā samanujñātā gaccha devi ! yathecchayā | 

iti mantraṃ samuccāryya devyā vāmakare budhaḥ | 

samarpayejjapaphalaṃ tataḥ stotrādikaṃ paṭhet || 

atha gāyatrīkavacam || 

śrīdevyuvāca || 

p. 365) devadeva ! mahādeva ! saṃsārārṇavatāraka ! | 

gāyatrīkavacaṃ deva ! kṛpayā kathaya prabho ! || 

śrīdevadeva uvāca || 

mūlādhāreṣu yā nityā kuṇḍalī tattvarūpiṇī | 

sūkṣmātisūkṣmā paramā visatantusvarūpiṇī | 

vidyutpuñjapratīkāśā kuṇḍalākṛtirūpiṇī | 

paramabrahmagṛhiṇī pañcāśadvarṇarūpiṇī | 

śivasya narttakī nityā paraṃ brahmaprapūjitā | 

brahmaṇaḥ saiva gāyatrī saccidānandarūpiṇī | 

tadbhramāvarttavāto'yaṃ prāṇātmā nityanūtanaḥ | 

nityaṃ tiṣṭhatu sānandā kuṇḍalī bhavavigrahe | 

atigopyaṃ mahat puṇyaṃ trikoṭītīrthasaṃyutam | 

sarvayajñamayaṃ devi ! sarvadānamayaṃ sadā | 

sarvajñānamayaṃ devi ! paraṃbrahmamayaṃ sadā | 

kavacaṃ kathayāmyadya pārvati ! prāṇavallabhe ! | 

oṃ oṃ bhūḥ oṃ oṃ oṃ oṃ bhuvaḥ oṃ oṃ oṃ oṃ svaḥ oṃ oṃ oṃ oṃ ta oṃ 
oṃ tsa oṃ oṃ vi oṃ oṃ tu oṃ oṃ rva oṃ oṃ re oṃ oṃ ṇi oṃ oṃ yaṃ oṃ 
oṃ bha oṃ oṃ rgo oṃ oṃ de oṃ oṃ va oṃ oṃ sya oṃ oṃ dhī oṃ oṃ ma oṃ 
oṃ hi oṃ oṃ dhi oṃ oṃ yo oṃ oṃ yo oṃ oṃ naḥ oṃ oṃ pra oṃ oṃ co oṃ 
oṃ da oṃ oṃ yāt oṃ oṃ oṃ oṃ | 

oṃ bhūḥ oṃ pātu me mūlaṃ caturdalasamanvitaṃ | 

oṃ bhuvaḥ oṃ pātu me liṅgaṃ ṣaḍdalasamanvitam | 

oṃ svaḥ oṃ pātu me kaṇṭhaṃ sākāśaṃ dalaṣoḍaśam | 

oṃ ta oṃ pātu me rūpaṃ brahmaṇyakāraṇaṃ param | 

oṃ tsa oṃ vadanaṃ pātu rasanāsaṃyutaṃ mama | 

oṃ vi oṃ pātu me gandhaṃ sadā śarīrasaṃyutam | 

oṃ tu oṃ pātu me sparśaṃ śarīrasya ca kāraṇam | 

oṃ rva oṃ pātu me śabdaṃ śabdavigrahakāraṇam | 

oṃ re oṃ pātu me nityaṃ tvacaṃ śarīrarakṣakam | 

oṃ ṇi oṃ pātu me akṣaṃ sarvatattvaikakāraṇam | 

oṃ yaṃ oṃ pātu me śrotraṃ śravaṇasya ca kāraṇam | 

oṃ bha oṃ pātu me ghrāṇaṃ gandhopādānakāraṇam | 

oṃ rgo oṃ pātu me vākyaṃ sabhāyāṃ śabdarūpiṇī | 

p. 366) oṃ de oṃ pātu me bāhuyugalaṃ brahmakāraṇam | 

oṃ va oṃ pātu me pādayugalaṃ brahmakāraṇam | 

oṃ sya oṃ pātu me liṅgaṃ sajalaṃ ṣaḍdalairyutam | 

oṃ dhī oṃ pātu me nityaṃ prakṛtiṃ śabdakāraṇam | 

oṃ ma oṃ pātu me nityaṃ manobrahmasvarūpiṇīm | 

oṃ hi oṃ pātu me buddhiṃ paraṃbrahmamayaṃ sadā | 

oṃ dhi oṃ pātu me nityamahaṅkāraṃ yathā tathā | 

oṃ yo oṃ pātu me nityaṃ pṛthivīṃ pārthivaṃ vapuḥ | 

oṃ yo oṃ pātu me nityaṃ jalaṃ sarvatra sarvadā | 

oṃ naḥ oṃ pātu me nityaṃ tejaḥpuñjaṃ yathā tathā | 

oṃ pra oṃ pātu me nityamanilaṃ śarīrakāraṇam | 

oṃ co oṃ pātu me nityamākāśaṃ śaśisannibham | 

oṃ da oṃ pātu me jihvāṃ japayajñasya kāraṇam | 

oṃ yāt oṃ pātu me cittaṃ śivajñānamayaṃ sadā | 

oṃ tattvāni pātu me nityaṃ gāyatrī paradevatā | 

oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥsvaḥ pātu me nityaṃ brahmāṇī jaṭharaṃ kṣudhā | 

tvaṣṭā me satataṃ pātu brahmāṇī bhūrbhavaḥsvaraḥ | 

asyāḥ śrīgāyatryāḥ paraṃ brahma ṛṣir-ṛgyajuḥsāmātharvāṃśchandāṃsi 
śrīgāyatrībrāhmaṇo devatā dharmārthakāmamokṣārthe vinigogaḥ || 

tato gāyatrīṃ paṭhet | 

kāmakrodhādikaṃ sarvaṃ smaraṇād yāti sāmyatām | 

idaṃ kavacamajñātvā gāyatrīṃ prajaped yadi | 

śatakoṭijapenaiva na siddhirjāyate priye ! | 

gāyatrīvācanāt sarvaṃ smaraṇaṃ sidhyati dhruvam | 

paṭhitvā kavacaṃ vipro gāyatrīṃ sakṛduccaret | 

sarvapāpavinirmukto jīvanmukto bhaveddvijaḥ | 

idaṃ kavacamajñātvā kavacānyaṃ paṭhettu yaḥ | 

sarvaṃ tasya vṛthā devi ! trailokyamaṅgalādikam | 

gāyatrīkavacaṃ yasya jihvāyāṃ vidyate sadā | 

tadāmṛtamayī jihvā pavitrā japapūjane | 

idaṃ kavacamajñātvā brahmavidyāṃ japed yadi | 

vyarthaṃ bhavati cārvaṅgi ! tajjapaṃ vanarodanam | 

brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ steyaṃ gurvaṅganāgamaḥ | 

mahānti pātakānyeva smaraṇānnāśamāpnuyuḥ | 

p. 367) tvagasṛṅmāṃsamedo'sthimajjāśukravinirmitam | 

vātapittakaphairyuktaṃ sthūladehaṃ taducyate | 

sūkṣmaṃ jyotirmayaṃ dehaṃ pañcabhūtātmakaṃ viduḥ | 

mahāpadmavanāntaḥsthaṃ sarvāvayavasaṃyutam | 

ādhārādheyasambandhād gāyatrī brāhmaṇaḥ svayam | 

ata-eva paraṃ brahma kathyate cobhayātmakam | 

brāhmaṇasyaiva jīvātmā gāyatrīsahitaṃ vapuḥ | 

ātmano hṛdayāmbhoje pradīpakalikopamam | 

nirdhūmañca yathā jyotistailāgnivarttiyogataḥ | 

tajjyotiḥ paramaṃ brahma sa eva paramaḥ śivaḥ | 

gāyatrīkavacanyāsaṃ mātṛkāsthānasandhiṣu | 

yaḥ kṛtvā brāhmaṇaḥ śreṣṭho hyanyanyāsaṃ samācaret | 

anyanyāsastadā siddho hyanyathāraṇyarodanam | 

gāyatrīnyāsamātreṇa paraṃbrahmamayo dvijaḥ | 

idaṃ kavacamajñātvā brahmacaryyaṃ karoti yaḥ | 

brahmacaryyaṃ bhavedvyarthaṃ gāyatrīkavacaṃ vināṃ | 

kavacasya prasādena brāhmaṇo jvaladagnivat | 

kavacaṃ parameśāni ! sṛṣṭisthitilayātmakam | 

kavacasya prasādena brahmā sṛṣṭiṃ karoti hi | 

sthitiñca kurute viṣṇūrudro'haṃ layakāraṇaḥ | 

anyaddhi kavacaṃ devi ! sṛṣṭisthitilayaṃ vinā | 

idameva brahmamayaṃ sṛṣṭisthitilayātmakam | 

kavacaṃ brāhmaṇo nāma prātarutthāya yaḥ paṭhet | 

gāyatrīñca sakṛt smṛtvā japalakṣaphalaṃ labhet | 

gāyatrīṃ daśadhā japtvā śatalakṣaphalaṃ labhet | 

evaṃ krameṇa gāyatrīṃ śatadhā prajaped yadi | 

śatalakṣaphalaṃ prāpya vihareddevavadbhuvi | 

grahaṇe candrasūryyasya paṭhitvā kavacaṃ dvijaḥ | 

sakṛd yadi japedvidvān gāyatrīṃ paramākṣarīm | 

tatkṣaṇāt sa bhavet siddho brahmasāyujyamāpnuyāt | 

idaṃ kavacamajñātvā gāyatrīṃ prajapettu yaḥ | 

japa eva sa eva syānnistejā na ca siddhidaḥ | 

yaḥ paṭhet kavacaṃ devi ! satataṃ śivasannidhau | 

sannidhau viṣṇudevasya kavacaṃ śaktisannidhau | 

tejaḥpuñjamayo viprastatkṣaṇājjāyate dhruvam ||

ityāgamasandarbhe jñānadarpaṇe gāyatrībrāhmaṇasarvasve devadevīsaṃvāde 
gāyatrīkavacaṃ samāptam ||




p. 368) athāsyāḥ puraścaraṇam || 

śāradāyām || 

lakṣaṃ tattvavidhānena bhikṣāśī vijitendriyaḥ | 

kṣīraudanaṃ tilān dūrvāḥ kṣīradrumasamidvarān | 

pṛthak sahasratritayaṃ juhuyānmantrasiddhaye | 

lakṣaṃ tattvavidhānenetyanena caturviṃśatilakṣaṃ puraścaraṇamuktam | 

vidhānena puraścaraṇoktavidhānena | 

kṣīraudanamiti kṣīramodanam | 

tilān dūrvāśceti dravyacatuṣṭayam | 

kṣīradrumeti aśvatthoḍumbaraplakṣavaṭā iti dravyacatuṣṭayam | 

pṛthak sahasratritayamityanena caturviṃśatisahasrahomaḥ || 

nirvāṇatantre tu || 

vidhivallakṣajāpena puraścaraṇamīritam | 

taddaśāṃśaṃ humet paścāt puraścaraṇasiddhaye | 

homasya tu daśāṃśena tarpaṇaṃ samudīritam | 

tarpaṇasya daśāṃśena cābhiṣekaṃ tataḥ param | 

abhiṣekadaśāṃśena kuryyādbrāhmaṇabhojanam | 

tataḥ siddho bhaveddevi ! trivargaphalasādhakaḥ | 

māhātmyaṃ cāsya mantrasya caturvedena bhāṣitam || 

atha kāmyakarmāṇi śāradāyām | 

tattvasaṃkhyasahasrāṇi mantravijjuhuyāttilaiḥ | 

sarvapāpavinirmukto dīrghamāyuḥ sa vindati | 

āyuṣe sājyahaviṣā kevalenātha sarpiṣā | 

dūrvātrikaistilairmantro juhuyāt trisahasrakam | 

aruṇāñjaistrimadhvaktairjuhuyādayutaṃ tataḥ | 

mahālakṣmīrbhavettasya ṣaṇmāsānnātra saṃśayaḥ | 

brahmaśriye prajuhuyāt prasūnairbrahmavṛkṣajaiḥ | 

bahunā kimihoktena yathāvat sādhusādhitā | 

dvijanmanāmiyaṃ vidyā siddhikāmadughā matā || 

atha tāntrikagāyatrī annadākalpe pañcamapaṭale || 

mocanī sarvapāpānāṃ śoṣaṇī sakalāpadām | 

dāridryadamanī nityaṃ sukhamokṣapradāyinī | 

ekoccāreṇa sakalaṃ brahmahatyādipātakam | 

nāśamāyāti niyataṃ janmāntarasamudbhavam | 

daśabhirdaśajanmotthaṃ śatena śatajanmajam | 

sahasreṇa sahasrotthamitthaṃ pātakanāśinī | 

p. 369) sarvāgamāṇāṃ sāṅgānāṃ sārabhūtā sanātanī | 

citkaleyaṃ samākhyātā sahasreṇa puraskriyā || 

iti puraścaraṇam || 

ṛṣirbrahmāsya gāyatrīcchando devī prakīrttitam | 

devatānnapradā devī caturvargapradāyinī || 

ānapradeti svīyeṣṭadevatopalakṣaṇam || 

māyābījaṃ namaḥ śaktiḥ kīlakaṃ syāt pracodayāt | 

dhyāyet kālatraye devīṃ triguṇāṃ guṇabhedataḥ | 

prātaḥ brāhmī raktavarṇā dvibhujā ca kumārikā | 

kamaṇḍaluṃ tīrthapūrṇamakṣamālāñca bibhratī | 

kṛṣṇājināmbaradharā haṃsārūḍhā śucismitā | 

madhyāhne sā śyāmavarṇā vaiṣṇavī ca caturbhujā | 

śaṅkhacakragadāpadmadhāriṇī garuḍāsanā | 

pīnonnatakucadvandvā vanamālāvibhūṣitā | 

yuvatī ca sadā dhyeyā madhye mārttaṇḍamaṇḍale | 

sāyaṃ sarasvatīrūpā candrārddhakṛtaśekharā | 

arddhāstamitamārttaṇḍe dhyeyā satatayauvanā | 

gāyatrī tu tantrasāre sarvāsāṃ devatānāṃ prāyeṇa draṣṭavyā || 

annadākalpe || 

pūjārthaṃ jalamānīya tīrthaṃ natvā yathāvidhi | 

devīṃ dhyāyan paṭhan stotraṃ yāgamaṇḍapamāviśet || 

matsyasūkte pañcadaśapaṭale ca | 

deśiko vidhivat snātvā kṛtvā pūrvāhṇikīḥ kriyāḥ | 

yāyādalaṅkṛto mantrī yāgārthaṃ yāgamaṇḍapam || 

rudrajāmale pūrvakhaṇḍe dvitīyapaṭale'pi || 

pūjāmaṇḍapamāgatya prakṣālyāṅghrikarau tataḥ | 

astreṇa dvāramabhyukṣya vāmadakṣiṇaśākhayoḥ | 

gaṅgāyai yamunāyai ca nama uccāryya pūjayet | 

ūrddhvadvāre śriyai adhodehalyai ca namastataḥ || 

yāgamaṇḍapamityatra lakṣaṇayā yāgamaṇḍapasamīpamiti labdhamanyathā aṅgaṃ 
saṅkocayannantaḥ praviśeddakṣiṇāṅghriṇā iti na saṅgacchate || 

matsyasūkte || 

ācamya vidhivattatra sūryyārghyantu vidhāya ca | 

paścimadvāramāsādya praviśeddakṣiṇāṅghriṇā | 

dvārānastrāmbunā prokṣya dvārapūjāṃ samācaret | 

ūrddhvādhaḥkramabhāveṇa gaṇeśaṃ vighnanāśanam | 

p. 370) mahālakṣmīṃ mahāmāyāṃ tathā devīṃ sarasvatīm | 

kṣetreśañca tathā gaṅgāṃ yamunāṃ puṣpavāribhiḥ | 

astramantreṇa deveśi ! dehalīñca samarccayet || 

astramantreṇa astrāya phaḍiti mantramuccāryya dvāramabhyukṣyetyarthaḥ || 

śāradāyām || 

ūrddhvoḍumbarake vighnaṃ mahālakṣmīṃ sarasvatīm | 

tato dakṣiṇaśākhāyāṃ vighnaṃ kṣetreśamanyataḥ || 

ūrddhvoḍumbarake dehalyāṃ gṛhadakṣiṇadvāri dehalyāṃ koṇadvaye vighnaṃ sarasvatīṃ 
madhye mahālakṣmīṃ pūjayediti tātparyyam | 

dakṣiṇaśākhāyāṃ vighnam anyataḥ vāmaśākhāyāṃ kṣetreśamarccayedityarthaḥ || 

tayoḥ pārśvagate gaṅgāyamune puṣpavāribhiḥ | 

dehalyāmarccayedastraṃ pratidvāramiti kramāt || 

tayo pārśva iti vighnakṣetreśayoḥ pārśve | 

durgāpūjanamapyuktaṃ rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtena || 

koṇeṣu vighnaṃ durgāñca vāṇīṃ kṣetreśamarccayet || 

eteṣāṃ mantrastu || 

tāraṃ sabindumānādyakṣaraṃ caturthyantaṃ nāma namo'nvitam | 

taduktamuḍḍāmareśvare || 

oṅkāraṃ bindusaṃyuktaṃ nāmadheyādyamakṣaram | 

ṅeyutaṃ nāma sarveṣāṃ mantro devi ! namo'nvitaḥ || 

vaiṣṇavamate dvārapūjā tu rāghavabhaṭṭenoktā yathā || 

vaiṣṇavādiprabhedena dvārapālān samarccayet | 

pratidvāraṃ pārśvayostu dvau dvāvaṣṭāviti kramāt | 

nandaḥ sunandaścaṇḍākhyaḥ pracaṇḍaścaṇḍanāyakaḥ | 

prabalo bhadranāmā ca subhadro vaiṣṇavā matāḥ || 

śaivadvārapālāstu || 

atha nandimahākālau gaṇeśavṛṣabhau punaḥ | 

tato bhṛṅgariṭiḥ skandaḥ pārvatīśaśca saptamaḥ | 

caṇḍeśvaro'ṣṭamaḥ śaivādvārapālāḥ kramādamī | 

gaṇeśasya tu || 

vakratuṇḍaikadaṃṣṭrau ca mahodaragajānanau | 

lambodarākhyaśca kaṭho vighnarājaśca saptamaḥ | 

dhūmrarājo'ṣṭamo jñeyā gāṇapatyā iti kramāt | 

brāhmyādyā mātaraḥ proktāḥ śākteyā dvārapālakāḥ || 

śāradāyām || 

anantaraṃ sādhakendro divyadṛṣṭyāvalokanāt | 

divyānutsārayedvighnānastrādbhiścāntarīkṣagān | 

pārṣṇighātaistribhirbhaumāniti vighnān nivārayet | 

p. 371) kiñcit spṛśan vāmaśākhāṃ dehalīṃ laṅghayettataḥ | 

aṅgaṃ saṅkocayannantaḥ praviśeddakṣiṇāṅghriṇā | 

matsyasūkte'pi || 

avalokyaṃ divyadṛṣṭyā vighnānutsārayettataḥ | 

triḥ kṛtvā ca padāghātaṃ dattvā tālatrayaṃ tataḥ | 

aṅgaṃ saṅkocayannantaḥ praviśeta sādhakottamaḥ || 

athāsanam | 

śāktānandataraṅgiṇīdhṛtagaurījāmale || 

salile yadi kurvīta devatānāṃ prapūjanam | 

tathāpyāsanamāsīno notthitastu tathācaret | 

āsanaṃ kalpayitvā tu manasā pūjayejjale || 

āsanastho japet samyaṅmantrārthagatamānasaḥ || 

sammohanatantre || 

raktāsanopaviṣṭastu lākṣāgurugṛhe sthitaḥ | 

namaḥkalpitarakto vā sādhakaḥ sthirasānasaḥ | 

tūlakambalavastrāṇāṃ siṃhavyāghramṛgājinam | 

kalpayedāsanaṃ dhīmān saubhāgyajñānavarddhanam | 

kauśeyaṃ vātha vā carma tailataulamathāpi vā | 

śarapatraṃ tālapatraṃ kambalaṃ dāravāsanam || 

kṛṣṇājine jñānasiddhirmuktiḥ syādvyāghracarmmaṇi | 

kṛṣṇājine gṛhasthānāṃ nādhikāraḥ kathañcana | 

nādīkṣito viśejjātu kṛṣṇasārājine gṛhī || 

viśedyatirvanasthaśca brahmacārī ca bhikṣukaḥ | 

vastrāsane vyādhināśaḥ kambalaṃ duḥkhamocanam | 

japadhyānatapohāniṃ vastrāsanaṃ karoti hi || 

atra vastraniṣedhaḥ kevalavastraparaḥ anyathā virodhāpatteḥ | 

kuśāsane bhavedāyurmokṣaḥ syādvyāghracarmmaṇi | 

ajine tu bhavet putrī kambale siddhiruttamā | 

śāntike dhavalaḥ proktaḥ sarvārthaṃ citrakambale | 

syāt pauṣṭike tu kauśeyaṃ kambale duḥkhamocanam | 

tripurāpūjane devi ! praśastaṃ raktakambalam | 

naitaddvihasthato dīrghaṃ sārddhahastānna vistṛtam | 

na tryaṅgulāt samucchāyaṃ pūjākarmmaṇi saṃgraham | 

āsanañca tataḥ kuryyānnātinīcaṃ na cocchritam || 

gandharvatantre || 

dharaṇyāṃ duḥkhasambhūtirdaurbhāgyaṃ dārujāsane | 

āmranimbakadambānāmāsanaṃ vaṃśanāśanam | 

vakule kiṃśuke caiva panaseṣu hataśriyaḥ | 

vaṃśeṣṭakā ca dharaṇī tṛṇapallavanirmitam | 

p. 372) varjjayedāsanaṃ mantrī dāridryavyādhiduḥkhadam || 

eteṣāṃ phalāntaramuktam || 

dharaṇyāṃ duḥkhasambhūtirdaurbhāgyaṃ dārujāsane | 

vaṃśāsane daridraḥ syāt pāṣāṇe vyādhipīḍanam || 

tṛṇāsane yaśohāniḥ pallave cittavibhramaḥ  | 

japadhyānatapohānirvastrāsane vidhīyate | 

mṛdvāsane yadā mantrī celājijakuśottare | 

nādīkṣito viśejjātu kṛṣṇājināsane gṛhī | 

viśedyatirvanastho vā brahmacārī ca bhikṣukaḥ | 

ityuktam || 

dārujāsanaṃ viśeṣayati gandharvatantre || 

gāmbhārīnirmmitaṃ śastaṃ nānādārumayaṃ śubham | 

caturviśatyaṅgulaṃ hi dīrghaṃ kāṣṭhāsanaṃ priye ! | 

ṣoḍaśāṅgulavistīrṇamucchrāgre caturaṅgulam | 

kambalam cārmmaṇaṃ śailaṃ mahāmāyāprapūjane | 

praśastamāsanaṃ devi ! kathitaṃ varavarṇini ! || 

muṇḍamālātantra tṛtīyapaṭale || 

vyāghrājinaṃ sarvasiddhyai jñānasiddhyai mṛgājinam | 

vastrāsanaṃ rogaharaṃ vetrajaṃ prītivarddhanam | 

kauśeyaṃ puṣṭidaṃ proktaṃ kambalaṃ sarvasiddhidam | 

śuklaṃ vā yadi vā kṛṣṇaṃ viśeṣādraktakambalam | 

uttarāmnāyāsanantu tatraiva || 

mṛdu komalamāstīrṇaṃ saṃgrāmapatitaṃ hi yat | 

jantuvyāpāditaṃ vāpi mṛtaṃ vānaramāsanam | 

garbhacyutāṃ tvacaṃ vāpi nārīṇāṃ yonijāṃ tvacam | 

sarvasiddhipradaṃ devi ! sarvato'tisamṛddhidam | 

tvacaṃ vā yauvanasthānāṃ kuryyādvīratarāsanam | 

śmaśānakāṣṭhaghaṭitaṃ pīṭhaṃ vā dārujaṃ śubham | 

nādīkṣito viśejjātu kṛṣṇasārājine gṛhī | 

udāsīnavadāsīnaḥ snātakabrahmacāriṇām | 

kuśājināmbareṇāḍhyaṃ caturasraṃ samantataḥ | 

ekahastaṃ dvihastaṃ vā caturasraṃ samācaret | 

karmmaviśeṣe āsanaviśeṣastu samayācāratantre dvitīyapaṭale || 

meṣāsanantu vaśyārthamākṛṣṭau vyāghracarmma ca | 

śāntau mṛgājinaṃ śastaṃ mokṣārthaṃ vyāghracarmma ca | 

gocarmma stambhane devi ! sambhave vājicarmma ca | 

carmmoṣṭrasya tu coccāṭe vidveṣe śvānacarmma ca | 

p. 373) māraṇe māhiṣaṃ carmma karmoddiṣṭaṃ samācaret | 

sarvakāmārthadaṃ devi ! vastrapaṭṭāsanaṃ tathā | 

ūrṇāsanaṃ kambalaṃ vā sarvakarmmasu pūjitam | 

duḥkhadāridryakaraṇaṃ kāṣṭhapāṣāṇajāsanam | 

svarṇādiratnajaṃ devi ! mahāsaubhāgyavarddhanam | 

śṛṇu devi ! viśeṣeṇa uttarāmnāyasya cāsanam | 

yenāśu labhate puṇyaṃ phalaṃ devasya durlabham | 

śavāsanantu subhage ! mṛtamuṇḍāsanaṃ tathā | 

śmaśānamāsanaṃ devi ! bhagasya darśanaṃ tathā | 

bhagadarśāsanañcaiva maithunāsanakaṃ tathā | 

kṛtamaithunakaṃ vāpi vāñchitārthaphalapradam || 

mātsye pañcadaśe || 

rurucarmaṇi satkāmaḥ śrīmokṣau vyāghracarmaṇi | 

śakteḥ śastaṃ kṛṣṇacarma kṛṣṇavyāghrasya bhāvini ! | 

prastāramṛcchile caiva rakte pīte ca vāsasī | 

kambale drutasiddhiḥ syādbhadradārumaye'pi ca | 

tathā | 

yogāsanañca kūrmañca viśet kṣauṇīmanusmaran || 

yogasāre dvitīyaparicchede || 

kambalaṃ komalañcaiva kauśañca dārabaṃ tathā | 

eteṣāmāsanaṃ proktaṃ carmāsanaṃ tathaiva ca | 

lomni caiva yadāsīnastadā sarvaṃ vinaśyati | 

lomadarśanamātreṇa siddhihāniḥ prajāyate | 

kāmyārthaṃ kambalaṃ proktaṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ syādraktakambalam | 

kṛṣṇājine jñānasiddhiḥ śrīmokṣau vyāghracarmaṇi | 

kuśāsane mantrasiddhirnātra kāryyā vicāraṇā || 

samayācāratantre || 

pṛthvīmantrasya ca ṛṣirmerupṛṣṭha udāhṛtaḥ | 

sutalañca tathā chandaḥ kūrmo devo'sya sundari ! | 

āsane viniyogaḥ syādbaddhapadmāsane viśet | 

baddhapadmāsana ityupalakṣaṇam || 

padmasvastikavīrādiṣvekāsanasamāsthitaḥ | 

japārccanādikaṃ kuryyādanyathā niṣphalaṃ bhavet | 

iti rāghavabhaṭṭavacanāt || 

āsanantu yatīnāṃ kūrmākāramanyeṣāṃ catuṣkoṇam | 

yathā rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtam || 

yatīnāmāsanaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ kūrmākārantu kārayet | 

anyeṣāntu catuṣpādaṃ caturasrantu kārayet || 

tathā || 

dānamācamanaṃ homaṃ bhojanaṃ devatārccanam | 

p. 374) prauḍhapādo na kurvīta svādhyāyañcaiva tarpaṇam || 

prauḍhapādalakṣaṇamapi tatraiva | 

āsanārūḍhapādastu jānunorvātha jaṅghayoḥ | 

kṛtāvasaktakoṣastu prauḍhapādaḥ sa ucyate || 

iti rāghavaṭṭadhṛtaprauḍhapādalakṣaṇam || 

gorakṣasaṃhitāyām | 

āsanāni ca tāvanti yāvanto jīvajantavaḥ | 

eteṣāmakhilān bhedān vijānāti maheśvaraḥ | 

caturaśītilakṣāṇāmekaikaṃ samudāhṛtam | 

tathā śivena pīṭhānāṃ ṣoḍaśānāṃ śataṃ kṛtam | 

āsaneṣu samasteṣu dvayametadudāhṛtam | 

ekaṃ siddhāsanaṃ proktaṃ dvitīyaṃ kamalāsanam | 

yonisthānakamaṅghrimūlaghaṭitaṃ kṛtvā dṛḍhaṃ vinyaset meḍhre pādamathaikameva 
hṛdayaṃ kṛtvā samaṃ vigraham | 

sthāṇuḥ saṃyamitendriyo'nyanadṛśā paśyan bhruvorantaram 
etanmokṣakapāṭabhedanakaraṃ siddhāsanaṃ procyate || 

iti siddhāsanam || 1 || 

vāmorūpari dakṣiṇaṃ niyamataḥ saṃsthāpya vāmaṃ tathā dakṣorūpari paścimena 
vidhinā dhṛtvā karābhyāṃ dhṛtam | 

aṅguṣṭhaṃ hṛdaye nidhāya civukaṃ nāsāgramālokayet etadvyādhivikāranāśanakaraṃ 
padmāsanaṃ procyate || 

anyadatrāpi || 

vāmorūpari dakṣiṇaṃ hi caraṇaṃ saṃsthāpya vāmaṃ tathāpyanyorūpari tasya 
bandhanavidhau dhṛtvā karābhyāṃ dhṛḍham | 

aṅguṣṭhau hṛdaye nidhāya civukaṃ nāsāgramālokayedetadvyādhivināśakāri yamināṃ 
padmāsanaṃ procyate || 2 || 

iti padmāsanam || 

ityādyāsanāni yogopayogīnyeva | 

pūjāyāṃ naiteṣāṃ prayojanam || 

matsyasūkte || 

pañcagavyena saṃśodhya maṇḍapaṃ yāgabhūmikam | 

śareṇa prokṣaṇaṃ kuryyānmūlamantreṇa vīkṣaṇam | 

vikirān vikirettatra saptajaptān śarāṇunā | 

dhūpayedantare paścāccandanāgurubhistathā || 

tathā || 

ātmano dakṣiṇe bhāge pūjādravyāṇi sthāpayet | 

savye suvāsitaṃ kumbhaṃ tīrthatoyena pūritam | 

vastrapūtaṃ tataḥ kāryyaṃ rātritoyaṃ vivarjjayet || 

śāradāyām || 

prakṣālanāya karayoḥ patścā pātraṃ niveśayet | 

p. 375) ghṛtaprajvalitān dīpān sthāpayat paritaḥ śubhān || 

pādyārghyācamanamadhuparkācamanapātrāṇyapyadhunaiva sthāpayet || 

devīpurāṇe || 

hemarājatatāmrāṇi mṛcchilāśailajāni ca | 

balidānādepātrāṇi śubharekhotkarāṇi ca | 

arghyanaivedyapūjārthaṃ balidāne prakalpayediti | 

śivarahasye śeṣārddham || 

haimapātrāṇi sarvāṇi vihitāni bhavantu me | 

arghyaṃ dattvā tu raupyeṇa āyūrājyasutāllabhat | 

tāmrapātreṇa saubhāgyaṃ dharmaṃ mṛṇmayasambhave | 

sarvābhāve tu māheyaṃ svahastaghaṭitaṃ yadi | 

māheyaṃ mṛṇmayam || 

āsanaṃ cārghyapātrañca bhagnamāsādayettataḥ | 

sarvatra svarṇakaṃ tāmramanyamātre tato'dhikam | 

pātrāṇāmādaraḥ kāryyaḥ pātrāṇyevottamādaraḥ | 

balihomakriyārthaṃ hi vinā pīṭhantu sidhyati | 

ṣaṭtriṃśadaṅgulaṃ pātramuttamaṃ parikīrttitam | 

madhyamaṃ tattribhāgonaṃ kaniṣṭhaṃ dvādaśāṅgulam || 

caturviṃśāṅgulaṃ madhyaṃ kaniṣṭhaṃ dvādaśāṅgulamiti kvacit pāṭhaḥ | 

vasvaṅgulavihīnantu na pātraṃ kārayedbudha iti pāṭhāntaram || 

sarvābhāve tyajet pātraṃ sravatai yatra vāri tat | 

yatrodaka na sravate tat karmārhamiti kāryyakṣamopalambhakamiti | 

nābhīvivararūpāṇi puṇḍarīkākṛtīni ca | 

śaṅkhaśuktivṛkṣajāni pātrāṇi parikalpayet || 

atha pādyapātralakṣaṇam | 

vaikhānasagranthe || 

pādāvanejanajalagrahaṇaṃ pātramadbhutam | 

lauhajaṃ vā sarojātaṃ haimaṃ rājatameva vā | 

tāmraṃ sacaraṇamapi vāyave na satāmiti || 

siddhāntaśekhare || 

ṣaḍaṅgulapravistāramutsedhaṃ caturaṅgulam | 

oṣṭhamekāṅgulaṃ kuryyānnāsikāṃ caturāṅgulām | 

pṛṣṭhe pādasamāyuktaṃ caturaṅgulamānataḥ | 

pādyapātramiti khyātaṃ sarvadevaprapūjane || 

iti pādyapātralakṣaṇam || 

atha pādyaprakṣepaṇīyapātralakṣaṇam || 

tatraiva || 

aṣṭāṅgulasamutsedhaṃ vistāraṃ ṣoḍaśāṅgulam | 

oṣṭhamekāṅgulaṃ kuryyāccharāvākṛtipātrakam | 

p. 376) pātrayorubhayoḥ kuryyādvṛttakaṃ kaṇake same | 

pṛṣṭhe pādasamāyuktaṃ ṣaḍaṅgulasuvistṛtam | 

pādotsedhaṃ caturmātraṃ pādyādhāraṃ prakīrttitam | 

iti pādyaprakṣepaṇīyapātralakṣaṇam | 

athārghyapātralakṣaṇam || 

siddhāntaśekhare || 

arghyasya lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye paladravyādibhedataḥ | 

sāmānyārghyaṃ tirodhāmyaṃ viśeṣārghyaparāṅmukham | 

arghyapātrāṇi catvāri svarṇadūrvākṣatāni ca || 

durbalaṃ rājataṃ jñeyamadhamāni tataḥ param | 

tāmraśuktiśilādārukarmamṛtpātrajāni ca | 

uttamaṃ palaviṃśatyā madhyamaṃ daśabhiḥ palaiḥ | 

hīnaṃ pañcapalaṃ kuryyādevamācamya pādyayoḥ | 

catuḥparvāṇi tāni syuḥ niḥsṛtānyaṣṭaparvabhiḥ | 

pṛṣṭhenāṅkaśubhāṅkāni kṛtākārāṇi vā punaḥ | 

caturasrāṇi kurvīta catuṣpātrāṇi cārghyake |
