karpūraṃ kuṅkumaṃ dūrvāṃ sidhārthaṃ vilvapatrakam | 

yavadarbhān kuśāṃśceti sāmānyārghyaṃ vinikṣipet | 

tilaprasūnasiddhārthadarbhadūrvāyavākṣatān | 

dravyāṇyetāni saptaiva tirodhāya vinikṣipet | 

tilataṇḍulasiddhārthadūrvāmudgayavānapi | 

parāṅmukhārghyadāne ca dravyaśuddhiṃ prakalpayet || 

ityarghya pātralakṣaṇam || 

athācamanapātralakṣaṇaṃ vaikhānasagranthe || 

ācamya vārigrahaṇapātramuttamalauhajam | 

sarojakarṇikākāraṃ kuryyādadbhutadarśanam || 

kārttasvaramayaṃ sthūlaṃ rājataṃ vātha pāvanam || 

kārttasvaraṃ suvarṇamiti || 

uttamalauhamapi tathā || 

sarvantu taijasaṃ lauhamiti svarasāt rajataṃ tāmraṃ vā || 

siddhāntaśekhare || 

dvādaśāṅgulavistāraṃ kukṣimadhyaṃ savṛttakam | 

ṣaḍaṅgulaṃ taddalāntamoṣṭhaṃ kuryyāttathāṅgulam || 

asya tāraṃ trimātraṃ syāt pārśve nāsāguṇāṅgulam | 

randhraṃ kaniṣṭhakātulyamagre taddviguṇe tvadhaḥ | 

pṛṣṭhe pādaṃ catuḥ kuryyādutsedhaṃ dvyaṅgulaṃ tataḥ | 

saḍaṅgulapravistāraṃ pātramācamanārthakam || 

iti ācamanapātralakṣaṇam || 

atha gandhapātralakṣaṇam || 

p. 377) sidhāntaśekhare || 

arghyavadgandhapātraṃ syāditi | 

atra arghyapātravadityanena sampūrṇārghya pātralakṣaṇātideśaḥ | 

yathā arghyapātraṃ kriyate tathaiva gandhapātraṃ karttavyamityarthaḥ || 

iti gandhapātralakṣaṇam || 

atha dhūpapātralakṣaṇaṃ vaikhānasagranthe || 

dhūpapātraṃ sarojātaṃ suvarṇādivinirmitam | 

pādayorapi tasya syādutsedhaṃ caturaṅgulam | 

anekaśuṣiraṃ tasya pidhānaṃ sadṛśaṃ bhavet | 

śuṣiraṃ chidram || 

pidhānamācchādanam | 

siddhāntaśekhare || 

dhūpapātravidhiṃ vakṣye paladravyādipūrvavat | 

catuḥpañcarasāśvairvai pātravistāramaṅgulaiḥ | 

rasāḥ ṣaṭ aśvāḥ sapta | 

pātrotsedhato dairghyaṃ syādoṣṭhamarddhāntakaṃ matam | 

padmābhaṃ paritaḥ pātraṃ dvyaṅgulo'dhogalo bhavet | 

pādamekāṅgulotsedhaṃ vistṛtaṃ dvyaṅgulaṃ tataḥ | 

ṣaṭparvadīrghanābhaṃ syādunnataṃ dvyaṅgulaṃ matam | 

pūrvakotsedhasaṃyuktaṃ vṛttañca cakracāpavat | 

pidhānaṃ dvyaṅgulotsedhaṃ nānācchidrasamanvitam | 

ekacchidrayutaṃ kuryyāt kalasākāramūrddhvataḥ || 

iti dhūpapātralakṣaṇam || 

vaikhānasagranthe || 

dīpapātraṃ tatā nālamadhye kumudakojjvalam | 

varttyādhāraiḥ śatenāpi yuktamaṣṭābhireva ca | 

trisaptakāṣṭabhiścaiva dvābhyāṃ vā daśabhiśca vā | 

pañcabhirvā yathāśakti kalpayeccihnavittamaḥ | 

siddhāntaśekhare || 

dīpapātravidhiṃ vakṣye paladravyādirarghyavat | 

padena nāgalādyaṃ hi dhūpapātravaducyate | 

ekapatraṃ catuṣpatraṃ ṣaḍdalaṃ vāṣṭapatrakam | 

tatraikapatraṃ madhye syānnimnāśvatthadalākṛti | 

catuṣpatraṃ caturdikṣu madhyamā nimnakarṇikā | 

ṣaḍdale tu smṛtaṃ tadvadupapatrasamanvitam || 

iti dīpapātralakṣaṇam || 

atha dīpamālālakṣaṇam || 

siddhāntaśekhare || 

dārvayastāmrasambhūtā dīpamālā'dhamā matā | 

madhyamā ca tathā śreṣṭhā svarṇā vā raupyajā kramāt | 

yaṣṭyā tu dvāravat kuryyāt liṅgapṛṣṭhe prabhākṛtim | 

dvārābhāmanyadeśe tu dīpamālāṃ suśobhanām | 

caturbhiraṅgulairdīrghaṃ skandhamaṅgulavistṛtam | 

p. 378) evaṃ kṛtvā vibhāgaikaṃ daṇḍamadhye niyojayet | 

skandhāt skandhaṃ samaṃ kuryyādantaraṃ bhūtamātrakam | 

dīpamāleti kathitā sarvadevādipūjane | 

iti dīpamālālakṣaṇam || 

atha dīpādhāralakṣaṇam || 

siddhāntaśekhare || 

hematāmrādisambhūtaṃ vidadhyāduttamādikam | 

dvādaśāṅgulamārabhya dvidvyaṅgulavivṛddhitaḥ | 

aṣṭādaśoditotsedhā dīpādhārasya cocchratā | 

snehādhārasya pāde tūnnāhaṃ dairghyasamaṃ matam | 

pādanāvaśarāvāṃśaḥ snehādhāro'pi tatsamaḥ | 

tadūrddhvaṃ mukulaṃ kuryyāt prādikādisamanvitam | 

nānāparīsamāyuktaṃ nānākumbhāṃśamālikam | 

dīpādhāra iti prokto lauhajān dīpakāṃstu tān | 

nānāvarṇān tathā ramyān caturhastapramāṇakān | 

trihastānekahastān vā dvihastān vā yathābalam | 

tāmrajān rājatīyān vā yugmaistaiḥ snehadhārakaiḥ || 

iti dīpādhāralakṣaṇam || 

vaikhānasagranthe || 

nīrājanakriyāpātrī hemādridravyanirmmitā | 

tritālopetavistīrṇaghṛtamadhyā manoharā || 

sidhāntaśekhare || 

hiraṇyatāratāmrādinirmmitaṃ hyuttamādikam | 

ṣaṭ triṃśatāṅgulaiḥ śreṣṭhaṃ madhyamaṃ triṃśatāṅgulaiḥ | 

caturviṃśatimātraiḥ syādadhamaṃ vistṛtau kramāt | 

agnibhāgaikabhāgena kuryyānmadhye ca karṇikām | 

uttarakāsamutsedhaṃ karṇikāyāḥ pramāṇataḥ | 

pīṭhikaṃ paritaḥ kuryyāt yavayugmena vistṛtam | 

tatsamānonnatānīha parito'ṣṭadalāni ca | 

parito'rddhapramāṇena kāṣṭhānāmaṣṭakaṃ samam | 

oṣṭhamekāṅgulaṃ kuryādvistārāt karṇikonnate | 

samānaṃ pūrṇacandrābhaṃ dīpādhāraśca kumbhavat || 

unnatādicaturmātrairvistṛtaiścāṅguladvayam | 

tatra randhradvayaṃ kuryyāt karṇikāmadhyamaṃ samam || 

iti nīrājanapātralakṣaṇam || 

atha ghaṇṭālakṣaṇam || 

vaikhānasagranthe | 

śabdabrahmamayī ghaṇṭā hastotsedhapramāṇikā | 

brahmāṇḍagolakākārā surādyātmatamā bhavet | 

adhomukhatālamānavistārotsedhanaṃ tataḥ | 

p. 379) nānāvidhāścatasro vā ghaṇṭāḥ pratidiśaḥ kṛtā | 

cakrāṅkā paṅkajāṅkā vā sā ghaṇṭā parikīrttitā | 

evaṃ vidhā bhavedghaṇṭā yadvā cittānusārataḥ | 

sidhāntaśekhare || 

pañcāṅgulonnatā ghaṇṭā tadvadvistārasaṃyutā | 

ghaṇṭāyāstu parīṇāho vistārārddhena kīrttitaḥ | 

aṅgulastu bhaveddaṇḍo dairghye vṛttasamaṃ matam | 

daṇḍāgre vṛṣabhaṃ śūlaṃ nalinaṃ vāpi kārayet | 

śuddhakāṃsyena karttavyaṃ ghaṇṭālakṣaṇamīritam || 

iti ghaṇṭālakṣaṇam ||

atha naivedyapātralakṣaṇam || 

vīramitrodaye || 

naivedyapātraṃ vakṣyāmi keśavāya mahātmane | 

hairaṇyaṃ rājataṃ tāmraṃ kāṃsyaṃ mṛṇmayameva vā | 

pālāsaṃ madhyamaṃ plākṣapātraṃ viṣṇoratipriyam | 

havirdravyapramāṇāni pātrāṇi parikalpayediti || 

siddhāntaśekhare || 

suvarṇarūpyatāmrebhyaḥ sthālikāṃ hyuttamādikām | 

śuddhakāṃsyena vā kuryyāddevanaivedyapātrake | 

ekapātraṃ samārabhya cāsaptāttu tataḥ kramāt | 

kuryyānnaivedyapātrāṇi hīnanaivedyakādiṣu | 

pātraṃ śataṃ palaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ tadarddhaṃ madhyamaṃ matam | 

tadarddhaṃ madhyamaṃ jñeyam pātrasaṃkhyeti kīrttitā | 

sthālikā navadhā kāryyā bhāvāḥ ṣaṭtriṃśadaṅgulam | 

aṅgulārddhamitairmūlairaṣṭadhāṅgulakaiḥ kramāt | 

iti vistāramākhyātaṃ sthālikānavake kramāt | 

uttamatrayamagre syānmadhyame madhyamatrayam | 

punastrayamadhastāt syāt sthālikānāṃ yathākramam || 

iti naivedyapātralakṣaṇam || 

atha pānīyapātralakṣaṇam || 

vaiśvānarasaṃhitāyām || 

pānīyapātraṃ sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ tāmrameva vā | 

vṛkṣāyatasuvṛttaṃ vā bhavedāyatameva vā | 

prasthamānapramāṇāmbhaḥ purayogyāntaraṃ śubham || 

iti pānīyapātralakṣaṇam || 

ala tāmbūlapātralakṣaṇam || 

vaikhānasagranthe || 

kalpayedrāgalatikā dalapatrasamanvitā | 

aratnimānavistīrṇaṃ kārayedvā yathābalam | 

iti tāmbūlapātralakṣaṇam ||

iti prāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ tṛtīye'rthakāṇḍe gandhādipātrasthāpanāntarūpapallavakathanaṃ 
nāma caturthaḥ paricchedaḥ |



p. 380) śāradāyām || 

kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā vāmadakṣiṇapārśvayoḥ | 

natvā guruṃ gaṇeśānaṃ bhūtaśuddhimathācaret || 

karaśuddhiṃ samāpādya kuryyāttālatrayaṃ tataḥ | 

ūrddhvorddhamastramantreṇa digbandhamapi deśikaḥ | 

tena saṃjvalitaṃ tejo rakṣāṃ kuryyāt samantataḥ | 

caturthapaṭale || 

suṣumnāvartmanātmānaṃ paramātmani yojayet | 

yogayuktena vidhinā cinmantreṇa samāhitaḥ | 

ātmānaṃ hṛdayakamalasthitaṃ suṣumnāvartmanā kuṇḍalinyā saha yogayuktena vidhinā 
cinmantreṇa haṃsa iti mantreṇa paramātmani yojayedityanvayaḥ | 

suṣumnā vartma yasyāḥ sā tayā | 

hṛdayakamalastha ātmā tu | 

hṛdambuje brahmarandhrasambhūte jñānanālake | 

tārāgramagre jīvastu cintanīyo manīṣibhiriti rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtavacanena jñātavyaḥ | 

yogayuktena vidhinā ityasyāyamarthaḥ | 

gurūpadiṣṭamārgeṇa haṅkāreṇa kuṇḍalinīmutthāpyaitāṃ hṛdayakamalagatāṃ vibhāvya 
tato jīvaṃ mukhe gṛhītvā sahasrāragatāṃ vibhāvayet | 

tena vyo haṃsamantreṇa dvādaśārṇoṣite pare | 

iti vacanenaikavākyatayā cinmantro haṃsa iti | 

śāradāyām || 

kāreṇa sarvabhūtānāṃ tattvānyapi vicintayet | 

vījabhāvena līnāni vyutkramāt paramātmani | 

sarvabhūtānāṃ kāraṇe paramātmani vījabhāvena līnāni tattvāni cintayedityarthaḥ || 

vyutkramāditi sṛṣṭikramāpekṣayā | 

api śabdādvarṇānapi | 

taduktaṃ piṅgalāmate || 

saṅkalpyaivaṃ tato nyāsasthānādvarṇāṃśca saṃharet | 

pratilomena kṣalayo lakārasya hakārake | 

halayaśca sakāre'tha salayaśca yakārake | 

krameṇaivamaparyyantaṃ layamutpādya yatnataḥ | 

akāraṃ brahmarandhre ca sahasrāre niyojayediti || 

p. 381)  śāradāyām || 

tataḥ saṃśoṣayeddehaṃ vāyuvījena vāyunā | 

vahnivījena tenaiva sandahet sakalāṃ tanum | 

vāyuvījena yakāreṇa vāyunā tadutthavāyunā | 

vahnivījena repheṇa tenaivāgnivījotthavahninā sakalāmiti kalaṃ pāpaṃ 
pāpapuruṣasahitāṃ tanuṃ nidahedityarthaḥ | 

saviśeṣaṇavidhitayā na prakṛtaśarīranāśaḥ | 

tatkāraṇantu saviśeṣaṇe vidhiniṣedhau viśeṣaṇamupasaṃkrāmataḥ sati viśeṣye bādhe iti 
nyāyaḥ | 

bādhastvanvayānupapattiḥ | 

tātparyyaviṣayībhūte arthe anvayānupapattau | 

yatra tu viśiṣṭe tātparyyaṃ tatra viśiṣṭamapyupagacchato vidhiniṣedhau na viśeṣyamātram 
| 

tathāca bhartṛhariḥ || 

nīlaṃ paṭaṃ vidhehīti loke'pi dvayamīkṣate | 

kvacinnīlaguṇasyaiva viśiṣṭasya vidhiḥ kvacit || 

ata-eva guptasādhanatantre ṣaṣṭhapaṭale || 

bhūtaśuddhau mahādeva ! yadi dehantu nāśayet | 

kutra sthāne bhavet sṛṣṭiramṛtaṃ kutra sañcayediti devīsandehapraśnasyottaram || 

mahādeva uvāca || 

vāmakukṣau sthitaṃ pāpapuruṣaṃ kajjalaprabham | 

tasya saṃharaṇārthāya mahatī prakaṭīkṛtā | 

liṅgadeho maheśāni ! tasya deho na saṃśayaḥ | 

pāpadehaṃ bhaveddagdhaṃ svadehaṃ naiva nāśayet || 

iti śivavacanam || 

pāpapuruṣadhyānantu rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtam || 

brahmahatyāśiraskantu svarṇasteyabhujadvayam | 

surāpānahṛdā yuktaṃ gurutalpakaṭidvayam | 

tatsaṃsargipadadvandvamaṅgapratyaṅgapātakam | 

upapātakaromāṇaṃ raktaśmaśruvilocanam | 

khaḍgacarmadharaṃ pāpamaṅguṣṭhaparimāṇakam | 

adhomukhaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ vāmakukṣau vicintayet || 

śāradāyām || 

viśleṣayaṃstadā doṣānamṛtenāmṛtāmbhasā | 

āpādya plāvayeddehamāpādatalamastakam | 

doṣasvarūpapāpapuruṣabhasmaviśleṣo'pi vāyuvījenaiva | 

tathāca  gaṇeśavimarṣiṇyām || 

tadbhasmakūṭamakhilaṃ vāyuvījotthavāyunā | 

vikīryyāmṛtavījena svadehaṃ plāvayettataḥ | 

amṛteneti amṛtavījena vamityanena | 

p. 382) amṛtāmbhasā tadutthenāmṛtenāpādatalamastakaṃ 
dehamāpādyāplāvayedityanvayaḥ || 

śāradāyām || 

ātmalīnānāni tattvāni svasthāna prāpayettadā | 

ātmānaṃ hṛdayāmbhojamānayet paramātmanaḥ | 

manunā haṃsadevasya kuryānnyāsādikaṃ tataḥ | 

paramātmanaḥ sakāśāt haṃsadevasya manunā ātmānaṃ 
hṛdayāmbhojamānayedityanvayaḥ || 

haṃsasya vījasya devaḥ paramātmā upāsyatvāt tanmantreṇa so'hamityanena | 

taduktaṃ vaśiṣṭhasahitāyām || 

 so'ha mantreṇa tā mantrī nādānte siddhibhāvitām | 

dhyātvaivaṃ brahmarandhrācca hṛdi jīvaṃ nyasediti || 

tatra gurūpadeśataḥ prakāro likhyate | 

omadyetyādi śrīdevapūjādyadhikārasiddhaye bhūtaśuddhyādyahaṃ kariṣye iti saṅkalpya 
mūlādhārādutthitāṃ vidyutsahasraprabhābhāsvarāṃ visatanturūpāṃ suṣumnāmārgeṇa 
hṛdayakamalagatā kuṇḍalinīṃ vibhāvya hṛdayakamalājjīvaṃ pradīpakalikākāraṃ mukhe 
gṛhītvā dvādaśārṇāmbujaṃ sahasradalagataṃ vibhāvya tatra jīvātmānaṃ 
haṃsamantreṇa paramātmani yojayet | 

tataḥ pādādijānuparyyantaṃ caturasraṃ pṛthivīmaṇḍalaṃ vibhāvya tatra 
pādagamanakriyāgantavyagandhaghrāṇapṛthivībrahmanivṛtti samānavāyu saṃsmṛtya oṃ hlīṃ 
brahmaṇe pṛthivyadhipataye nivṛttikalātmane huṃ phaṭ svāhā ityamuṃmantramuccāryya 
tān kuṇḍalinīdvārā apsu pravilāpayedityapsthāne saṃharet | 

jānvādinābhiparyyantaṃ śuklamarddhacandrākāraṃ jalamaṇḍalaṃ vibhāvya tatra 
hastādānadātavyarasarasanājalaviṣṇuṃ pratiṣṭhedanilasya oṃ hlīṃ viṣṇave jalādhipataye 
pratiṣṭhākalātmane huṃ phaṭ svāhā ityamuṃ mantramuccāryya tān sarvān 
kuṇḍalinīdvārā agnau pravilāpayāmītyagnisthāne saṃharet | 

nābhyādihṛdayaparyyantaṃ trikoṇaṃ vahnimaṇḍalaṃ raktavarṇaṃ vibhāvya tatra 
pāyuvisarjanīyarūpacakṣustejorudravidyāvyānānilaṃ saṃsmṛtya oṃ huṃ rudrāya 
tejo'dhipataye vidyākalātmane huṃ phaṭ svāhā ityamuṃ mantramuccāryya tān sarvān 
kuṇḍalinīdvārā vāyau pravilāpayāmīti vāyusthāne saṃharet | 

p. 383) tato hṛdayādribhrūparyyantaṃ kṛṣṇavarttulaṃ ṣaḍvindulāñchitaṃ vāyumaṇḍalaṃ 
vibhāvya tatrāpasthānatadviṣayasparśaspraṣṭavyavāyavīyaśāntyapānān saṃsmṛtya oṃ 
hauṃ īśānāya vāyvadhipataye śāntikalātmane huṃ phaṭ svāhā ityamuṃ 
mantramuccāryya tān sarvān kuṇḍalinīdvārā ākāśe pravilāpayāmītyākāśasthāne 
saṃharet | 

tato bhrūmadhyādibrahmarandhrāntaparyyantaṃ śuklavarttulamākāśamaṇḍalaṃ vibhāvya 
tatra vāgvadanavaktavyaśabdaśrotrākāśasadāśivaśāntyatītaprāṇān saṃsmṛtya oṃ 
hauṃ sadāśivāya ākāśādhipataye śāntyatītakalātmane huṃ phaṭ svāhā ityamuṃ 
mantramuccāryya tān sarvān kulakuṇḍalinīdvārā ahaṅkāre pravilāpayāmītyahaṅkāre 
pravilāpya mahattattvaṃ mātṛkāsaṃjñakaśabdabrahmasvarūpāyāṃ 
hṛllekhānubhūtāyāṃ prakṛtau pravilāpayāmīti pravilāpya tāṃ tathāvidhāṃ 
nityaśuddhasvabhāve paramabrahmaṇi pravilāpayāmīti pravilāpayet || 

taduktaṃ mantratantraprakāśe || 

gandhādighrāṇasaṃyuktāṃ pṛthivīmapsu saṃharet | 

rasādijihvayā sārddhaṃ jalamagnau vilāpayet | 

rūpādicakṣuṣā sārddhamagniṃ vāyau vilāpayet | 

samīramīśvare vidvān sparśāditvaksamanvitam | 

ahaṅkāre haredvyoma saśabdaṃ tanmahatyapi | 

mahacca sarvaśaktānāmavyakte kāraṇe pare | 

saccidānandarūpaṃ yad vaiṣṇavaṃ paramaṃ padam || 

pṛthivyādikramāt sarvaṃ tatra līnaṃ vicintayet | 

tataḥ śarīrasyāntaryyāmī ṛṣiḥ satyaṃ devatā prakṛtipuruṣaṃ chandaḥ śodhanena 
viniyogaḥ | 

ityuktvā vāmakukṣiṣu muktarūpaṃ pāpapuruṣaṃ vicintya yamiti vāyuvījasya kiṣkindha 
ṛṣirvāyurdetatā jagatīcchandaḥ śarīrapāpapuruṣaśodhane viniyogaḥ | 

ityuktvā nābhimūle bindudvayalāñchitamaṇḍale yaṃ vicintayet | 

p. 384) tadvāyubījaṃ dhūmraṃ cañcaladhvajasaṃyuktaṃ dhūṃ dhūṃ śabdayutaṃ 
sarvaśoṣaṇaṃ haṃsadaivatamiti dhyātvā pūrakaprayogeṇa ṣoḍaśavāraṃ vījamuccāryya 
tadvījotthavāyunā svaśarīrasthaṃ pāpapuruṣaṃ śodhitaṃ vibhāvayet | 

tato ramityagnivījasya kaśyapa ṛṣiragnirdevatā triṣṭupcchandaḥ taddāhe viniyogaḥ | 

ityuktvā hṛdaye raktaṃ trikoṇaṃ vahnimaṇḍalaṃ vidyākalāyutaṃ rudradaivatamiti dhyātvā 
prayogeṇa catuḥṣaṣṭivāraṃ vījamāvarttya tadvījotthāgninā tadbhasmībhūtaṃ vibhāvayet | 

tato recakeṇa dvātriṃśanmātrayā pūrvoktarūpaṃ vāyuvījamāvarttya pāpapuruṣabhasma 
recayet | 

tato vamiti varuṇavījasya hiraṇyagarbha ṛṣirhaṃso devatā triṣṭupchandaḥ plāvane viniyogaḥ 
| 

ityuktvā | 

vāruṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ mūrdhni śubhraṃ candrārddhasannibham | 

sitapaṅkajayugmābhayakaraṃ syādvaruṇadaivatam || 

iti dhyātvā tadvījotthasrutāṃmṛtena taccharīrabhasma piṇḍībhūtaṃ vibhāvayet | 

tato lamiti pṛthivīvījasya brahma-ṛṣirindro devatā gāyatrīcchandaḥ kaṭhinīkaraṇe viniyogaḥ | 

ityuktvā | 

ādhāramaṇḍale pṛthvīmaṇḍalaṃ vajralāñchitam | 

catuṣkoṇañca kaṭhinaṃ pītavarṇendradaivatam | 

laṃvījena samāyuktaṃ dhyāyan manasi pūrvavat || 

iti dhyātvā tadvījotthakāṭhinyena tanuṃ dṛḍhāṃ bhāvayet | 

tato hamityākāśavījasya brahma ṛṣirākāśaṃ devatā paṃkticchandaḥ vyūhane viniyoga 
ityuktvā | 

ākāśamaṇḍalaṃ vṛttaṃ dvādaśānte hyanujjvalam | 

śāntyatītākalāyuktaṃ vicintyākāśadaivatam | 

iti dhyātvā tadutthenākāśenākāśaṃ vibhāvayet | 

tadvat hamiti vījenāvāyvīkaraṇaṃ bhavediti | 

evaṃ svaśarīraṃ vicintya paramātmanaḥ sakāśāt sṛṣṭikrameṇa tattvāni svasvasthānaṃ 
prāpayediti || 

tataḥ paramātmanaḥ sakāśāt so'haṃ mantreṇa jīvaṃ hṛtpadmamānayediti saṃkṣepaḥ | 

iyañca bhūtaśuddhirāvaśyakī || 

pañcaśuddhivihīnena kṛtā pūjābhicāravat | 

viparītaphalaṃ dadyādabhaktyā pūjanaṃ yathā |

p. 385) nir-ṛtirvidhihīnānāṃ phalaṃ hanti hi karmmaṇām | 

niśācarādhipatyañca kurute śaṅkarājñayā || 

iti śaivāgamokteḥ | 

anyatrāpi | 

dehakāraṇabhūtānāṃ bhūtānāṃ yadviśodhanam | 

avayavaḥ brahmasaṃyogād bhūtaśuddhiriyaṃ matā | 

bhūtaśuddhiṃ vinā karmma japahomārccanādikam | 

bhavettu niṣphalaṃ sarvaṃ prakāreṇāpyanuṣṭhitam || 

iti matsyasūkte pañcadaśapaṭale || 

natvā guruṃ gaṇeśānaṃ bhūtaśuddhiṃ samācaret | 

ubhābhyāmapi pāṇibhyāṃ tarjjanyaṅguṣṭhayogataḥ | 

gṛhītvā yaṃ kṣamaṃ devi ! yamityāghrāya nikṣipet || 

mahānirvāṇatantre pañcamollāse ca || 

svāṅke nidhāya ca karāvuttānau sādhakottamaḥ | 

mano niveśya mūle ca huṅkāre śaivakuṇḍalīm | 

utthāpya haṃsamantreṇa pṛthivyā sahitāntu tām | 

svādhiṣṭhānaṃ samānīya tatastattve niyojayet | 

ghrāṇādighrāṇasaṃyuktāṃ pṛthivīmapsu saṃharet | 

rasādi jihvayā sārddhaṃ jalamagnau vilāpayet | 

rūpādi cakṣuṣā sārddhamagniṃ vāyau vilāpya ca | 

sparśādi tvagyutaṃ vāyumākāśe vipralāpayet | 

ahaṅkāre taredvyoma saśabdaṃ tanmahatyapi | 

mahattattvañca prakṛtau tāṃ brahmaṇi vilāpayet | 

evaṃ vilāpya matimān vāmakukṣau vicintayet | 

puruṣaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇañca raktaśmaśruvilocanam | 

khaḍgacarmmadharaṃ kruddhamaṅguṣṭhaparimāṇakam | 

sarvapāpasvarūpañca sarvadādhomukhasthitam | 

tatastu vāmanāsāyāṃ yaṃ bījaṃ dhūmravarṇakam | 

sañcintya pūrayettena vāyuṃ ṣoḍaśamātrayā | 

tena pāpātmakaṃ dehaṃ śodhayet sādhakāgraṇīḥ | 

nābhau raṃ raktavarṇañca dhyātvā tajjātavahninā | 

catuḥṣaṣṭyā kumbhakena dahet pāpavatīṃ tanuṃ | 

lalāṭe varuṇaṃ bījaṃ śuklavarṇaṃ vicintya ca | 

dvātriṃśatā recakena plāvayedamṛtāmbhasā | 

āpādaśīrṣaparyyantamāplāvya tadanantaram | 

utpannaṃ bhāvayeddehaṃ navīnaṃ devatāmayam | 

pṛthvībījaṃ pītavarṇaṃ mūlādhāre vicintayan | 

tena divyāvalokena dṛḍhīkuryyānnijāṃ tanum | 

p. 386) hṛdaye hastamādāya āṃ hrīṃ kroṃ haṃ sa uccaran | 

so'haṃ mantreṇa taddehe devyāḥ prāṇān pratiṣṭhayet | 

bhūtaśuddhiṃ vidhāyetthaṃ devībhāvaparāyaṇaḥ || 

nīlatantre caturthapaṭale'pi || 

bhūtaśuddhiṃ tato vakṣye śṛṇuṣvāvahitānaghe ! | 

apānamūrddhvagaṃ kṛtvā divyatattvaiḥ prapūryya ca | 

haṃsamantreṇa cādhārācchaktiṃ kuṇḍalinīṃ parām | 

kramāccakrāṇi nirbhidya jīvātmānaṃ tato'mbujam | 

sahasrāraṃ tato nītvā pare brahmaṇi yojayet | 

indriyāṇi daśekañca mahattattvāni tadguṇān | 

śuddhaṃ karoti cittāni tattvāni prakṛtervapuḥ | 

caturviṃśatitattvāni vījabhāvena yojayet | 

tataḥ śūnye śarīre ca vāmabhāge vicintayet | 

nirlepaṃ nirguṇaṃ śuddhamātmānaṃ tāriṇīmayam | 

antarīkṣe tato dhyāyedāḥ kārādraktapaṅkajam | 

bhūyastasyopari dhyāyet ṭāṅkārecchetapaṅkajam | 

punastasyopari dhyāyed huṅkāraṃ nīlasannibham | 

tato huṃṅkāravīje tu karttṛkā vījabhūṣitam | 

karttuḥ parigatāṃ dhyāyedātmānaṃ tāriṇīmayam | 

tathā | 

vaśyādau raktavarṇaṃ tamākṛṣṭau pītarūpiṇam | 

māraṇoccāṭanairdhūmraṃ kṛṣṇam tattadapekṣayā | 

evambhūtaṃ svamātmānaṃ dhyāyecca tāriṇīmayam || 

viśvasāratastre dvitīyapaṭale || 

yamiti vāyuvījena ṣoḍaśena tu pūrayet | 

catuḥ ṣaṣṭyā ca tenaiva pāpadehaṃ praśodhayet | 

tena vījena vidhinā piṅgalāyāñca recayet | 

dvātriṃśanmātrayā bhasma śvāsamārgeṇa vai śanaiḥ | 

evaṃ vāratrayaṃ kṛtvā bhūtānāṃ śodhanaṃ smṛtam | 

pūrayet pratilomena vahnivījena deśikaḥ | 

ṣoḍaśena catuḥṣaṣṭyā kumbhayedrecayettataḥ | 

dvātriṃśanmātrayā caiva dahanaṃ parikīrttitam | 

caturddhā ca prajapyātha tadvarṇaṃ prajapet kramāt | 

śoṣaṇaṃ dahanañcaiva plvāvanaṃ parikīrttitam | 

kiskindhaḥ kaśyapaścaiva hiraṇyagarbhastataḥ param | 

vījānāṃ ṛṣayaḥ proktāḥ śṛṇuṣva kamalekṣaṇe ! | 

prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ kuryyāt krameṇa deśikottamaḥ | 

p. 387) sahasrāre mahāpadme cintayediṣṭadevatām | 

yathā dhyānaṃ yathoktena gurumārgeṇa deśikaḥ | 

tadā bhūtāni tajjñāni saguṇāni vidhānataḥ | 

sakalāśaktiyuktāni varṇamātrakrameṇa ca |

pratilomena saṃhṛtya satattvadhātusaṃyutaiḥ |

mūlādhāraṃ sahasrāraṃ ṣaṭcakrakramayogataḥ |

vyutkrameṇa vicintyātha sahasrāraṃ tato'vadhi |

oṃ mantre cālayedbījaṃ ṣaṭcakrakramayogataḥ |

aikyaṃ vibhāvya tatsthāne mantramekaṃ samabhyaset |

so'haṃ mantraṃ samuccāryya sūkṣmabrahma vicintayet |

tatkṣaṇānnirmalaṃ dehaṃ nirdvandva niṣparigraham |

advaitaṃ cintayettatra manasā vahniyogataḥ |

akārādikṣakārāntavarṇamālākrameṇa ca |

cintayet śvāsarodhena pūrakumbhakarecakaiḥ |

evaṃ vicintyatān varṇān tataścandramanuṃ smaret |

ṭāntaṃ bindusamāyuktamarddhacandravibhūṣitam |

caturddhā prajapenmantraṃ manasā vāyunā sudhīḥ |

indrabījaṃ japeddevi ! tattvabījaṃ tataḥ param |

dūrīkṛtya vidhānena dhārayā sudhayā punaḥ |

tataḥ sañcintayeddevi ! pūrvoktaṃ mātṛkāmayam |

sakalānādasaṃyuktaṃ svaśaktisahitaṃ tataḥ |

pañcāśadvarṇasaṃyuktaṃ mahābhūtasamanvitam |

pañcabhūtātmakaṃ dehamanulomena cintayet |

antaryyāmī ṛṣistasya prāṇāyāmasya devatā |

vakṣaḥsthale dakṣahastaṃ dattvā sādhakasattamaḥ |

pūrvoktaprāṇamanunā ṛṣiśchandaḥsamanvitam |

haṃsamantreṇa taṃ prāṇamāvāhanamudāhṛtam |

sadhātūni satattvāni ihāgatya ciraṃ sukham |

tiṣṭhantu vahnijāyāntaṃ mantramenaṃ samuddharet |

akārādikṣakārāntaṃ pañcāśadvarṇasaṃyutam |

vicintya manasā dehaṃ tadā devo bhavennaraḥ |

prāṇāyāmaṃ trirāvṛtya balaṃ prāṇeṣu nikṣipet |

akārādikṣakārāntaṃ pūrakumbhakarecakaiḥ |

pratilomānulomena vicintya prāṇamāvahan |

tatkṣaṇādvirajo mantrī bhavet sākṣāt sadāśivaḥ |

nṛsiṃhenaiva mantreṇa dehe bījaṃ vinikṣipet |

p. 388) mūlādhāraṃ tataḥ prāpya jīvātmānaṃ vicintayet |

kuṇḍalīrūpasambhūtā vidyudagnisamaprabhā |

nirmmalā nirvikārasthā sarvasiddhisamanvitā |

tadante vinyaseddehe mātṛkāṃ varṇarūpiṇīm |

mantreṇānena tāṃ dhyātvā vinyaset sakalāṃ tanum ||

atha prāṇāyāmaḥ || 

dakṣiṇāmūrttitantre saptamapaṭale ||

mūlamantreṇa deveśi ! vāmenāpūryya codaram |

kumbhakena trirāvṛttyā dakṣiṇena ca recayet |

kaniṣṭhānāmikāṅguṣṭhairyannāsāpuṭadhāraṇam |

prāṇāyāmaḥ sa vijñeyastarjjanīmadhyame vinā ||

prāṇāyāme viśeṣaḥ paścādvakṣyate ||

tato dīkṣāpaddhatyuktaṃ tantrasāradhṛtatattatkalpoktañca nyāsādikaṃ kuryyāt ||

atha pūjādhāranirūpaṇam | 

mātṛkābhedatantre dvādaśapaṭale ||

śālagrāme maṇau yantre pratimāyāṃ ghaṭe jale |

pustikāyāñca gaṅgāyāṃ śivaliṅge prasūnake |

ādhārabhede phalaviśeṣastatraiva |

śālagrāme śataguṇaṃ maṇau tadvat phalaṃ labhet |

yantre lakṣaguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ mūrttau lakṣaṃ sulocane ! |

ghaṭe caikaguṇa puṇyaṃ jale caikaguṇaṃ priye ! |

pustikāyāṃ sahasrantu gaṅgāyāstatsamaṃ phalam |

śivaliṅge hyanantaṃ hi vinā pārthivaliṅgakam |

puṣpayantre maheśāni ! pūjanāt sarvasiddhibhāk |

śālagrāmādinikaṭe yantrakaraṇaniṣedho'pi |

śālagrāme ca pūjāyāṃ na likhed yantramuttamam |

maṇau sthite maheśāni ! na likhedyantramuttamam |

pratimāyāśca purato ghaṭaṃ saṃsthāpya yatnataḥ |

parivārān yajettatra ghaṭe tu parameśvari ! |

yantrādhiṣṭhātṛdevāṃśca ghaṭavaktre prapūjayet |

samastadevatārūpaṃ ghaṭañca paricintayet |

suradrumasvarūpo hi ghaṭo'yaṃ parameśvari ! ||

pratimānikaṭe yantrakaraṇaniṣedho'pi tatraiva |

janmasthānaṃ mahāyantraṃ yadi kuryyāttu sādhakaḥ |

tatra mūrttiṃ na kuryyāttu yadi yantrañca kārayet |

yadi kuryyāttu mohena yajedvāradvayaṃ priye ! |

dviguṇaṃ pūjanaṃ tatra dviguṇaṃ balidānakam |

dviguṇaṃ prajapenmantraṃ dviguṇaṃ homamācaret |

p. 389) anyathā viphalā pūjā viphalaṃ balidānakam |

sarvaṃ hi viphalaṃ yasmāttasmād yantraṃ na kārayet |

guptasādhanatantre aṣṭamapaṭale | 

sāmānye ca jale devi ! pūjā doṣādiśāntaye | 

tathā |

na kuryyāt pārthive liṅge devīpūjādikāḥ kriyā |

pārthive pūjanāddevi ! siddhihāniḥ prajāyate |

yadi daivānmaheśāni ! mṛttikāskhalanaṃ bhavet |

tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi narake pūyaśoṇite |

kumbhīpāke mahāghore pitṛbhiḥ saha pacyate |

ata eva maheśāni ! pārthive na hi pūjayet |

sphāṭikādīn samānīya liṅgaṃ nirmāya yatnataḥ |

tatra liṅge pūjanāddhi sarvasiddhiyuto bhavet ||

tantrāntare ||

kālikāṃ tāriṇīñcaiva tripurāṃ bhuvaneśvarīm |

yo'rccayet pārthive liṅge sa yāti paramāṃ gatim |

tāriṇyāḥ kālikāyāśca sundaryyāḥ parameśvari ! |

pārthive pūjanāddevi ! caturvargaphalaṃ labhet |

evañca guptasādhanoktavacanaṃ kālikādiviśeṣetaraparam samānyaviśeṣanyāyāt |

yadyapi devatāyā nirākāratvena dhyānādikamasambhavaṃ tathāpi sākāratayā 
dhyānaṃ sarvasamañjasaṃ tasyāḥ sākāratvakāraṇantūktam muṇḍamālātantre 
saptamapaṭale ||

śiva uvāca ||

nirguṇā prakṛtiḥ satyamahameva ca nirguṇaḥ |

yadaiva saguṇā tvaṃ hi saguṇo'haṃ sadāśivaḥ |

satyaṃ hi saguṇā devī satyaṃ hi nirguṇaḥ śivaḥ |

upāsakānāṃ siddhyarthaṃ saguṇā saguṇo mataḥ ||

mahānirvāṇatantre pañcamollāse ||

dhyānantu dvividhaṃ proktaṃ svarūpārūpabhedataḥ |

arūpaṃ tava yaddhyānamavāṅmanasagocaram |

avyaktaṃ sarvato vyāptamidamitthaṃ vivarjjitam |

agamyaṃ yogibhirgamyaṃ kṛtsnairbahusamādhibhiḥ |

manaso dhāraṇārthāya śīghraṃ svābhīṣṭasiddhaye |

sūkṣmadhyānaṃ purodhāya sthūladhyānaṃ vadāmi te |

trayodaśollāse || 

devyuvāca || 

mahādeveśādiśaktermahākālyā mahādyuteḥ |

sūkṣmātisūkṣmabhūtāyāḥ kathaṃ rūpanirūpaṇam ||

rūpaṃ prakṛtikāryyāṇāṃ sātu sākṣāt parātparā |

p. 390) etanme saṃśayaṃ deva ! viśeṣācchettumarhasi ||

iti devīpraśnānantaraṃ śrīsadāśiva uvāca ||

upāsakānāṃ kāryyāya puraiva kathitaṃ priye ! |

guṇakriyānusāreṇa rūpaṃ devyāḥ prakalpitam ||

ityuktam ||

śvetapītādiko varṇo yathā kṛṣṇe vilīyate | 

praviśanti tathā kālyāṃ sarvabhūtāni śailaje ! |

atha tasyāḥ kālaśakternirguṇāyā nirākṛteḥ |

hitāyāḥ prāptamūrttestu varṇaḥ kṛṣṇo nirūpitaḥ |

nityāyāḥ kālarūpāyā arūpāyāḥ śivātmanaḥ |

amṛtatvāllalāṭe'syāḥ śaśicihnaṃ nirūpitam |

śaśisūryyāgnibhirnetrairakhilaṃ kālikā jagat |

sampaśyati yatastasmāt kalpitaṃ nayanatrayam |

grasanāt sarvasattvānāṃ kāladaṇḍena carvaṇāt |

tadraktavāsorūpeṇa bhāsitaṃ sakalaṃ jagat |

samaye samaye jīvarakṣaṇaṃ vipadaḥ śive ! |

preraṇaṃ svasvakāryyeṣu varañcābhayamīritam |

rajojanitaviśvāni viṣṭabhya paritiṣṭhati |

ato hi kathitaṃ bhadre ! raktapadmāsanasthitām ||

kulārṇave pañcamakhaṇḍe ṣaṣṭhollāse'pi ||

cinmayasyāprameyasya niṣkalasyāśarīriṇaḥ |

sādhakānāṃ hitārthāya brahmaṇo rūpakalpanā |

liṅgasthaṇḍilavahnyambu yantrakuḍyapaṭeṣu ca |

maṇḍale phalake mūrdhni hṛdi vā daśa kīrttitāḥ |

eṣu sthāneṣu deveśi ! yajanti paramāṃ śivām |

arūpāṃ rūpiṇīṃ kṛtvā karmakāṇḍaratā narāḥ |

gavāṃ sarvāṅgajakṣīraṃ sravet stanamukhād yathā |

tathā sarvagato devaḥ pratimādiṣu rājate |

ābhirūpyācca vimbasya pūjāyāśca viśeṣataḥ |

sādhakasya ca viśvāsāddevatāsannidhirbhavet |

nāradapañcarātre dvitīyarātre ca ||

nārada uvāca ||

bhaktijñānañca bhaktānāṃ yogajñānañca yoginām |

keṣāṃ vartma praśastañca tanmāṃ kathitumarhasi ||

śrīmahādeva uvāca ||

dhyāyanti yoginaḥ sarve jyotīrūpaṃ sanātanam |

nirguṇasya śarīrañca na manyante ca yoginaḥ |

p. 391) śarīraṃ praktanaṃ sarvaṃ nirguṇaṃ prakṛteḥ param |

guṇena sañjātadeho nirguṇasya kuto bhavet |

iti sarvaṃ yogaśāstraṃ yogavidbhiḥ prakīrttitam |

vaiṣṇavāstaṃ na manyante kumārādyā vayaṃ dvija ! |

vadanti vaiṣṇavāḥ sarve tejastejasvināṃ vinā |

kṛṣṇo nityaśarīrī ca tasya tejo hi varttate |

tejo'bhyantara evāhuḥ kṛṣṇamūrttiḥ sanātanī |

dhyāyante yoginaḥ sarve tattejo bhaktipūrvakam |

supakvabhaktyā kāmena sa yogī vaiṣṇavī bhavet |

tejo'bhyantararūpañca dhyāyante yoginaḥ sadā |

dāsānāñca kuto dāsyaṃ vinā dehena nārada ! |

vaiṣṇavānāṃ mataṃ śastaṃ sarvebhyo'pi ca nārada ! |

na vaiṣṇavāt paro jñānī brahmāṇḍeṣu ca brahmaṇām ||

evaṃ devatāyāḥ sākāratve siddhe svasvakalpoktaṃ tantrasārādyuktaṃ devatādhyānaṃ 
kṛtvāḥ mānasaiḥ sampūjya śaṅkhasthāpanādyāvaraṇapujāntaṃ tantrasāroktaṃ karmma 
kuryyāt |

mānasapūjāntu agre upapādayiṣye |

samprati prāṇapratiṣṭhāyāṃ viśeṣo likhyate ||

śāradātilake trayoviṃśatipaṭale ||

prāṇapratiṣṭhāmantrasya vidhānamabhidhīyate |

yena prāgīritā mantrāḥ prāṇavanto bhavanti te |

yadyapi mantrāṇāṃ prāṇapratiṣṭhā nāsti teṣāṃ svata eva samarthatvāt tathāpi 
yantrādau likhitānāṃ prāṇapratiṣṭhā kriyate taddvāreṇa prāṇavanta ityuktiḥ |

prāṇapratiṣṭhayā samastayantravihitayantraprayogaputtalyādi sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitaprāṇaṃ kriyate 
ityarthaḥ ||

pāśāṅkuśapuṭā śaktirvāṇībinduvibhūṣitaḥ | 

yādyāḥ sapta kalāvanto vyoma sārddhendusaṃyutam |

prathamaṃ pāśabījaṃ tataḥ śaktibījaṃ tato'ṅkuśabījaṃ vāṇīyaḥ bindubhūṣita ityanena 
yaṃ yādyā iti uddhṛtayakārānuvādena saptamantu tadyamiti bhinnavījam |

pūrvaṃ pṛthaguddhārastu saptānāmapi sabindutākhyāpanāya |

tathā ca rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtam ||

pāśāṅkuśāntaritaḥ śaktimanoḥ parastāduccāryya yadi vasuvarṇaguṇaṃ sahaṃsamiti 
vyoma hakāraḥ sārddhendusaṃyutaṃ haṃ | 

kvacittu sanendusaṃyutamiti pāṭhastadā homiti |

p. 392) tadante haṃsamantraḥ syāt tato'muṣya padaṃ vadet |

prāṇā iti vadet paścādiha prāṇāstataḥ param |

amuṣya jīva ihataḥ sthito'muṣya padaṃ vadet |

sarvendriyāṇyamuṣyānte vāṅmanaścakṣurantataḥ |

śrotraghrāṇapade prāṇā ihāgatya sukhaṃ ciram |

tiṣṭhantvagnibadhūrante prāṇamantro'yamīritaḥ |

pratyamuṣya padaṃ pūrvaṃ pāśādīni prayojayet |

ṣaḍadhikaṣaṣṭyakṣaro'yaṃ mantraḥ |

prayogeṣu samākhyātaḥ prāṇamantro manīṣibhiḥ |

kevalametatsādhanārthaṃ japavyatirekeṇa sarvatrānyatra prayoga eva |

tenātmani yatra puttalyādāvapi prāṇapratiṣṭhāsamaye pratyamuṣya padaṃ pāśādīni 
saṃyojya amuṣya padasthāne ṣaṣṭhyantaṃ sādhyanāma saṃyojya mantramuccarediti 
jñeyam ||

brahmaviṣṇuśivāḥ proktā munayastantravedibhiḥ |

uktamṛgyajuṣāṃsāmacchandaśchandoviśāradaiḥ |

caitanyarūpāḥ prāṇā me devatāśaktirīritā |

kavargeṇa viyatpūrve bhūte hṛdayamīritam |

śivaśca vargasandhādyairīritaṃ tadanantaram |

jñānendriye'ṣṭavargādyaistacchikhā parikīrttitā |

karmendriyaistavargādyaiḥ kavacaṃ parikīrttitam |

vacanādyaiḥ pavargādyairvilocanamudīritam |

buddhyādyairyādisaṃyuktairastrasya nyāsamīritam |

ātmane'ntāḥ samākhyātā aṅgamantrāḥ sajātibhiḥ ||

prayogo yathā ||

ṅaṃ kaṃ khaṃ ghaṃ gaṃ ākāśavāyuvahnisalilabhūmyātmane hṛdayāya namaḥ |

ñaṃ caṃ chaṃ jhaṃ jaṃ śabdasparśarūparasagandhātmane śirase svāhā |

ṇaṃ ṭaṃ ṭhaṃ ḍhaṃ ḍaṃ śrotratvakcakṣurjihvāghrāṇātmane śikhāyai vaṣaṭ |

naṃ taṃ thaṃ dhaṃ daṃ vākpāṇipādapāyūpasthātmane kavacāya huṃ |

maṃ paṃ phaṃ bhaṃ baṃ vacanādānaviharaṇavisargānandātmane netratrayāya vauṣaṭ 
|

haṃ yaṃ raṃ vaṃ laṃ kṣaṃ śaṃ ṣaṃ laṃ saṃ 
buddhimano'haṅkāracittajñānātmane astrāya phaṭ ||

nābheścaraṇaparyyantaṃ pāśavījaṃ pravinyaset |

hṛdayānnābhiparyyantaṃ śaktivījaṃ tataḥ param |

mūrddhvādihṛdayaṃ yāvadaṅkuśaṃ vinyasettataḥ |

p. 393) hṛdaye dhātuṣu nyasyedatidīptaṃ yathākramam |

prāṇe jīve tato nyasyeddhaṃsavarṇadvayaṃ pṛthak ||

prāṇajīvayorapi hṛdaya eva nyāsaḥ | 

pṛthagiti pratyekam ||

kuryyādvyāpakametena samastena vidhānavit || 

samastena mūlenetyarthaḥ ||

tato vicintayeddevīṃ jīvabhūtāṃ jaganmayīm |

raktābdhipotāruṇapadmasaṃsthāṃ pāśāṅkuśāvikṣuśarāsavāṇān |

śūlaṃ kapālaṃ dadhatīṃ karābjai raktāṃ trinetrāṃ praṇamāmi devīm |

evaṃ dhyātvā jagaddhātrīṃ lakṣamekaṃ japenmanum |

juhuyāttaddaśāṃśena carubhirghṛtasaṃyutaiḥ |

sapāśāḍhyaśaktipīṭhe vidhinānena pūjayet |

arccayet ṣaṭsu koṇeṣu brahmāṇaṃ viṣṇumīśvaram |

vaṇīṃ lakṣmīmumāṃ paścāt ṣaḍaṅgāni prapūjayet |

pūjākramastu ||

āgneyavāruṇeśeṣu brahmaviṣṇvīśā aindranair-ṛtavāyavyeṣu vāṇīlakṣmyumāḥ pūjyāḥ 
|

pramāṇantu prāguktam || 

daleṣu mātaraḥ pūjyāstadvāhye lokanāyakāḥ |

evaṃ sampūjayeddevīṃ sugandhikusumādibhiḥ |

iti saṃsādhito mantraḥ ṣaṭkarmmaphalado bhavet |

sthāpayenmanunānena prāṇān sarvatra deśikaḥ |

bījānte'muṣyaśabdānāmādau dūtīḥ prayojayet ||

sarvatra sarvakarmmasu puttalyādau ca prāṇapratiṣṭhāsthāpanaprakāro'nena darśitaḥ |

vījānte pāśādihaṃsamantrānte |

amuṣyaśabdānāmādāviti sādhyanāmna ādau |

taduktaṃ rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtena | 

gatvā baddhvā pāśavījena sādhyaṃ śaktyā gṛhṇan śaktivījena bhūyaḥ |

prāṇānākṛṣyāṅkuśenātha yādīn dūtīścoktvā sādhyanāmātha mantrī ||

mṛtavaivasvatā bhūyo jīvahā prāṇahā tataḥ |

ākṛṣyāgrahaṇā paścāt pramadā visphuliṅginī |

kṣetrajñā pratihā hyetāḥ prāṇadūtyo nava smṛtāḥ |

pāśena baddhaceṣṭasya śaktyā svīkṛtacetasaḥ |

aṅkuśenāhṛtasyādbhiḥ sārddhasyāśu samāharet |

dvādaśāṅgulamānena kṛtvā sādhyasya puttalīm |

tasyāṃ prāṇātmakaṃ mantraṃ sakīṭaṃ hṛdaye nyaset |

niśīthasamaye sādhye supte'sya hṛdayāmbuje |

p. 394) daleṣu vāyuvahnīndravaruṇānāmataḥ param |

īśarākṣasaśītāṃśuyamānāṃ karṇikāntare |

yādīn haṃsasamāyuktān bhṛṅgākārānanusmaret |

śivo vindusamudbhūtaṃ tantusambaddhavigrahān |

evamātmahṛdambhoje bhṛṅgīrūpā savartmanā |

ekaikāṃ sādhyahaṃpadmāṃ bhṛṅgamekaikamānayet |

puttalyāṃ sthāpayenmantrī sacitte vā vidhānavit ||

atra prathamavat dvitīyādīnāmapi sādhyanāmnāmekaikasyādau pāśādihaṃsamantrāntāni 
bījānyuccāryya sambuddhyantaṃ prathamadūtīnāma evaṃ caturṣu sthāneṣu prathamaṃ 
prayujyate |

evaṃ dūtyantareṣvapi | 

navakṛttvo mantrāvṛttiḥ |

mantro'yamitthamudito grahasaṃkhyayaiveti rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtavacanāt |

atraikaikasyāmāvṛttau ekaikabhṛṅgānayanamiti jñeyam |

prathamadūtīnāmaprayoge prathamabhṛṅgānayanaṃ dvitīyadūtīnāmaprayoge 
dvitīyabhṛṅgānayanamiti navāntamayameva kramaḥ |

tantucchedaṃ prakurvīta vahnibījena saṃyutaḥ |

ākṛṣṭvā sādhyahṛdbhṛṅgāṃstataḥ saṃjñambhramedbhavā |

evamekādaśāvṛttīḥ kuryyāt sarveṣu karmasu |

vaśyākarṣaṇayoryādīn varuṇān saṃsmaret sudhīḥ |

mohavidveṣayordhūmrān kṛṣṇān māraṇakarmaṇi |

pītān saṃstambhane dhyāyet prāṇākarṣaṇakarmmavit |

ākṛṣṭān sādhyahṛtprāṇān sthāpayedātmano hṛdi |

krūrakarmasu puttalyāṃ teṣāṃ sthāpanamīritam |

prāṇān sādhyasya maṇḍūkānātmānantu bhujaṅgamān |

saṃsmarettatra nipuṇaḥ sadā krūreṣu karmasu |

vāyvagniśakravaruṇeśvararākṣasendupreteśapatralikhitairatha yādivarṇaiḥ |

bindvāntikaiḥ kṣagata haṃsasametasādhyaṃ prāṇātmayantramatha varṇavṛtaṃ dharāstham 
|

itthaṃ prayogakuśalo manunānena mantravit |

vaśayet sakalān devān kiṃ punaḥ pārthivān janān ||

āvāhanādimudrā tu tantrasārakāreṇa dhṛtā na tvatra prayojanaṃ tāsāmiti |

athāsanaṃ mātṛkābhedatantre pañcamapaṭale |

caturaṅgulavistāramityadi || 

atha pādyam || 

p. 395) prapañcasāre ṣaṣṭhapaṭale ||

pādyaṃ śyāmākadūrvābjaviṣṇukrāntābhirucyate iti pādyam ||

athārghyam || 

gandhapuṣpākṣatayavakuśāgratilasarṣapāḥ |

dūrvā ceti kramādarghya dravyāṣṭakamudīritam |

śākteyārghye viśeṣo yoginītantre saptamapaṭale ||

vakradhānyodbhavaṃ yacca sūkṣmadhānyodbhvaṃ tathā |

tattu taṇḍulamakṣuṇaṃ saptāṣṭanavasaṃkhyayā |

dūrvāṅkurasametañca bhagavatyai nivedayet |

aṣṭamyāṃ vā navamyāṃ vā so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 

ityarghyam ||

athācamanīyam || 

jātīlavaṅgakakkolairnirmitācamanīyakam ||

ityācamanīyam | 

atha madhuparkkam || 

madhuparkañca sakṣaudraṃ dadhi proktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ || 

śāradāyām ||

ājyaṃ dadhi madhūnmiśrametaduktaṃ manīṣibhiriti madhuparkam ||

atha punarācanamīyam || 

śuddhābhiradbhirvihitaṃ punarācamanīyakam ||

iti punarācamanīyam | 

atha gandhaḥ | 

gandhaśabdavyutpattistu kulārṇave pañcamakhaṇḍe saptadaśollāse ||

gambhīrāpāradaurbhāgyakleśanāśanakāraṇāt |

dharmajñānapradānācca gandha ityabhidhīyate || 

prapañcasāre ||

candanāgurukarpūrapaṅkaṃ gandhamihocyate || 

taddānaphalantu yoginītantre ||

gandhānulepanaṃ dattvā jyotiṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet |

kuṅkumena vilipyāryyāṃ gosahasraphalaṃ labhet |

candanāgurukarpūraiḥ śuklapuṣpaiḥ sakuṅkumaiḥ |

viliptāṃ pūjayeddūrgāṃ vahniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet |

atha gandhāṣṭakam || 

merutantre caturthaprakāśe || 

gandhāṣṭakaṃ tasya tasya devasyātha prakīrttyate |

candanāgurukarpūrarocanākuṅkumaṃ madam |

raktacandanajāvāsagāṇapatyamudāhṛtamiti gaṇeśagandhāṣṭakam ||

hrīveraṃ candanaṃ kuṣṭhamaguruṃ candanaṃ murā |

sevyakañca jaṭāmāṃsī vaiṣṇavaṃ tadudāhṛtamiti viṣṇugandhāṣṭakam ||

candanāgurukarpūrajalakuṅkumarocanā |

jaṭāmāṃsī kapiyutaṃ śāktaṃ gandhāṣṭakaṃ viduriti śaktigandhāṣṭakam ||

jalakāśmīrakuṣṭhaistu raktacandanacandanaiḥ |

p. 396) uśīrāgurukarpūraiḥ sauraṃ gandhāṣṭakaṃ viduriti sūryyagandhāṣṭakam ||

gorocanā ca kuṣṭhañca karpūrāgurukuṅkumaiḥ |

candanena sadāsyañca varadena ca patrakam || 

śāradāyām ||

candanāgurukarpūratamālajalakuṅkumam |

kuśītakuṣṭhasaṃyuktaṃ śivaṃ gandhāṣṭakaṃ smṛtamiti śaivagandhāṣṭakam || 

atha puṣpam ||

puṣpaśabdavyutpattistu kulārṇave || 

puṇyasaṃvarddhanāccāpi pāpaughaparihārataḥ |

puṣkalārthapradānāñca puṣpamityabhidhīyate || 

prapañcasāre ||

tulasyau paṅkaje jātyau ketakyau karavīrakau |

śastāni daśa puṣpāṇi tathā raktotpalāni ca |

utpalāni ca nīlāni kahlārakumudāni ca |

viśvasāratantre dvitīyapaṭale śeṣacaraṇe bahūni kusumāni ceti pāṭhaḥ ||

mālatīṃ kundamandāraṃ nandyāvarttyādikāni ca |

palāśapāṭalāpārthaparantyāvartmakāni ca |

campakāni sa nāgāni raktamandārakāṇi ca |

aśokodbhavavilvākhyakarṇikārodbhavāni ca ||

viśvasāratantre śaktiviṣaye viśeṣa ukto yathā ||

atha devyā mate vakṣye puṣpāṇi śṛṇu pārvati ! |

ityuktvā tulasyau paṅkaje ityādyuktam || 

tadanantaram |

etānyanyāni tantre'smin puṣpāṇi santi vai priye ! |

nānādeśodbhavāni syuḥ sarvakālodbhavāni ca |

kulāni caiva puṣpāṇi dadyāddevyai viśeṣataḥ |

atha puṣpaṃ pravakṣyāmi karmayoge maheśvari ! |

śṛṇuṣva parayā bhaktyā yathoktaṃ brahmaṇā purā |

kamale karavīre dve kusumbhe tulasīdvayam |

jātyaśoke ketake dve kumārīcampakotpalam |

kundamandārapunnāgapāṭalānāgacampakam |

āragvadhaṃ karṇikāraṃ pāvantī navamallikā |

saugandhikaṃ sakoraṇḍaṃ palāśāśokasarjakāḥ |

sindhuvāra apāmārgaṃ vāpulīkañca kāmajam |

vyāghracelaṃ damanakaṃ maruvakañca tataḥ param |

lavaṅgaṃ jalakarpūraṃ tagarañca javā tathā |

śivapuṣpaṃ droṇapuṣpaṃ kāmarājaṃ suketakam |

anyāni vanapuṣpāṇi jalajasthalajāni ca |

girijāni deśajāni nānāpuṣpāṇyataḥ param || 

p. 397) śāradāyāmapi ||

kamale karavīre dve kumade tulasīdvayam |

jātīdvayaṃ ketake dve kahlāraṃ campakotpale |

kundamandārapunnāgapāṭalā nāgacampakam |

āragbadhaṃ karṇikāraṃ pāvantī navamallikā |

saugandhikaṃ sakoraṇṭaṃ palāśāśokamallikāḥ |

dhustūraṃ sarjakaṃ vilvamarjunaṃ munipuṣpakam |

anyānyapi sugandhīni patrapuṣpāṇi deśikaiḥ |

upadiṣṭāni pūjāyāmādadīta vicakṣaṇaḥ ||

yoginītantre tṛtīyabhāge saptamapaṭale'pi ||

śṛṇu devi ! pravakṣyāmi puṣpādhyāyaṃ samāsataḥ |

ṛtukālodbhavaiḥ puṣpairmallikājātikuṅkumaiḥ |

sitaraktaistathā puṣpairnīlapadmaiśca pāṇḍaraiḥ |

kiṃśukaistagaraiścaiva javākanakacampakaiḥ |

vakulaiścaiva mandāraiḥ kundapuṣpaiḥ kuruṇḍakaiḥ |

dhustūrakādiyuktaiśca bandhūkāgastyasambhavaiḥ |

madanaiḥ sindhuvāraiśca dūrvāṅkurarasukomalaiḥ |

mañjarībhiḥ kuśānāñca vilvapatraiḥ sukomalaiḥ || 

navamapaṭale'pi ||

tulasī dve mālatī tamālāmalakī tathā |

punnāgaṃ munipuṣpañca mallikāñca nivedayet |

karavīrasya kusumairye'rccayanti janārdanam |

darśanāttasya deveśi ! narakāgniḥ praṇaśyati ||

atha karavīrādimāhātmyam | 

puraścaraṇarasollāse daśamapaṭale ||

karavīraṃ javā devi ! svayaṃ kālī na cānyathā |

tārā ca aparā caiva svayaṃ tripurasundarī | 

tathā |

karavīrajavāmūle tulasyā naganandini ! |

yadi prāṇāṃstyajeddevi ! māhātmyaṃ tasya sundari ! |

vaktrakoṭiśatenāpi jihvākoṭiśatena ca |

varṇituṃ tasya māhātmyaṃ na śaknomi kadācana || 

iti karavīrajavāmūle prāṇatyāgaphalam |

śuklakṛṣṇaṃ tathā pītaṃ haritaṃ lohitaṃ tathā |

karavīraṃ maheśāni ! javāpuṣpaṃ tathaiva ca |

svayaṃ kālī ! mahāmāyā svayaṃ svayaṃ tripurasundarī |

anādaro na karttavyaḥ kṛtvā ca narakaṃ vrajet || 

daśamapaṭale ||

kṛṣṇāparājitā sākṣādbhadrakālī na saṃśayaḥ ||

karavīrañca bhuvanā droṇaṃ bhuvanasundarī |

p. 398) javā sākṣādbhagavatī sarvavidyāsvarūpiṇī |

ye sādhakā jaganmātararccayanti śivapriyām |

etaiśca kusumaiścaṇḍi ! sa śivo nātra saṃśayaḥ |

kiṃ japaiḥ kiṃ tapobhirvā kiṃ dānairvā kimadhvaraiḥ |

yenārccitā jagaddhātrī droṇakṛṣṇājavādibhiḥ |

rājasūyāśvamedhādyairvājapeyāgnihotrakaiḥ |

phalaṃ yajjāyate caṇḍi ! tatsarvaṃ kusumārccanāt |

javā droṇaṃ tathā kṛṣṇā mālūraṃ karavīrakam |

sākṣādbrahmasvarūpañca mahādevyai nivedayet |

śvetacandanasaṃyuktaṃ raktacandanalepitam |

yo dadyādbhaktibhāvena sa viśveśo na saṃśayaḥ ||

atha sakāmasya puṣpaviśeṣastatraiva || 

karavīrasya māghyasya sahasrāṇi dadāti yaḥ |

sa kāmān prāpya cābhīṣṭān devīloke mahīyate |

tatraikakaravīreṇa padmānāṃ dve sahasrake |

mahāghore mahotpāte mahāpadi ca saṅkaṭe |

mahāduḥkhe mahāroge mahāśoke mahābhaye |

pūjayet kālikāṃ tārāṃ bhuvanāṃ ṣoḍaśīṃ śivām |

bālāṃ chinnāñca vagalāṃ dhūmāṃ bhīmāṃ karālinīm |

kamalāmannapūrṇāñca durgāṃ duḥkhavināśinīm |

sarvavidyā javādroṇakaravīrairmanoharaiḥ |

mālūrapatraiḥ kṛṣṇābhiḥ kṛṣṇāṃ sampūjya bhūtale |

sādhakendro maheśāni ! bhavenmukto na saṃśayaḥ || 

muṇḍamālāyāṃ daśamapaṭale ||

javāpuṣpairdroṇapuṣpaiḥ karavīrairmanoharaiḥ |

kṛṣṇāparājitāpuṣpairabjaiśca munipuṣpakaiḥ |

pūjayet parayā bhaktyā caṇḍikāṃ parameśvarīm ||

yoginītantramatsyasūktayoḥ ||

ye'rccayanti janādhyakṣaṃ karavīraiḥ sitāsitaiḥ |

caturyugāṇi deveśi ! prīto bhavati mādhavaḥ |

sitāsitairiti śvetakṛṣṇamityādyuktavacanenaikavākyatayā asitaśabdena 
raktapītādikamavagamyate na tu kṛṣṇamātram | 

yoginīhṛdaye ||

vakapuṣpaṃ jātipuṣpaṃ tathā rudrajaṭasya ca |

vājapeyasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti nānyathā |

sarveṣāmeva puṣpāṇāṃ pravaraṃ nīlamutpalam |

nīlotpalasahasreṇa yastu mālāṃ prayacchati |

p. 399) durgāyai vidhivaddevi ! tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu |

varṣakoṭisahasrāṇi varṣakoṭiśatāni ca |

devyā anucaro bhūtvā rudraloke mahīyate | 

muṇḍamālāyāmi ||

lakṣāṇāṃ mahiṣairmeṣairajairdānairmakhaiḥ śubhaiḥ |

pūjitā sā jagaddhātrī yadyeṣā kusumārccitā |

māhātmyañcaiva kṛṣṇāyāḥ kṛṣṇā jānāti kṛtsnaśaḥ |

tadarddhañcāpyahaṃ devi ! tadarddhaṃ śrīpatiḥ sadā |

tadarddhamabjajanmā vai tadarddhaṃ vedasādhakaḥ |

anya puṣpasya māhātmyaṃ saṃkṣepādvacmi śaṅkari ! |

pṛthivyā maṇḍale svargo vaikuṇṭhe kālikāpure |

javādikaravīraiśca dalaiḥ kiṃ kiṃ phalaṃ labhet |

na jānāti jagaddhātri ! ko veda pārvatīṃ vinā |

karavīraiḥ śvetaraktai raktacandanamiśritaiḥ |

pūjayet kṣmātale yastu sa viśveśo bhaveddhruvam |

kṛṣṇāparājitāpuṣpairyastu devīṃ prapūjayet |

so'śvamedhasahasrāṇāṃ phalaṃ prāpya śivāṃ vrajet |

mahāvipattau yo dadyājjavāṃ kṛṣṇāparājitām |

droṇaṃ vā karavīraṃ vā sa gacchet kālikāpuram || 

iti mahāvipattinivṛttikāraṇajavādidānenāpi kālipuragamanam || 

tatraiva ||

kiñca pādyaiḥ kiñca vādyairnaivedyaiḥ kiñca pūjanaiḥ |

madhudānairmadhuparkaiḥ kumbhakaiḥ kiñca recakaiḥ |

pūrakaiḥ kiñca vā dhyānaiḥ prāṇāyāmaiśca kiñca vā |

kiṃ japaiḥ kiṃ tapobhirvā matsyairmāṃsaiśca pañcamaiḥ |

kimanyamantraiḥ kiṃ tantraiḥ kiṃ yantraiḥ kiñca sādhanaiḥ |

kiṃ vedairāsavaiḥ kiṃ vā śmaśānairmantrasādhanaiḥ |

kimadhvarairmantrapūtairmantrārthairmantrajīvanaiḥ |

kiṃ yonimudrayā kiṃ vā tīrthaiḥ kiṃ brahmasādhanaiḥ |

kiṃ  mātṛkānyāsagaṇaiḥ kiṃ kaṭaiḥ kiṃ ghaṭaiḥ paṭaiḥ |

kiṃ kākacañcubhiḥ ṣoḍhānyāsaiḥ kiṃ dharmmasādhanaiḥ |

yenārccitā mahādevī karavīrairjavādibhiḥ |

kṛṣṇāparājitāpuṣpaiḥ karavīrairmanoharaiḥ |

droṇaistu ketakīpuṣpairjavāmālūrapatrakaiḥ |

pūjitā yairbhagavatī teṣāṃ kiṃ karmmasādhanaiḥ || 

nityātantre ekādaśapaṭale ||

na tulasyā yajet kālīṃ nākṣatairviṣṇumarccayet |

p. 400) aparājitāyā dānena sākṣāttuṣṭā bhavecchivā |

kālikāyāśca tārāyāḥ karavīramatipriyam |

javāpuṣpaṃ maheśāni ! dadyānna dhārayet kvacit |

raktotpalena deveśi ! kālikāṃ pūjayet sakṛt |

śatavarṣasahasrāṇāṃ pūjāyāḥ phalamāpnuyāt || 

tathā |

dūrvāpi garbhasaṃyuktā devītuṣṭikarī bhavet |

puraścaraṇarasollāse || 

mañjarīṃ sahakārasya keśavāya nivedayet |

jambutindukayoreva tathā vai keśavasyaṃ ca |

śephālikāñca tagaraṃ bandhūkañca nivedayet |

rudrajaṭāṃ śirīṣañca dāḍimaṃ kāñcanaṃ tathā |

nīlakaṇṭhaṃ mayūrañca yonyākārañca varjjayet |

puṣpadāne kāmyaphalaṃ nityātantre tatraiva ||

īśvara uvāca ||

brahmahatyādipāpānāṃ prāyaścittaṃ sureśvari ! | 

raktapuṣpairmahādevi ! cakrarājaṃ prapūjayet |

kulācārakrameṇaiva karpūrakṣodamaṇḍitam |

mahāpātakakoṭīśca janmāntarakṛtā api |

māsamātreṇa hanyante satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |

lakṣmīstasya bhavedgehe susthirā vīravandite ! |

javāpuṣpairmaheśāni ! pūrvavadyadi pūjayet |

māsamātreṇa naśyanti saptajanmakṛtānyapi |

brahmahatyādipāpāni dhanavān jāyate kaviḥ |

pūrvavat ketakīpuṣpaiḥ patrairvā yadi pūjayet |

māsamātreṇa deveśi ! upapātakakoṭayaḥ || 

naśyantīti pūrvoktenānvayaḥ |

labhate rājasaubhāgyaṃ sādhako nātra saṃśayaḥ |

śatapatrairmaheśāni ! pūrvavat pūjayecchivām |

māsamātreṇa deviśi ! sarvapāpaṃ vināśayet |

campakaiḥ pūjayeddevīṃ pūrvavanmāsamātrakam |

nihatya parameśāni ! pātakaṃ śatajanmajam |

saubhāgyaṃ labhate mantrī triṣu lokeṣu pārvati ! |

śvetapadmairmaheśāni ! māsamātraṃ prapūjayet |

triṃśajjanmakṛtān pāpānnāśayennātra saṃśayaḥ |

bandhūkaiḥ pūrvavaddevi ! māsamātraṃ prapūjayet |

nihatya sarvapāpāni trailokyaṃ vaśamānayet |

mālatīmallikājātīkundaiḥ śvetotpalaiḥ saha |

sumiśraiḥ pūrvavaddevi ! māsamātraṃ prapūjayet |

p. 401) brahmahatyādipāpāni śatajanmakṛtānyapi |

nāśayet parameśāni ! muktistasya kare sthitā |

raktotpalajavāvahnibandhūkāgastyakaiḥ śivām |

pūrvavat parameśāni ! māsamātraṃ prapūjayet |

pātakaṃ nāśayitvāsau mama tulyo bhavennaraḥ |

nāgakeśarakahlāravakulaiḥ sindhuvārakaiḥ |

pāṭalaiḥ pūjayeddevi ! śrīpīṭhāntarnivāsinīm |

pūrvavat pūjayeddevi ! māsamātraṃ prasannadhīḥ |

sahasrajanmajaṃ pāpaṃ nāśayennātra saṃśayaḥ |

saubhāgyamatulaṃ tasya bhaveddevīprasādataḥ || 

yoginītantre ||

notsṛjya dadyāt puṣpāṇi vanasthāni kadācana |

na śaknuvanti vai devāḥ samākarṣitumudyatāḥ |

ekaikaṃ kusumaṃ yakṣā rakṣanti daśa vai yataḥ |

tathā yakṣāṅganāḥ pañca sarvataḥ kusumāvṛtāḥ |

tasmādāhṛtya kusumaṃ yajeddevān pitṝnapi || 

iti vṛkṣasthapuṣpadānaniṣedhaḥ || 

matsyasūkte ||

snātvā madhyāhnasamaye yaśchindyāt kusumaṃ naraḥ |

tatpuṣpairarcane devi ! raurave pratipacyate ||

madhyāhna ityupādānāt prātaḥsnātasya puṣpāharaṇe na doṣaḥ || 

atha puṣpamālā || 

viśvasāratantre ||

nīlotpalasahasreṇa yastu mālāṃ prayacchati |

durgāyai vidhivaddevi ! tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu |

varṣakoṭisahasrāṇi varṣakoṭiśatāni ca |

devyā anucaro bhūtvā rudraloke mahīyate || 

yoginītantre navamapaṭale ||

mālatīmālayā viṣṇurarccito yena kārtike |

pāpākṣarakṛtamālā nāśitā tasya viṣṇunā |

paṭṭe saure pramārjjitā iti matsyasūkte caturdaśapaṭale śeṣacaraṇe pāṭhaḥ || 

tathā ||

karavīrakṛtāṃ mālāṃ mādhavāya prayacchati |

devendro'pi maheśāni ! karoti karasampuṭam ||

atha māsaviśeṣe dhātrīphalādidānaphalaṃ tatraiva ||

dhātrīphalena patreṇa yo'rccayenmeṣage ravau |

daśānāmaśvamedhānāṃ phalantu labhate priye ! |

mañjarīṃ sahakārasya keśavāya nivedayet |

jambūtindukayoreva tathā vai keśavasya ca || 

matsyasūkte ||

karavīra kṛtāṃ mālāṃ yo dadyānmādhavāya ca |

p. 402) devendro'pi maheśāni ! karoti karasampuṭam ||

cakārādanyasyai devatāyai ca || 

tatraiva ||

na bhedayed yajñasūtraṃ mālāñcaiva na bhedayet |

viśeṣe mālatīmālā vyāghracarmma tathaiva ca || 

vṛhannīlatantre dvitīyapaṭale ||

kokanadañca bandhūkaṃ karṇikādvayameva |

vakamandāraraktāni karavīrāṇi śasyate |

mallikātritayaṃ jātī kṣaumapuṣpaṃ jayantikā |

vilvapatraṃ kuruvakaṃ munipuṣpañca keśaram |

vasantīñcaiva saugandhaṃ kālapuṣpaṃ manoharam |

āmalakañca kādambaṃ vakulaṃ yūthikāṃ tathā |

vilvairmaru vakādyaiśca tulasīvarjitaiḥ śubhaiḥ |

oḍrapuṣpairviśeṣeṇa vajrapuṣpeṇa śobhitam |

sarvaṃ puṣpaṃ pradadyācca bhaktiyuktena cetasā |

javāpuṣpaṃ maheśāni ! dadyāddevyai viśeṣataḥ |

padmaṃ priyataraṃ devyāḥ śephālī vakulaṃ tathā |

raktotpalena deveśi ! pūjayet paramāṃ śivām |

lakṣavarṣasahasrāṇāṃ pūjāyāḥ phalamāpnuyāt |

śirīṣaṃ paramaṃ devyāḥ prītidaṃ tagaraṃ tathā |

sthalapadmaṃ suṣṭhutaraṃ lakṣasaṃkhyakrameṇa tu |

yadi dadyānmaheśāni ! sarvasiddhiḥ sureśvari ! |

tadaiva mantrasiddhiḥ syānnātra kāryyā vicāraṇā |

vijātīṃ tulasīṃ ramyāṃ tasyāṃ prītikarāṃ parām |

kāñcanaṃ raktavarṇañca ādipriyataraṃ mahat |

bhaktiyukto maheśāni ! sarvaṃ puṣpaṃ nivedayet ||

vṛhannīlatantre ||

cāturmāsye tu lakṣaikamālatyā yo'rccayeddharim |

śatajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇādeva naśyati |

karavīrasya kusumairye'rccayanti harerdine |

darśanāttasya deveśi ! narakāgniḥ praṇaśyati || 

atha varjyapuṣpāṇi ||

nārccayet jhiṇṭipuṣpeṇa pītaiśca tagaraistathā |

śvetenoḍreṇa kṛṣṇena vijayena na cārcayet ||

prapañcasāre ṣaṣṭhapaṭale ||

aṅgane patitairmālyaiḥ śīrṇairvā jantudūṣitaiḥ |

āghrātairaṅgasaṃspṛṣṭairūṃṣitairnāpi cārcayet || 

śāradāyām ||

malinaṃ bhūmisaṃspṛṣṭaṃ kṛmikeśādidūṣitam-aṅgaspṛṣṭaṃ samāghrātaṃ tyajet 
paryyuṣitaṃ guruḥ || 

matsyasūkte caturddaśapaṭale ||

p. 403) śephālikāntu kahlāraṃ śaratkāle praśasyate |

anyatra na spṛśeddevi ! prāyaścittantu pūjanāt |

nārcayedraktakṛṣṇena tathogragandhikena ca |

karavīrasya māghyasya bandhūjīvasya caiva hi |

keśarasya ca vajrasya raktaṃ devi ! praśasyate ||

yoginītantre ||

vilvapatraṃ śamīpatraṃ tamālāmalakīdalam |

apāṅgabhṛṅgapatrañca kuśaṃ dūrvāṃ tathaiva ca |

puṣpāṇāmapyabhāve tu phalānyapi nivedayet |

phalānāmapyabhāve tu tatpatraiḥ pūjayeddharim ||

iti puṣpādyabhāve patreṇāpi pūjanam || 

guptapūjāpuṣpañca tatraiva ||

siṃhāsyañcaiva māsūraṃ dhustūrañca caturvidham |

tathā rudrajaṭe dve ca guhyapuṣpañca śaṅkari ! ||

padme nīlotpale devi ! vakamandārakāñcane |

mādhavī dve tamālañca guptametadvarārcane ||

divārātribhedena puṣpaviśeṣeṇa pūjanamuktaṃ tatraiva ||

kanakāni sugandhīni rātrau deyāni śaṅkari ! |

divā cānyāni puṣpāṇi divā rātrau ca mallikā ||

devālayasya puṣpeṇa yo devaṃ pratipūjayet |

andhatvaṃ prāpnuyāt so'pi daśa varṣāṇi pañca ca ||

devatāviśeṣe puṣpaviśeṣavarjjanamapi tatraiva ||

śive vivarjayet kundaṃ māghe māghyaṃ praśasyate |

gaṇeśe varjjayenmāghyamaśokaṃ tagaraṃ tathā |

sūryye māghyañca mandāraṃ kanakañca tathaiva ca |

mahālakṣmyai ca tulasīṃ jhiṇṭikāṃ kāñcanaṃ tathā |

atra jhiṇṭikāvarjanamadhikadoṣārtham ||

bandhūjīvañca droṇañca sarasvatyai na dāpayet |

grahāṇāṃ vilvapatrañca śamīpatraṃ tathaiva ca |

brahmaṇe varjayet kāśaṃ kausumbhaṃ śamipuṣpakam |

dhātrīpuṣpaṃ kuruṇṭañca jalapuṣpaṃ tathaiva ca |

durgāyai na pradadyācca somapuṣpaṃ tathaiva ca |

tripurāyai kāñcanañca kanakaṃ vāsakaṃ tathā |

kiṃśukaṃ kṛṣṇakāntāñca indrākṣīṃ varccanāṃ tathā ||

yoginītantre ||

vilvapatrañca māghyañca tamālāmalakīdalam |

kahlāraṃ tulasīñcaiva padmañca munipuṣpakam |

etat paryyuṣitaṃ na syāt yaccānyat kalikātmakam |

na duṣyecchinnabhinnañca jātīpuṣpañca śaṅkari ! |

p. 404) dhūpaśabdatryutpattistu kulārṇave ||

dhūtāśeṣamahādoṣapūtigandhaprabhāvataḥ |

paramānandajananāddhūpa ityabhidhīyate ||

prapañcasāre ||

sa guggulvagurūśīrasitājyamadhucandanaiḥ |

sārāṅgāre vinikṣiptairmantrī pīṭheḥ pradhūpayet ||

yoginītantre ||

kṛṣṇāgurusakarpūraṃ candanaṃ sihlakaṃ tathā |

bhagavatyai naro yastu idaṃ dattvā maheśvari ! |

iha kāmamavāpnoti durgāloke mahīyate || 

iti dhūpaḥ ||

dīpavyutpattistu kulārṇave ||

mohadhvāntapraśamanāt kṣayajanmanivāraṇāt |

divyarūpapradānācca paratattvaprakāśanāt |

mokṣadīpa iti khyāto mokṣamārgaikasādhanam ||

prapañcasāre ||

gosarpiṣā vā tailena vartyā ca laghugarbhayā |

dīpitaṃ surabhiṃ dugdhaṃ dīpamuccaiḥ pradīpayet || 

iti dīpaḥ ||

vastradānantu varadātantre caturthapaṭale ||

ṣaṭtriṃśadaṅgulānyūnāyāmaṃ vastraṃ na cārpayet |

vibhave sati deveśi ! kāmye karmaṇi kutracit ||

alaṅkārastu tatraiva ||

aṣṭavarṣakanyakāyā nopayuktaṃ bhavettu yat |

alaṅkāraṃ na dadyācca vittaśāṭyaṃ vivarjayet ||

hīnottamayoḥ phalantu sarvopacārāṇāṃ tatraiva ||

hīne hīnaphalaṃ devi ! śreṣṭhe śreṣṭhaphalaṃ labhet ||

naivedyaśabdavyutpattistu kulārṇave ||

caturvidhaṃ kuleśāni dravyaṃ te ṣaḍrasānvitam |

nivedanādbhavettṛptirnaivedyaṃ tadudāhṛtam ||

prapañcasāre ||

susitena suśuddhena pāyasena sasarpiṣā |

sitaudanaṃ sakadalīdadhyādyaiśca nivedayet | 

iti naivedyam ||

tāmbūlaṃ matsyasūkte ṣaḍviṃśatipaṭale ||

atha vāstuguvākañca kṛtvā parṇasamanvitam |

śaṅkhaśambūkacūrṇena viliptena nivedayet | 

tathā |

karpūravāsitaṃ devi ! mṛgadarpasamanvitam |

dhanyākamadhurīkādyaiḥ sādhitantu nivedayet |

elācampakamustādyairmukhavāsaḥ praśasyate ||

niṣiddhacūrṇaparṇe api tatraiva ||

kapardakasya vṛkṣasya palāśasya ca śaṅkari ! |

taccūrṇaṃ varjjayeddevi ! vṛkṣaparṇaṃ na dāpayet |

kalivṛkṣasthitaṃ parṇaṃ panasasthaṃ na dāpayet |

p. 405) aśokaśālmalisthaṃ vā sadaiva parivarjayet ||

kalivṛkṣo bhūtāvāsaḥ gauḍe vayaḍā iti yasya prasiddhiḥ |

śastamapi tatraiva ||

āmranimbagatañcaiva śastaṃ parṇaṃ mama priye ! | 

iti tāmbūlam ||

tataḥ prāṇāyāmādikaṃ kṛtvā aṣṭottarasahasraṃ śataṃ vā mūlamantraṃ japtvā 
japaṃ samarpya stavakavacādikaṃ paṭhitvā praṇamet |

taduktaṃ gandharvatantre pañcadaśapaṭale ||

stuvannānāvidhaiḥ stotrairbhaktibhāvasamanvitaḥ |

praṇamet parameśānīṃ vidhinā sādhakottamaḥ |

devamānuṣagandharvā yakṣarākṣasapannagāḥ |

namaskāreṇa tuṣyanti mahātmānaḥ samantataḥ |

namaskāreṇa labhate caturvargaṃ mahodayam |

sarvatra sarvasiddhyarthaṃ natirekā pravarttate |

natyā vijayate lokān natyā dharmaḥ pravarttate |

namaskāreṇa dīrghāyuracchinnā labhate prajāḥ |

caturvargaṃ labhedbhakto na cirādeva sādhakaḥ |

namaskāro mahāyajñaḥ prītidaḥ sarvataḥ sadā |

namaskuru mahādevīṃ pradakṣiṇañca bhaktitaḥ ||

yoginītantre pūrvakhṇḍe'ṣṭamapaṭale ||

pradakṣiṇatrayaṃ kuryyāt padmākāraṃ namettataḥ |

dakṣiṇāduttaraṃ gatvā devasya ca maheśvari ! ||

pradakṣiṇatrayamityādi madhyāvasāne jñeyam |

tathā ca kumārīpūjāmadhikṛtya rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍe ṣaṣṭhapaṭale ||

pradakṣiṇatrayaṃ kuryyādādau madhye tathāntataḥ |

paścāttu dakṣiṇāṃ kuryyādrajataiḥ svarṇamauktikaiḥ |

kṛtāñjaliṃ tato baddhvā bhrāmayitvā namettataḥ |

pratyekabhramaṇe devi ! daṇḍavat praṇipātayet |

yo no namedbhramitvā tu aparādho bhavettadā |

abaddhāñjalinā yastu namaskāraṃ karoti saḥ |

mohāndhakāranarake pacyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |

pātāntare ca praṇamenmūrdhnā na ca kṣitiṃ spṛśet |

śapanti devatāstasya viphalaṃ tat prakīrttitam |

praṇāme devadevasya yāvatyo mṛttikāḥ priye ! |

śarīre vā maheśāni ! tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu |

yāvanto reṇavastasya yāvatkālañca tiṣṭhati |

tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi brahmaloke mahīyate ||

p. 406) gandharvatantre ||

kāyiko vāgbhavaścaiva mānasastrividhaḥ smṛtaḥ |

namaskārāśca vijñeyā uttamādhamamadhyamāḥ |

kāyikaistu namaskarairdevāstuṣyanti sarvadā |

namaskāreṣu sarveṣu kāyikaḥ prathamaḥ smṛtaḥ |

jānubhyāmavanīṃ gatvā saṃspṛśya śirasā kṣitim |

kriyate yo namaskāraḥ procyate kāyikastu saḥ |

puṭīkṛtya karau śīrṣe sarvadharmārthasādhanam |

prasāryya dakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ svayaṃ namraśirāḥ punaḥ |

darśayan dakṣiṇaṃ pārśvaṃ bhaktiśraddhāsamanvitaḥ |

sakṛt pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā varttulākṛti sādhakaḥ |

sa tu pradakṣiṇo jñeyaḥ sarvadevaikatuṣṭidaḥ |

aṣṭottaraśataṃ yastu devyāḥ kuryyāt pradakṣiṇam |

sa sarvakāmamāsādya paścānmokṣamavāpnuyāt |

trikoṇamatha ṣaṭkoṇamarddhacandraṃ pradakṣiṇam |

daṇḍamaṣṭāṅgamugrañca saptadhā natilakṣaṇam |

dakṣiṇādvāyavīṃ gatvā tasmādvyāvṛtya dakṣiṇam |

gatvā yo'sau namaskāraḥ so'rddhacandro mama priyaḥ |

namaskāreṣu jānīyādāsāṃ prākṛtamadrije ! |

tyaktvā svamāsanaṃ sthānaṃ paścādgatvā namaskṛtaḥ |

nipatya daṇḍavadbhūmau daṇḍa ityucyate budhaiḥ |

tathaiva daṇḍavadbhūmau nipatya hṛdayena ca |

civukena mukhenātha nāsayā hanukena ca |

cakṣuṣā cātha karṇābhyāṃ brahmarandhreṇa caiva hi |

tadaṣṭāṅgamiti proktaṃ yadbhūmiṃ spṛśate kramāt ||

merutantre ||

hastābhyāṃ caraṇābhyāñca jānubhyāṃ vakṣasā tathā |

mūrdhnā dṛṣṭyā tathā vācā citte cāṣṭāṅga īritaḥ |

hastajānuśirovākyadhībhiḥ pañcāṅga īritaḥ ||

gandharvatantre ||

brahmarandhreṇa saṃsparśaḥ kṣiteryasyā namaskṛte |

sa ugra iti vijñeyo viṣṇostuṣṭipradāyakaḥ |

yā svayaṃ gadyapadyābhyāṃ ghaṭitābhyāṃ namaskṛtiḥ |

kriyate bhaktiyuktena vācikastūttamaḥ smṛtaḥ |

paurāṇikairvaidikairvā tāntrikaiḥ kriyate natiḥ |

sa madhyamo namaskāro bhavedācārataḥ sadā |

pareṣāṃ gadyapadyābhyāṃ namaskāro yadā bhavet |

sa vāciko'dhamo jñeyo namaskāreṣu sarvataḥ |

p. 407) iṣṭamadhyāniṣṭagatairmanobhistrividhaṃ bhavet |

namanaṃ mānasaṃ proktamuttamādhamamadhyamam |

trividhe ca namaskāre kāyikaścottamaḥ smṛtaḥ ||

tato devatāṃ visṛjyanaivedyāvaśeṣeṇa tattatkalpoktāṃ devatāṃ sampūjya 
sthānamārjjanādikaṃ kuryyāt ||

atha nirmālyakālanirūpaṇam yoginītantre tṛtīyabhāge saptamapaṭalīyavacanantu 
tantrasārakāreṇaiva dhṛtaṃ na tvatra prayojanam |

matsyasūkte ṣoḍaśapaṭale ||

maṇimuktāprabālāni devadattāni yāni ca |

na nirmālyaṃ dvādaśābdaṃ taijasāni tathaiva ca |

tūlikā caiva ṣaṇmāsamavijaṃ pañcavatsaram |

paṭanirmāṇakañcaiva kārpāsaṃ vastrameva ca |

vatsarañca tadarddhañca mṛduvastraṃ tadarddhakam |

naivedyaṃ darśanādeva mahāpūjāvasānake |

yāvaduṣṇaṃ bhavedannaṃ yajñasūtraṃ tataḥ smṛtam |

sodakaṃ kṛśarañcaiva yāmārddhena maheśvari ! |

karavīrasrajo devi ! tathā padmotpalāni ca |

yāmārddhena maheśāni ! tathā vilvamayasya ca |

mastakaṃ rudhirañcaiva ahorātreṇa sundari ! tulasī dve mālatī dve tamālāmalakī tathā |

punnāgamunipuṣpañca mallikā trividhāni ca |

jātī dve ketakī dve ca kumude padmakadvayam |

vāsudevāya yo dadyāttasya puṇyamanantakam ||

atha devatāsampradānakavihitaniṣiddhadravyāṇi ||

vṛhannīlatantre ṣaṣṭhapaṭale ||

sandeśaṃ paramaṃ dadyāddevyai dravyeṇa saṃyutam |

guḍaṃ kṣīraṃ madhudrākṣāmikṣudaṇḍaṃ purātanam |

rambhāphalaṃ bījapūraṃ tathā ca nārikelakam |

madhuyuktaṃ nārikelaśasyaṃ dadyānmaheśvari ! |

madhuyuktaṃ miṣṭayuktaṃ vyavahāro'pi ca tathā ||

yoginītantrottarakhaṇḍīyāṣṭamapaṭale ||

iṅgudīphalavilvāni vadarāmalakāni ca |

kharjjūraṃ panasañcaiva mātuluṅgaṃ madhūni ca |

āmrātoḍumbarañcaiva tathā tālaphalāni ca |

dāḍimaṃ kadalañcaiva prayatnena niyojayet |

lakucaṃ madhukaṃ yuktaṃ tathā pūgaphalāni ca |

bījapūrañca madhuraṃ karkandhūñca nivedayet |

mūlakasya ca śākañca anyacca vividhaṃ tathā |

p. 408) sarṣapasya ca śākañca rājīvasya tathaiva ca |

yasya mūlaṃ viśālañca śākaṃ tasya prarohakam |

vāstūkasya śākañca pālaṅkasya mama priye ! |

deyāni varjjanīyāni tathā ca tintiḍīphalam |

kuṣmāṇḍaṃ parvatīyañca yathā cāraṇyasambhavam |

kadalaṃ bījapūrañca vāmakaṃ pīṇḍrakaṃ tathā |

akālapanasañcaiva tathānyadapi varjjayet || 

iti vihitaniṣiddhaphalakathanam |

vrīhīṇāñca pravakṣyāmi upayogyāni śaṅkari ! |

ekacittaṃ samādhāya prāpaṇaṃ śṛṇu śaṅkari ! |

somadhānyaṃ vṛhaddhānyaṃ raktaśālikameva ca |

mahaddhānyaṃ yavañcaiva tilamāṣañca mudgakam |

kalāyaṃ varttulañcaiva siddhārthaṃ kuṅkumāni ca |

kṣudrajīrañca madhurīmetānyapi niyojayet ||

varjyaśasyānyapi tatraiva ||

kālaśākaṃ kalāyañca jīvakīyaṃ tathaiva ca |

caṇakaṃ kodravañcaiva varjjayenmama sundari ! ||

atra kalāyaṃ trikoṇama cipiṭaṃ vā |

varttulasya vihitatvāttenātasīmasūrādīni varjjayedityarthaḥ ||

kṣīrañca kṛṣṇākṣīrañca lavaṇaṃ mṛttikodbhavam |

lavaṇaṃ prācīsambhūtaṃ tathottarasamudbhavam ||

kṣīraṃ kṛṣṇagavyādikṣīram ||

paśūnāñca pravakṣyāmi vanyānāṃ grāmavāsinām |

yena cāpyupayojyāni gavyaṃ devi ! payo ghṛtam |

mārgaṃ māṃsaṃ tathā chāgaṃ śālabhaṃ śāśakantathā |

etaistu prāpaṇaṃ dadyādviṣṇoścaiva priyāvaham |

māhiṣaṃ varjjayenmāṃsaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ tataḥ |

pakṣiṇāñca pravakṣyāmi ye prayojyā mama piye |

yāṃścaiva viṣṇuyāgeṣu upayuñjanti nityaśaḥ |

haritañca mayūrañca lāvakaṃ varttakaṃ tathā |

kapilaścaiva cāsaśca kākakukkuṭakau śiraḥ |

vanyakukkuṭakaścaiva śarāriśca kapotakam |

vellakaḥ kulikaścaiva raktapucchaśca ṭiṭṭibhaḥ |

kṛṣṇo matsyāśināñcaiva patatrīṇāṃ viśiṣyate |

abhakṣyañcaiva yadvā pañcanakhasya ca |

yadvā yadivārthe | 

māṃsamabhakṣyaṃ yadi vā bhakṣyaṃ tadā pañcanakhasya cakārādeśca ityanvayaḥ ||

p. 409) citramatsyaṃ rohitañca pāṭhīnaṃ nalamīnakam |

mahāśālañca śālañca rājīvaṃ siṃhatuṇḍakam |

matsyānyetāni devāni vaḍālīñca vivarjayet |

citramatsyaścitala iti | 

nalamīnaściṅḍīti prasiddhaḥ | 

siṃhatuṇḍaḥ riṭhā iti prasiddhaḥ | 

vaḍālī bhedā iti prasiddhaḥ ||

jānapādāṃśca śakunān kṣudravārāhakaṃ tathā |

kausumbhaśākapinnākaṃ raktapādāṃśca varjayet |

kharjūraṃ panasaṃ drākṣāṃ mātulaṅgañca śīrakam |

kadalīṃ nāgaraṅgañca tathā jambū phalāni ca |

śālukaṃ madhukañcaiva prayatnena nivedayet |

kadalaṃ bījapūrañca dugdhapakvaṃ nivedayet |

kandupakvaṃ kaśeruñca jambūṃ bālapriyaṃ bhavet |

ārdrakaṃ lavaṇañcaiva jīrakaṃ pippalīyakam |

jātīkoṣaṃ tindukañca devyāḥ priyataraṃ bhavet |

varjanīyaphalamāha tatraiva ||

rāmarambhāphalaṃ pakvaṃ kadalaṃ dhūmratāpitam |

na yojayenmahādevyai utpalasya ca bījakam |

dhānyaṃ śrāvaṇikaṃ martyo dviḥsvinnañca vivarjayet |

nārikelaṃ suvarṇābhaṃ nārikelañca rāmakam |

nivedayenmahādevyai toyaṃ tasya viśeṣataḥ |

nārikelañca bhāṇḍīraṃ daive śrāddhe vivarjayet |

vakulasya phalaṃ pakvaṃ padmasya ca phalaṃ tathā |

niyojayenmahādevyai cāndrāyaṇaphalaṃ labhet ||

niṣiddhamāha tatraiva ||

karkkandhūṃ śarkarāyuktaṃ daṇḍaśākaṃ tathaiva ca |

kuṣmāṇḍaṃ parvatīyañca dūrataḥ parivarjayet ||

matsyasūkte ṣaḍviṃśatipaṭale ||

śṛṇu devi ! pravakṣyāmi māṃsabhedānnibodha me |

na deyaṃ tiktakamaṭhaṃ paśuśṛṅgiṇameva ca |

gomīnaṃ cakraśakulaṃ vaḍālaṃ rāghavaṃ tathā |

vāmīnaṃ canavarṇañca sacakraṃ ceṅgameva ca |

bhūvilaṃ cāniruddhañca gāṅgeyāni vivarjayet |

ātoṣiṇīhiraṇyāni cakravimbāni varjayet |

ajañca vṛhadājañca parvatīyaṃ tathaiva ca |

meṣañca dvividhañcaiva eṇaṃ caiva caturvidham |

akalau tu gavāṃ māṃsaṃ godhāmāṃsañca śāśakam |

lulāpañcaiva nepāle vārāhaṃ kāmarūpake |

p. 410) khaḍgaṃ caturmukhaṃ bhakṣyaṃ cāhāre parameśvari ! |

gavayaṃ gandhamṛgañcaiva mahiṣantu viśiṣyate |

sarvatra kṣudravārāhaṃ nīlahayaṃ tathaiva ca |

malaye saguṇe caiva tathā jālandhareṣu ca |

abhakṣyaṃ matsyamāṃsañca vaḍālaṃ sarvapīṭhake |

atra deśaviśeṣe matsyamāṃsabhojananiṣedhād deśāntare gauḍanepālādau 
matsyamāṃsabhojanamanumataṃ bhavati |

vaḍālī tu abhakṣyā sarvadeśa ityarthaḥ ||

māntriko varjjayennityaṃ pārvataṃ śṛṅgaverakam |

kuṣmāṇḍaṃ parvatīyañca svarṇarohitakaṃ tyajet |

svarṇābhāṃ godhikāñcaiva vārāhaṃ śvetameva ca |

cakrāṅkitañca kamaṭhaṃ rājahaṃsaṃ na bhakṣayet |

grāmyaṃ jalacaraṃ caiva bhakṣyaṃ tasya vivarjjayet |

kapotaṃ kukkuṭaṃ caiva mayūraṃ krauñcameva ca |

varddhanaṃ vabhrubālañca dātyūhaṃ kokilaṃ tathā |

gṛdhrolūkañca kaṅkañca vistārākṣaṃ na bhakṣayet |

alāvuṃ vārttākuṃ caiva tittirañca mayaṃ tathā |

bhāradvājaṃ matsyacakraṃ daśāvatārameva |

akāmena dvijo bhuktvā prāṇāyāmena śudhyati |

trirātrāvasthitaṃ māṃsaṃ tatsarvaṃ parivarjjayet |

bhuktasya parvatīyasya siṃhatuṇḍasya pārvati ! |

na hi svedātape śuddhaṃ vahniśuddhaṃ tathaiva ca |

saṃskṛtaṃ gandhapṛktena pallavaṃ parameśvari ! |

tanmāṃsabhakṣaṇāddevi ! bhūyaśca tatra vidyate |

yo māṃsaṃ varjjayeddevi ! māṃsamātraṃ viśeṣataḥ |

sarvayajñaphalaṃ samyak labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ |

saṃvatsare daśaguṇaṃ ṣaṇmāse ca caturguṇam |

parvakāle tvaśakte tu pitṛmedhaphalaṃ labhet |

viṣṇoḥ śayanakāle ca cāturmāsye ca yatnataḥ |

agniṣṭomasya yajñasya labhate phalamuttamam |

aśaktaḥ kārttike devi ! matsyamāṃsau vivarjjayet |

kārttike śaukaraṃ māṃsaṃ gomīnaṃ matsyameva ca |

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena kārttike parivarjjayet |

bhakṣayellāvakaṃ varttaṃ hariccitrakaraṃ tathā |

cāsaṃ citrakapotañca tṛṇākṣañca mayūrakam |

kāmañca kalabhañcaiva tolikālakameva |

etānyeva ca bhakṣyāṇi pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ ||

p. 411) niṣiddhamapi tatraiva ||

bhṛṅgarājadvayañcaiva pikaṃ kadalameva ca |

sāraṅgaṃ dīrghatuṇḍañca dīrghagrīvaṃ hiraṇmayam |

narttañcalaṃ raktakaṇṭhaṃ raktapakṣiṇameva ca |

cillaṃ kīlālakañcaiva gaṅgācillakameva ca |

etānyeva dvijātīnāmabhakṣyāṇīti śabditam |

raktapucchaṃ tathāraṇyaṃ kukkuṭaṃ śoṇatuṇḍakam |

śvetagrīvo nīlakaṇṭho raktakaṇṭhastathaiva ca |

śaśakaḥ patravāhaśca pīḍako jīvakastathā |

etāni śastamāṃsāni kāle prāptāni bhakṣayet ||

abhakṣyamāṃsamāha tatraiva ||

naravānara-ṛkṣāṃśca vyāghramārjārakacchapān |

viḍvarāhañca śaralaṃ hayamāṃsaṃ tathaiva ca |

na bhakṣayet śuno māṃsaṃ bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇañcaret |

sakāmo gavayañcaiva gavāṃ māṃsaṃ tathaiva ca |

hastimāṃsaṃ pramādena bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇadvayam |

śūkarasya ca khaḍgasya sthūlamāṃsaṃ tathāśayam |

sthūlaṃ dvābhyāṃ tribhirvarṣaiścaturbhiḥ ṣaḍbhireva ca |

atisthūlamiti proktaṃ tanna bhavet kadācana |

eṇasya śaśakasyaiva ajasya vṛṣabhasya ca |

tathā khasya bhṛgasyaiva varāhamahiṣasya ca |

atisthūlāni māṃsāni bhakṣyaṃ māṃsaṃ kalau yuge |

akalau tu gavāṃ māṃsaṃ deśācāreṇa śaukaram |

na bhakṣayenmṛtaṃ māṃsaṃ dvividhaṃ mūṣikasya ca |

mekasya kṣudrāṭakasya śambūkakarkaṭasya ca |

akāmena dvijobhuktvā prāṇāyāmena śudhyati |

anuktamatsyamāṃsasya bhoktā snānena śudhyati |

gomāṃsaṃ māṃsakāmena bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇañcaret |

pheravasya tathā bhuktvā ṛṣicāndrāyaṇañcaret |

pūtigandhasakīṭāni bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇañcaret |

raudraśuṣkāṇi bhuktvā vai prāṇāyāmena śudhyati |

trirātrāvasthitaṃ māṃsaṃ tatsarvaṃ parivarjjayet |

śallasya parvatīyasya siṃhatuṇḍasya pārvati ! |

na duṣyedātape śuddhaṃ vahniśuddhaṃ tathaiva ca |

saṃskṛtaṃ gandhapṛktena yannavaṃ parameśvari ! |

tanmāṃsabhakṣaṇāddevi ! prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate |

atra bhakṣaṇaniṣedhādanivedya bhakṣaṇadoṣadarśanena sutarāṃ devatāsampradānakatvam 
|

māṃsatyāga phalamapi tatraiva ||

p. 412) tulāmakarameṣeṣu yo māṃsaṃ parivarjjayet |

hayamedhasya yajñasya tadeva phalamāpnuyāt |

yāvajjīvaṃ tyajedvāpi rājasūyaphala labhet |

ekasya varjjanāddevi ! matsyamāṃsasya varjanam |

gṛhamedhī maheśāni ! pitṛśrāddhaṃ vinā tyajet |

pramādādvā tathājñānāt saṅkalpaṃ vā samācaret |

cāndrāyaṇaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā paurṇamāsyāṃ samācaret |

pitṛśrāddhaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā kṛtvā yastu nirāmiṣam |

satyatvaṃ varjayedvāpi pitṛhatyāvratañcaret |

sarvavratādhikataraṃ sarvayajñādhikaṃ param |

pitṛmedhaṃ maheśāni ! sarvasvenāpi kārayet |

tasyādhikataraṃ vāpi svabhāryyārttavalaṅghane |

ṣoḍaśedivase pūrṇe bharttā proṣita āgataḥ |

harivāsarakañcānyamathavā śrāddhavāsaram |

ṛtuṃ na laṅghayettatra tadeva vratameva hi |

ṛtuhā parameśāni ! brahmahatyāvratañcaret |

vrate vinaṣṭe deveśi ! prāyaścittaṃ bhavedanu |

tasmāt puṇyatamaṃ jñeyaṃ svabhāryyāparitoṣaṇam ||

atha haviṣyādau bhakṣyābhakṣyavidhiḥ ||

viśvasāre dvitīyapaṭale ||

atha bhakṣyaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva kamalānane ! |

annaṃ dugdhaṃ tathā takraṃ pāyasaṃ pūpasūpakam |

navanītaṃ phalānyeva guḍapāyasasaṃyutam |

sarvāṇi phalapuṣpāṇi vividhāni vidhānavit |

na mūlaṃ gṛñjanaṃ lakṣma masūraṃ lasunaṃ tathā |

jambīraṃ lakucaṃ kāṃsyamalāvuñcāmlameva ca |

rājamāṣaṃ tathā māṣaṃ śākañcaiva palāṇḍukam |

āmiṣaṃ raktaśākādīn tāmbūlañcātha tailakam |

tālakharjūramuṣitaṃ dugdhavuṣṭādikañca yat |

kucelañca tathā tailaṃ mādhvīñca nakhalomakān |

abhyaṅgamasadālāpaṃ haviṣyāśī vivarjjayet |

hariṣyeṣu sudhā mukhyā tadanu vrīhayo matāḥ |

akṣāralavaṇañcaiva śaṅkarasya matena ca |

yavagodhūmamudgāśca dugdhaṃ śarkarasaṃyutam |

phalamūlādikaṃ hṛdyamakṣāralavaṇaṃ ghṛtaṃ |

raktavastraṃ tathā kṣuraṃ haviṣyeṣu vivarjjayet || 

śāradāyāṃ dvitīyapaṭale ||

bhaikṣyaṃ haviṣyaṃ śākāni vihitāni phalaṃ payaḥ |

p. 413) mūlaṃ śakturyavotpanno bhakṣyāṇyetāni mantriṇām |

bhaikṣyamiti brahmacāriyatiparam |

bhikṣāsvarūpamuktaṃ rāghavabhaṭṭadhṛtavacane ||

vaidikācārayuktānāṃ śucīnāṃ śrīmatāṃ gṛhe |

satkulasthānajātānāṃ bhikṣā syādagrajanmanām |

haviṣyamuktaṃ smṛtau ||

haimantikaṃ sitāsvinnaṃ dhānyaṃ mudgāstilā yavāḥ |

kalāyakaṅgunīvārā vāstūkaṃ hilamocikā |

ṣaṣṭikā kālaśākañca mūlakaṃ kemuketarat |

kandaṃ saindhavasāmudre gavye ca dadhisarpiṣī |

payo'nuddhṛtasārañca panasāmrī harītakī |

tintiḍījīrakañcaiva nāgaraṅgañca pippalī |

kadalī lavalī dhātrīphalānyagurumaikṣavam |

atailapakvaṃ munayo haviṣyānnaṃ pracakṣate |

phalaṃ payo mūlaṃ vihitamityanuṣajyate ||

athābhakṣyaṃ matsyasūkte ||

nārikelodakaṃ kāṃsye ajākṣīrañca kācake |

pāṣāṇe mahiṣīkṣīramabhakṣyaṃ parikīrttitam || 

pañcadaśapaṭale ||

varāhapūjako yastu kolaṃ kūrmaṃ na bhakṣayet |

brahmāṇaṃ pūjayed yastu māhiṣaṃ cāmaraṃ carum |

gaṇeśayājī madhukaṃ panasaṃ koradūṣaṇam |

ādityabhakto deveśi ! mayūraṃ kuṇḍasāhakam |

śivayājī tilapiṣṭaṃ ciñcāñca cakrapūjakaḥ |

tathā ca madguraṃ matsyaṃ śakticiñcāṃ vaḍālakam |

kusumbhaśākaṃ deveśi ! laśunaṃ śṛṅgiṇaṃ tathā |

traipure tu nimbatiktaṃ tvaritārthī tathāmrakam |

gopālārthī tu pālaṅkamāmamatsyaṃ na bhakṣayet |

pārāvataṃ vāmanārthī cakraśākaṃ vaḍālakam |

nimbaṃ sārasvatairvarjyaṃ lakṣmyarthī ca karañjakam |

kālāndhaṃ kodravañcaiva candramatsyaṃ tathaiva ca |

sarvamantre vaḍālañca sarvayajñeṣu śaṅkari ! |

śvetapūtīñca vārttākīṃ kuṣmāṇḍaṃ parvatīyakam ||

ciñcāñcaiva parānnañca yatnena parivarjayet |

vaiṣṇavaiḥ patraśākañca mūlaṃ kandaṃ prarohakam |

śuṣkaṃ rājīvavāstūkaṃ pālaṅkīñca tathaiva ca |

yadā saṅkalpapūrveṇa yastyajedvaiṣṇavaḥ kvacit |

tathāpi na tyajeddevi ! prāyaścittaṃ samācaret |

sauravrate ghṛtaṃ varjyaṃ tāmbūlaṃ vaiṣṇaveṣu ca |

p. 414) gavyatyāgī mahāpāpī madhutyāgī śriyaṃ haret | 

saptaviṃśatipaṭale ||

jambū kharjūradāḍimbanārikelañca pūgakam |

jalavimbā trapūṣā tu urvāruṃ pīnakantathā |

vadarīlavalīdhātrīpanasāmraharītakī |

śālūkaṃ padmaṣaṇḍañca kaśeru karamarddakam |

nāgaraṅgañca madhukaṃ madhuraṃ tendukāni ca |

ānāḍañcaiva kuṣmāṇḍaṃ vāsantyāḥ phalameva ca |

tilakānnaṃ kalāyañca māṣañca rājamāṣakam |

taṇḍulaṃ yavadhānyañca nīvāraṃ kuṅkumaṃ tathā |

jīrakañca lavaṅgañca dhanyākaṃ jātikoṣakam |

elakañcaiva karpūraṃ candanāgurumustakam |

saralaṃ devadāruñca kṛṣṇabhallātakāni ca |

śṛṅgaveraḥ kaṭudravyaṃ maricaṃ lavaṇaṃ kaṇā |

phalaṃ yacca śivāyāśca svakṣāmraṃ panasasya ca |

kṛṣṇakoṣaṃ granthiparṇaṃ svargādevāṅkurāṇi ca |

drākṣāñca vakulaṃ kacvīphalañcekṣu ca khaṇḍitam |

etānyācamanīyāni surabhiḥ pācakānyapi | 

tathā |

parvatīyaṃ tathā varttu tathā kaitavajāni ca |

lakṣmīlakucakañcaiva tilakaṃ kṣudratillakam |

hintālaṃ panasāmrañca pīvaraṃ baddhajīrakam |

vṛhacchoṭā ca vadarī parvatīyaṃ vivarjjayet |

kṣudra urvārukañcaiva jīvantyāḥ kṣudrameva ca |

madhukaṃ madhurīṃ drākṣāṃ kalāyaṃ yanna varttulam |

śvetamudgaṃ tathā kṛṣṇaṃ phalaṃ tasya vivarjayet |

nārikelaphalañcaiva svarṇābhaṃ nāgaraṅgakam |

nārikelañca svarṇābhaṃ dugdhaśasyaṃ tathaiva ca |

somadhānyaṃ nīvārañca kalambaṃ ṣaṣṭikāni ca |

vṛhaddhānyaṃ kṣudradhānyaṃ vālkalikamupāsukam |

etāni vrīhimukhyāni haviṣyāṇyeva bhojane |

mauktikaṃ raktaśākañca uttamaudhaiḥ pracakṣyate |

ikṣuñca śvetatrapuṣaṃ tathā mṛgaphalāni ca |

madyakumbha praropañca jagdhānnaṃ taṭasambhavam |

tathā śrāvaṇikaṃ devi ! haviṣyānne vivarjjayet |

vrateṣu āmiṣaṃ devi ! tāmbūlaṃ parṇacūrṇake |

nirāmiṣaṃ cāture ca āmiṣaṃ lakucāni ca |

abhakṣyaṃ varttulaṃ tasya hastabhagnastathaiva |

p. 415) vārttākuṃ śvetaraktañca śvetapūtikameva ca |

vrate tu varjjayeddevi ! phalāhāravidhiṃ śṛṇu |

namaskāraṃ phalāhāraṃ sagavyaṃ vā viśiṣyate |

tribhāgapūraṇaṃ kṛtvā jalāhāraṃ kalau tyajet |

phalāhāraṃ maheśāni ! gṛhasthānāṃ kalau yuge |

śataṃ tilaṃ tadarddhaṃ vā tilāhāre maheśvari ! |

kṣīrāhāre ca caṇakaṃ pañcagavye phalaṃ bhavet |

ājyāhāre tu tolaikaṃ haviṣyasya vidhiṃ śṛṇu |

pakvamannaṃ svayaṃ vātha bhāryyayā sodareṇa tu |

mātāpiturgurorvāpi na bhuktvā yadi vā pacet |

laghvāhāro jitakrodho haviṣyavratabhojane | 

tathā |

vetrāṅkuraṃ tathā mustāṃ saindhavaṃ sindhusambhavam |

tathā parvatasambhūtaṃ havirdravyādi kīrttitam |

vilvāmalatilasyaiva vadaryyāḥ patrameva ca |

haviṣyabhojane prāpte haviṣyānnaṃ pracakṣate |

sundārghya pātre patre vā palāśasya ca patrake |

kadalyāśca tvace patre haviṣyavratabhojane |

sauravrate kaṭudravyaṃ śaive cānnaṃ vivarjayet |

vaiṣṇave koradūṣañca śākte śāliṃ vivarjayet |

pūgaṃ sarvavratetyājyaṃ āranālakameva ca |

na bhakṣayellauhacchinnaṃ hastabhagnaṃ na bhakṣayet |

kālikāsu sthitaṃ dravyaṃ tathā panasapatrake ||

vikhyātasvīyakīrttyā'jani jagati dayārāmaviśvāsanāmā dharmātmā tasya sūnuḥ 
sakalaguṇayuto rāmaharyyākhya eva |

jātau tasyātmajau śrīsukṛtiyutajaganmohanaprāṇakṛṣṇau jyeṣṭhaḥ śrīprāṇakṛṣṇo 
guṇigaṇakumudaśreṇipūrṇauṣadhīśaḥ ||

tadādiṣṭaḥ śiṣṭavidvanmanaḥsantuṣṭikāriṇīm |

śrīprāṇatoṣiṇīṃ vallīṃ nirmame rāmatoṣaṇaḥ ||

ādeṣṭṭasatvaratayā gurutābhiyā ca granthasya sarvaracanaṃ na samācacakṣe |

dhīrāḥ ! kṛtāñjalirahaṃ vinivedayāmi yuṣmābhireva sakalantu vicaraṇīyam ||

iti śrīprāṇakṛṣṇaviśvāsānumatāyāṃ 
śrīrāmatoṣaṇavidyālaṅkārabhaṭṭācāryyaviracitayāṃ śrīprāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ 
tṛtīyamarthakāṇḍāṅgaṃ samāptam ||
